[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/4:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/4:22:3)


**וְהַכַּפּוֹת.** לְבָזִיכֵי לְבוֹנָה (ויקרא כד:ז):


**and the spoons** for th e spoons of frankincense (Lev. 24:7).


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 4:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/4:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/4:22:4)


**וְהַמַּחְתּוֹת.** לַחְתּוֹת בָּהֶם תְּרוּמַת הַדֶּשֶׁן, לְהָרִים מֵעַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ: ״וְלָקַח מְלֹא הַמַּחְתָּה וְגוֹמֵר״ (ויקרא טז:יב):


**and the censers** with which to gather up the part of the ashes that is separated [from the rest and cast beside the altar, as in Lev. 6:31, (and the rest of the ashes) to pick up from the altar, (ibid. 16:12): “And he shall take a full censer, etc.”



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:1:1)


**וַיָּבֵא שְׁלֹמֹה אֶת קָדְשֵׁי.** כָּל מַה שֶּׁבַּפָּסוּק לְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ: מַה שֶּׁנִּשְׁאַר לוֹ מִן הַקְּדָשׁוֹת שֶׁהִקְדִּישׁ דָּוִד, כְּדִכְתִיב לְמַעְלָה ״גַּם אוֹתָם הִקְדִּישׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ דָּוִד לַה׳ עִם הַכֶּסֶף וְהַזָּהָב אֲשֶׁר נָשָׂא מִכָּל הַגּוֹיִם וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א יח:יא), וּמַה שֶׁהוֹתִיר לוֹ **נָתַן בְּאֹצְרוֹת בֵּית הָאֱלֹהִים**. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (פסיקתא רבתי ו): יֵשׁ מֵחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ לֵיחָרֵב, וְשֶׁלֹּא יְהוּ אוֹמְרִים הָעוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים: קָשָׁה יִרְאָתֵנוּ, שֶׁלָּקְחָה נִקְמָתָהּ מִן הַבַּיִת הַזֶּה שֶׁנִּבְנָה מִן הַגְּזֵילוֹת וַחֲמָסִים שֶׁגָּזַל דָּוִד מִבָּתֵּי עֲבוֹדַת כוֹכָבִים שֶׁלָּהּ; וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: כָּךְ אָמַר (הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא; צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר:) שְׁלֹמֹה: רָעָב הָיָה בִּימֵי דָוִד (אָבִי) שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים שָׁנָה אַחַר שָׁנָה (שמואל ב כא:א), וְהָיָה לוֹ לְבַזְבֵּז הַהֶקְדֵּשׁוֹת הַלָּלוּ לְהַחֲיוֹת בָּהֶן עֲנִיֵּי יִשְׂרָאֵל. וַאֲנִי מֵבִיא סִיּוּעַ, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּכָל הַהֶקְדֵּשׁוֹת שֶׁדָּוִד הִקְדִּישׁ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה (שם), לֹא נֶאֱמַר בָּהֶן עָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה דָּבָר פְּלוֹנִי זֶה. כַּאֲשֶׁר נֶאֱמַר בַּנְּחֹשֶׁת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּמִטִּבְחַת וּמִכּוּן עָרֵי הֲדַדְעֶזֶר לָקַח דָּוִד נְחֹשֶׁת רַבָּה מְאֹד בָּהּ עָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה אֶת יָם הַנְּחֹשֶׁת וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א יח:ח), אֲבָל מִכֶּסֶף וּמִזָּהָב שֶׁלֹּא נֶאֱמַר בּוֹ עָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה מֵהֶן שׁוּם דָּבָר – מִכְּלָל שֶׁלֹּא עָשָׂה. וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאֵין כְּתִיב כָּאן שׁוּם נְחֹשֶׁת, כִּי אִם **כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב**. וּלְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ: נִרְאֶה לִי: מַה שֶּׁלֹּא אָמַר נְחֹשֶׁת, לְפִי שֶׁהַנְּחֹשֶׁת בְּזוֹל, וְאֵינוֹ רָאוּי וְכַדַּי וְהָגוּן לְהָבִיא נְחֹשֶׁת **בְּאֹצְרוֹת בֵּית ה׳**:


**and Solomon brought his father David’s hallowed things** Whatever is mentioned in this verse—according to its simple meaning—refers to that which remained to him from the things that David hallowed, as is written above (I Chron. 18:11): “Those also King David dedicated to the Lord, [along] with the silver and the gold that he took from all the nations, etc.,” and that which he left over he deposited into the treasuries of the House of God. And [in] *Midrash Aggadah* (*Pesikta Rabbathi* 6:7), some sages say that [he did not use these articles for the building of the Temple] because he knew that it was destined to be destroyed, in order that the nations should not say, “Our deity is strong,” for it took revenge on this House, which was built from the loot and the spoils that David robbed from their heathen temples. Some say: So said Solomon, “There was a famine during the days of David my father for three consecutive years, year after year, and he should have given away these hallowed things to sustain the poor of Israel with them.” I wish to bring support [to the view that Solomon did not build the Temple with the spoils of the nations], for regarding all the devoted things that David dedicated, as is written above (ibid.), it is not stated that with this Solomon made such and such a thing, as it is stated regarding the copper, as it is said (ibid. verse 8): “And from Tibhath and from Cun, cities of Hadadezer, David took huge quantities of copper, with which Solomon made the copper sea, etc.,” but of the silver and gold, about which it is not stated that Solomon made anything from it, it may be deduced that he did not make [anything from it]. Therefore, it is not written here any copper, but silver and gold. According to the simple interpretation, it seems to me that Scripture does not mention copper because copper is cheap, and it is not proper or fitting to bring it into the treasuries of the House of God.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:2:1)


**אָז יַקְהֵל שְׁלֹמֹה אֶת זִקְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** נָהַג בְּמִדַּת חָכְמָה, שֶׁהִקְהִיל הַזְּקֵנִים וְלֹא הַבַּחוּרִים, יוֹתֵר מִדָּוִד שֶׁהִקְהִיל הוּא אַף הַבַּחוּרִים, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וַיַּקְהֵל דָּוִיד אֶת כָּל... יִשְׂרָאֵל... יְרוּשָׁלִָם וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א כח:א):


**Then Solomon assembled the elders of Israel** He conducted [the matter] with the attribute of wisdom, in that he gathered the elders, and not the youths, [which was] better than David [had done,] who gathered even the youths, as it is written, “And David assembled all the leaders of Israel...Jerusalem” (I Chron. 28:1).



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:3:1)


**בֶּחָג.** הוּא חַג הַסֻּכּוֹת:


**on the festival** the festival of Succoth.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:5:1)


**אֶת אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד.** שֶׁעָשָׂה מֹשֶׁה, גְּנָזוֹ שְׁלֹמֹה בְּבֵית אוֹצְרוֹת הָאֱלֹהִים עִם שְׁאָר כֵּלִים וְהַהֶקְדֵּשׁוֹת, לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הֻצְרַךְ לוֹ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (תוספתא סוטה יג:א), שֶׁנִּגְנְזוּ:


**and the Tent of Meeting** that Moses made, Solomon hid it in the treasuries of the House of God with the remaining vessels and the hallowed articles, because he did not need them, and our Sages (*Tosefta of Sotah 13:1) said that they were hidden away.*


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:5:2)


**וְאֶת כָּל כְּלֵי הַקֹּדֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר בָּאֹהֶל.** אֲשֶׁר נָתַן (ס״א נָטַע) דָּוִד לָאָרוֹן:


**all the sacred vessels that were in the Tent** that David had given to the Ark.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:5:3)


**הֶעֱלוּ אֹתָם הַכֹּהֲנִים הַלְוִיִּם.** פִּתְרוֹנוֹ מוּסָב לְמַעְלָה, שֶׁאָמַר **וַיִּשְׂאוּ הַלְוִיִּם אֶת הָאָרוֹן**, וּמְפָרֵשׁ מִי הָיוּ אוֹתָם הַלְוִיִּם שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לְךָ לְמַעְלָה: הַכֹּהֲנִים הַלְוִיִּם. שֶׁאַף הַכֹּהֲנִים מִבְּנֵי לֵוִי הֵם, וְלֹא לְוִיִּם מַמָּשׁ הָיוּ, וְכֵן כָּתוּב בַּמְּלָכִים ״וַיִּשְׂאוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים אֶת הָאָרוֹן״ (מלכים א ח:ג); וְגַם הַכֵּלִים וְאֹהֶל מוֹעֵד הֶעֱלוּ עִמָּהֶם הַלְוִיִּם כְּמִשְׁפָּטָם:


**the priests and the Levites brought them up.** This refers back to the above, where Scripture says: “and the Levites bore the Ark,” and he explains: Who were those Levites that I mentioned to you above? The Levitic priests, for the priests are also of the sons of Levi, but they were not real Levites, and so it is written (I Kings 8:3): “and the priests took up the Ark.” And the Levites also brought up the vessels and the Tent of Meeting according to their regulations.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:8:1)


**וַיִּהְיוּ הַכְּרוּבִים פֹּרְשִׂים כְּנָפַיִם עַל מְקוֹם הָאָרוֹן.** בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁהָאָרוֹן עוֹמֵד שָׁם, לְפִיכָךְ: **וַיְכַסּוּ הַכְּרוּבִים עַל הָאָרוֹן**:


**And the cherubim were spreading their wings over the place of the Ark** in the place where the Ark was standing Therefore -


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:8:2)


**וְעַל בַּדָּיו מִלְמָעְלָה.** לְמַעְלָה לָאָרוֹן כָּךְ הָיוּ עוֹמְדִים הַבַּדִּים, וְנִמְשְׁכוּ כָּל כָּךְ לַחוּץ כְּלַפֵּי מִזְרָח, שֶׁהָיָה יָכוֹל הָאָרוֹן לַעֲמוֹד תַּחַת הַכְּרוּבִים מַמָּשׁ בְּלֹא עִכּוּב הַבַּדִּים, וּלְפִיכָךְ: **וַיְכַסּוּ הַכְּרוּבִים עַל הָאָרוֹן**. וְגַם אֶת הַבַּדִּים כִּסּוּ קְצָת אֵצֶל הָאָרוֹן וְלֹא כֻּלָּם שֶׁהָיוּ מַגִּיעִים עַד הַמִּזְרָח. וְאֵינְךָ יָכוֹל לְפָרֵשׁ שֶׁכִּסּוּ הַכְּרוּבִים עַל כָּל הַבַּדִּים, וְגַם מִשּׁוּם דָּבָר זֶה **וַיַּאֲרִיכוּ הַבַּדִּים וַיֵּרָאוּ רָאשֵׁי הַבַּדִּים מִן הָאָרוֹן עַל פְּנֵי הַדְּבִיר**, שֶׁאִם לֹא כֵן, אֶלָּא כְּאֶחָד מִדָּה אַחַת הָיוּ מוֹשְׁכִין זֶה מִזֶּה, וַהֲלֹא הַבַּדִּים שֶׁעָשָׂה מֹשֶׁה עֲדַיִן הֵם הָיוּ שָׁם, וְהֵם עוֹמְדִים בְּקֹדֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁים שֶׁל אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה כִּי אִם עֶשֶׂר, וְזֶה הָיָה עֶשְׂרִים, וְהָיָה מַגִּיעַ מִקִּיר אֶל קִיר. וְהַכְּרוּבִים שֶׁל מֹשֶׁה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר אַף בָּהֶם ״פּוֹרְשִׂים״ וְ״סֹכְכִים״ (שמות לז:ט), אֵין צָרִיךְ לָזוּז כְּלָל מִן הַבַּדִּים מִמְּקוֹמָם, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַכְּרוּבִים שֶׁל מֹשֶׁה עוֹמְדִים הָיוּ לְמַעְלָה לָאָרוֹן עַל הַכַּפֹּרֶת, וּכְרוּבִים שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר כָּאן, אוֹתָם שֶׁעָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה, עוֹמְדִים עַל רַגְלֵיהֶם בַּקַּרְקַע, וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן אוֹתָן שֶׁל מֹשֶׁה עֲדַיִן הָיוּ אֲדוּקִין עַל הַכַּפֹּרֶת. כָּךְ פֵּרֵשׁ רַבִּי נָתָן. (וְרַבִּי פֵּרֵשׁ:) הַכֹּהֲנִים רָצוּ לְהַכְנִיס הָאָרוֹן עִם בַּדָּיו בַּדְּבִיר, וְלֹא הִסְפִּיקוּ, כִּי הֶעָנָן מָלֵא אֶת בֵּית ה׳, וְלֹא יָכְלוּ לְהָבִיא אֶת הַכְּרוּבִים עַד כּוֹתֶל מַעֲרָבִי מַמָּשׁ, וְעַל כֵּן הָיוּ בּוֹלְטִים בַּפָּרֹכֶת. אַךְ קָשֶׁה לִי, דִּכְתִיב **וַיְהִי בְּצֵאת הַכֹּהֲנִים** וְאַחַר כֵּן **מָלֵא כְבוֹד ה׳ אֶת בֵּית... אֱלֹהִים**. (וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵף זַצַ״ל פֵּרֵשׁ:) וַיִּתְאָרְכוּ לֹא נֶאֱמַר, דְּמַשְׁמַע וַיִּתְאָרְכוּ הֵן עַצְמָן, אֶלָּא **וַיַּאֲרִיכוּ**, דְּמַשְׁמַע אֲחֵרִים מַאֲרִיכִין אוֹתָם. וּמְפָרֵשׁ: הָאוּמָּנִין הֶאֱרִיכוּ הַבַּדִּים עַד לְעֶשְׂרִים, וְהָיוּ נוֹגְעִין שְׁתֵּי קְצוֹתָם לְכוֹתֶל מִזְרָחִי וּשְׁתֵּי קְצוֹתָם לְכוֹתֶל מַעֲרָבִי. וְתֵימָא יֵשׁ: וְהֵיאַךְ יָכוֹל לִפְרֹשׂ כַּנְפֵיהֶם וּלְכַסּוֹת הָאָרוֹן שֶׁהָיָה עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת מֵהֶן?!


**and the cherubim covered over the Ark and over its staves from above** above the Ark. So were the staves standing, and they extended so far out toward the east, that the Ark could stand exactly beneath the cherubim without the hindrance of the staves. Therefore, “and the cherubim covered over the Ark.” And they also covered part of the staves adjacent to the Ark, but not all of them, because they reached until the east. But you cannot explain that the cherubim covered the entire staves. And also, because of this—**And they extended the staves so that the ends of the staves were seen from the Ark before the Debir** For if it were not so, but [if they were] the same; i.e., if they were extended with one measure one from the other, [i.e., the part of the staves extending eastward equaled the part extending westward]. The staves that Moses made were still there, and they stood in the Holy of Holies of the Tent of Meeting, which was only ten [cubits], whereas this was twenty [cubits], and it reached from wall to wall. With the cherubim of Moses, about which it is also written, “spreading and covering,” they did not have to move any part of the staves from their place, because the cherubim of Moses were standing over the Ark on the Ark cover, but as for the cherubim mentioned here-Solomon made them standing on their feet on the ground, but nevertheless, those of Moses were still attached to the Ark cover. This is how Rabbi Nathan explained it, (but our Rabbi explained) that the priests wished to bring the Ark into the Debir with its staves, but they were unable to do so, because the Cloud filled the House of the Lord, and they could not bring the cherubim up to the western wall. Therefore, they [the staves] protruded into the dividing curtain. To me, this poses a difficulty, however, because it is written (I Kings 8:10f.): “And it came to pass when the priests came out, etc.,” and afterwards, “the glory of the Lord filled the House of God (sic).” (Rabbi Joseph of blessed memory, however, explained) that it does not say וַיִּתְאָרְכוּ [in the reflexive conjugation], which would mean that they became elongated by themselves, but וַיַּאֲרִיכוּ [in the causative conjugation], which means that others elongated them, and he explained that the craftsmen elongated them to twenty cubits, and their two ends touched the eastern wall and their [other] two ends touched the western wall. It is, however, puzzling how they could spread out their wings and cover the Ark, which was ten cubits from them.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:11:1)


**וַיְהִי בְּצֵאת הַכֹּהֲנִים מִן הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** מִיָּד הָיָה לוֹ לִסְמוֹךְ **וְהַבַּיִת מָלֵא עָנָן בֵּית ה׳**, אֶלָּא שֶׁמַּפְסִיק בֵּינְתַּיִם; וְכֵן בִּמְלָכִים ״וַיְהִי בְּצֵאת הַכֹּהֲנִים מִן הַקֹּדֶשׁ וְהֶעָנָן מָלֵא אֶת בֵּית ה׳״ (מלכים א ח:י):


**And it came to pass when the priests left the Holy** He should have immediately written, “and the House became full of the cloud of the House of the Lord,” but he interrupted in between, and so in I Kings (8:10): “And it came to pass, when the priests came out of the Holy, and the cloud filled the House of the Lord.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:11:2)


**כִּי כָל הַכֹּהֲנִים הַנִּמְצְאִים הִתְקַדָּשׁוּ.** לְדָבָר זֶה:


**for all the priests that were present had sanctified themselves** for this thing.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:11:3)


**אֵין לִשְׁמוֹר לְמַחְלְקוֹת.** עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע שֶׁל לְוִיִּם וְעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע שֶׁל כֹּהֲנִים, אֶלָּא **כָּל הַנִּמְצְאִים הִתְקַדָּשׁוּ**:


**not taking heed of the divisions** The twenty-four of the Levites and the twenty-four of the priests, but all those present sanctified themselves.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/5:13:1)


**וַיְהִי כְאֶחָד לַמְחַצְּרִים.** פִּתְרוֹן: וַיִּהְיוּ אֲחוּדִים יַחַד הַמְחַצְּרִים כֻּלָּם יַחַד. וּמִמָּה הָיוּ אֲחוּדִים? לְדָבָר זֶה: **לְהַשְׁמִיעַ קוֹל אֶחָד** כְּאֶחָד, **לְהַלֵּל וּלְהוֹדוֹת לַה׳**:


**And the trumpeters, etc. were as one** The meaning is: And they were united - all the trumpeters together. Now for what purpose were they united? To make one sound together to praise and to thank the Lord.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:1:1)


**אָז אָמַר שְׁלֹמֹה.** הוֹאִיל **וְהַבַּיִת מָלֵא** עָשָׁן, עַכְשָׁיו אֲנִי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁהַשְּׁכִינָה שְׁרוּיָה בּוֹ **בָּעֲרָפֶל**, כִּי כֵן דַּרְכּוֹ, דִּכְתִיב ״וּמֹשֶׁה נִגַּשׁ אֶל הָעֲרָפֶל אֲשֶׁר שָׁם הָאֱלֹהִים״ (שמות כ:יח); וַדַּאי שֶׁרְצוֹנוֹ לְהִתְעַכֵּב בּוֹ. וּבְסִפְרֵי פֵּרֵשׁ (מכילתא בא פסחא יב): **אָז אָמַר שְׁלֹמֹה.** כְּשֶׁרָאָה הֶעָנָן אָמַר: עַתָּה אֲנִי רוֹאֶה שֶׁהַשְּׁכִינָה שְׁרוּיָה בַּבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר בָּנִיתִי, שֶׁהֲרֵי כָּךְ הִבְטִיחַ לָבוֹא וְלִשְׁכֹּן בּוֹ מִתּוֹךְ עָנָן וַעֲרָפֶל; וְהֵיכָן אָמַר: ״כִּי בֶּעָנָן אֵרָאֶה עַל הַכַּפֹּרֶת״ (ויקרא טז:ב):


**Then Solomon said** Since the House is full of smoke, I now know that the Shechinah is resting in the thick darkness therein, for so is His wont, as it is written (Exod. 20:18): “and Moses drew near to the thick darkness wherein God appeared.” [Therefore, I deduce that] He surely wishes to stay therein, and in Sifre it is explained: Then Solomon said - when he saw the cloud, he said, “Now I see that the Shechinah rests in the House that I have built, for He promised to come and dwell therein out of the midst of the cloud and the thick darkness.” Now where did He say this? (Lev. 16:2): “for in the cloud I shall appear on the Ark cover.”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:2:1)


**בֵּית זְבוּל.** מָדוֹר:


**a House to dwell in** - Heb. בֵית זְבֻל a dwelling.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:2:2)


**לְשִׁבְתְּךָ עוֹלָמִים.** שֶׁמִּשֶּׁנִּבְחֲרָה לֹא שָׁרְתָה שְׁכִינָה בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר וְלֹא הֻתְּרוּ הַבָּמוֹת:


**for You to dwell in forever** For since it was selected, the Shechinah did not rest elsewhere, and the high places were prohibited.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:3:1)


**וַיְבָרֶךְ אֶת כָּל קְהַל יִשְׂרָאֵל.** בְּרָכָה הָאֲמוּרָה בְּסוֹף הָעִנְיָן (להלן פסוק יד ואילך):


**and blessed all the congregation of Israel** the blessing stated at the end of the episode.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:4:1)


**אֲשֶׁר דִּבֶּר בְּפִיו.** שֶׁיִּתֵּן לוֹ בֵּן שֶׁיִּבְנֶה לוֹ בַּיִת:


**Who spoke with His mouth** that He would give him a son who would build Him a House.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:4:2)


**מִלֵּא.** קִיֵּם דְּבָרוֹ הַטּוֹב:


**has fulfilled** He fulfilled His good word.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:5:1)


**מִן הַיּוֹם אֲשֶׁר הוֹצֵאתִי.** שֶׁאַף זֶה דִּבַּרְתָּ אֶל אָבִי:


**Since the day that I brought forth** For this too You spoke to my father.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:6:1)


**וָאֶבְחַר בִּירוּשָׁלִַם.** מוּסָב עַל **לֹא בָחַרְתִּי בְעִיר**:


**And I chose Jerusalem** This refers to “I chose no city.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:6:2)


**וָאֶבְחַר בְּדָוִיד.** מוּסָב עַל **וְלֹא בָחַרְתִּי בְאִישׁ**:


**and I chose David** This refers back to “neither did I choose a man.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:8:1)


**הֱטִיבוֹתָ כִּי הָיָה עִם לְבָבֶךָ.** כְּלוֹמַר: גַּם אֲנִי בּוֹחֵר בַּבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר עָלָה בְלִבְּךָ לִבְנוֹתוֹ, לְשַׁכֵּן שְׁמִי שָׁם; כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה (לעיל, ו):


**you did well that it was in your heart** i.e., I too choose the House that you thought to build, where My name shall dwell, as is stated above.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:12:1)


**וַיִּפְרֹשׂ כַּפָּיו.** הָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר כָּךְ: וַיִּפְרֹשׂ כַּפָּיו הַשָּׁמַיְמָה וַיֹּאמֶר וְגוֹ׳, אֶלָּא שֶׁמַּפְסִיק בֵּינְתַיִם לְהוֹדִיעַ עַל מָה עָמַד, וְכֵן בִּמְלָכִים (מלכים א ח:כב) ״וַיִּפְרֹשׂ כַּפָּיו הַשָּׁמַיְמָה וַיֹּאמֶר״:


**and he spread forth his hands** Scripture should have said the following: “and he spread forth his hands toward heaven, and he said, etc.,” but it interrupted in between to inform [us] on what he stood, and so in I Kings (8:22f.): “and he spread forth his hands towards heaven, and he said.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:13:1)


**כִּי עָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה.** מוּסָב עַל **נֶגֶד כָּל קְהַל יִשְׂרָאֵל**, כְּלוֹמַר: הֵיאַךְ הָיָה גָּבוֹהַּ מִכּוּלָּם לְהַשְׁמִיעַ דְּבָרָיו:


**For Solomon had made** This refers back to “opposite all the congregation of Israel,” i.e., how was he higher than all of them to make his words heard?


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:13:2)


**וַיַּעֲמֹד עָלָיו.** וְעַתָּה יְכוֹלִין לִרְאוֹתוֹ **כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל**. וּמִשּׁוּם שֶׁמַּפְסִיק הָעִנְיָן, צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר כְּבָרִאשׁוֹנָה: **נֶגֶד כָּל קְהַל יִשְׂרָאֵל** וְכֵן **וַיִּפְרֹשׂ כַּפָּיו הַשָּׁמָיְמָה**:


**and he stood on it** And now, all Israel could see him, and since he interrupted the topic, he had to repeat, “opposite all the congregation of Israel,” and so, “and he spread forth his hands toward heaven.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:14:1)


**אֵין כָּמוֹךָ אֱלֹהִים.** מִמָּה שֶׁאַתָּה **שׁוֹמֵר הַבְּרִית וְהַחֶסֶד**, שֶׁאַתָּה גּוֹזֵר מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם לָתֵת לְאוֹהֲבֶיךָ, אַתָּה מַצְנִיעוֹ וְגוֹנֵז **הַבְּרִית וְהַחֶסֶד** לְקַיֵּם הַבְטָחָתְךָ **בָּאָרֶץ**:


**there is no God like You** insofar as You keep the covenant and the kindness that You decree from heaven to give to those who love You. You hide and conceal the covenant and the kindness to fulfill Your promise on earth.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:14:2)


**הַהוֹלְכִים לְפָנֶיךָ.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָלַךְ דָּוִד אָבִי; וְזֶה **אֲשֶׁר שָׁמַרְתָּ לְעַבְדְּךָ דָוִד** – בִּלְשׁוֹן אֲמָנַת הַבְטָחָה:


**who walk before You** as my father David walked, and this is [the meaning of] **For You have kept for Your servant David** with an expression referring to the realization of a promise.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:18:1)


**כִּי הַאֻמְנָם יֵשֵׁב אֱלֹהִים אֶת הָאָדָם עַל הָאָרֶץ.** לְשׁוֹן תְּמִיהָּ: וְכִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיַּאֲמִין אָדָם שֶׁיֵּשֵׁב אֱלֹהִים עַל הָאָרֶץ! **כִּי שָׁמַיִם וּשְׁמֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם וְגוֹ׳**, אֶלָּא מֵחֲמַת חֶסֶד שֶׁגָּמַלְתָּ לְדָוִד אָבִי, צִמְצַמְתָּ שְׁכִינָתְךָ:


**But will God indeed dwell with man on the earth?** This is an expression of wonder. Is it possible for man to believe that God will dwell on the earth? For the heaven and the heaven of the heaven, etc. However, because of the kindness that You bestowed upon my father, David, You contracted Your Shechinah.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:18:2)


**אַף כִּי הַבַּיִת הַזֶּה אֲשֶׁר בָּנִיתִי.** וְלָמָּה בְּנִיתִיו? לְהִתְפַּלֵּל לִפְנֵי הָאֱלֹהִים וְלִפְנוֹת הוּא אֶל תְּפִלָּתֵינוּ:


**much less this House that I have built** So why did I build it? To pray before God and so that He should turn to our prayers.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:19:1)


**לִשְׁמֹעַ אֶל הָרִנָּה.** זוֹ צְעָקָה, כְּמוֹ ״וַתַּעֲבֹר הָרִנָּה בַּמַּחֲנֶה״ (מלכים א כב:לו). וְעַל כָּרְחֲךָ: עַל צַעֲקָתָם שֶׁל כָּל אָדָם שֶׁהוּא נָס מִפְּנֵי צָרָתוֹ, אַל יִתְפַּלֵּל אֶלָּא בִּצְעָקָתוֹ בְּסֵתֶר. כָּךְ חִבּוּר הַמִּקְרָאוֹת, וְסוֹף הָעִנְיָן מוֹכִיחַ, שֶׁאָמַר: **וְאַתָּה תִּשְׁמַע מִמְּקוֹם שִׁבְתְּךָ** – לָמַדְתָּ שֶׁלֹּא בְּנָאוֹ אֶלָּא לְדַעַת כֵּן, לְהוֹרִיד בּוֹ שְׁכִינָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּשֵׁב עַל הָאָרֶץ לִשְׁמֹעַ מִמְּכוֹן שִׁבְתְּךָ אֶל הַתְּפִלּוֹת שֶׁיִּתְפַּלְּלוּ בּוֹ:


**to the cry** - Heb. הָרִנָּה. This is a cry, like (I Kings 22:36): “A cry (הָרִנָּה) passed through the camp.” Perforce, concerning the cry of any man who flees in distress, he will not pray but express his cry in secret. This is the sequence of the verses, and the conclusion of the topic proves it, for he says, “You shall hear from Your abode.” You have thus learned that he built it only with this intention to bring down the Shechinah therein, in order that He dwell on the earth, to hear “from Your abode” the prayer that they will pray therein.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:20:1)


**לִהְיוֹת עֵינֶיךָ פְתֻחוֹת.** בְּכָל עֵת אֲשֶׁר יִתְפַּלְלוּ אֵלֶיךָ:


**That Your eyes may be open, etc.** whenever they pray to You.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:22:1)


**אִם יֶחֱטָא אִישׁ.** מִקְרָא זֶה עַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל הַמְחַיֵּב חֲבֵרוֹ שְׁבוּעָה: אִם בֶּאֱמֶת הוּא מַשְׁבִּיעוֹ, וְהַנִּשְׁבָּע נִשְׁבָּע לַשֶּׁקֶר, **אַתָּה תִשְׁמַע הַשָּׁמַיִם**, וְאִם הַדִּין עִם הַנִּשְׁבָּע, שֶׁבַּעַל הַדִּין מַזְקִיקוֹ עַל שְׁבוּעָה שֶׁל חִנָּם, אַף הַמַּשְׁבִּיעוֹ קָרוּי רָשָׁע:


**If a man sins** This verse refers to all Israel; whoever exacts an oath from his neighbor - if he is adjuring him legitimately, and the one swearing is swearing falsely, You shall hear from heaven. If, on the other hand, justice is on the side of the one who swears, for the litigant exacts an oath from him for naught, even the one who exacts the oath is called wicked.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:23:1)


**וּלְהַצְדִּיק צַדִּיק.** שֶׁלֹּא לְעָנְשׁוֹ עַל הַשְּׁבוּעָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי בֶּאֱמֶת הוּא נִשְׁבַּע. וְתוֹסֶפְתָּא דְּסוֹטָה מְפָרֵשׁ (תוספתא סוטה א:ג): אֶת אֲשֶׁר יֶחֱטָא – בּוֹעֵל אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ; **וְנָשָׂא בוֹ אָלָה** – וִיחַיֵּב בּוֹ אָלָה, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁהַמַּיִם בּוֹדְקִים אוֹתָהּ כָּךְ הַמַּיִם בּוֹדְקִין אוֹתוֹ; **לִפְנֵי מִזְבְּחֶךָ** – כְּדִכְתִיב ״וְהֶעֱמִיד הַכֹּהֵן אֶת הָאִשָּׁה״ (במדבר ה:יח):


**and to justify the righteous** not to punish him for the oath, for he swore in truth. *Tosefta* of *Sotah* (1: 3) explains [this verse as referring to the rite of the sotah, the married woman accused of adultery Num. 5:11-30)]: If a man sins by being intimate with a married woman. **and an oath be exacted of him** and an oath makes him guilty, for just as the water tests her, so does the water test him. before Your altar - as it is written (Num. 5:18): “And the priest shall have the woman stand [before the Lord].”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:23:2)


**לָתֵת דַּרְכּוֹ בְּרֹאשׁוֹ.** כְּדִכְתִיב ״וְצָבְתָה בִטְנָהּ״ (במדבר ה:כז):


**to bring his way upon his head** - as it is written (ibid. verse 27): “her belly will swell and her thigh will fall away.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:23:3)


**וּלְהַצְדִּיק צַדִּיק.** דִּכְתִיב ״וְנִקְּתָה וְנִזְרְעָה זָרַע״ (במדבר ה:כח):


**and to justify the righteous** - (ibid. verse 28): “and she will be blessed with offspring.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:24:1)


**וְאִם יִנָּגֵף עַמְּךָ.** וְלֹא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיָּדְךָ קְצָרָה מִלְּהוֹשִׁיעַ, אֶלָּא דָּבָר זֶה גּוֹרֵם: **כִּי יֶחֶטְאוּ לָךְ**:


**And if Your people...is struck down** not because You have no power, but because they will sin against You.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:24:2)


**וְשָׁבוּ וְהוֹדוּ אֶת שְׁמֶךָ.** פִּתְרוֹן: וְהִתְוַדּוּ אֶת עֲוֹנָם (ויקרא כו:מ), וְהָאַחֲרוֹן מוֹכִיחַ: **וְהוֹדוּ אֶת שְׁמֶךָ וּמֵחַטָּאתָם יְשׁוּבוּן כִּי תַעֲנֵם**:


**and confess** - Heb. וְהוֹדוּ. The meaning is: and they will confess their iniquity, and the latter verse proves this (verse 26): “and confess to Your name and repent of their sin, so that You may answer them.”



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:25:1)


**וְהַשִׁיבוֹתָם.** וְהָשֵׁב אוֹתָם **אֶל הָאֲדָמָה**; כִּי כְּשֶׁנּוֹפְלִים וְנִגָּפִים לִפְנֵי אוֹיְבֵיהֶם, תּוֹפְשִׂים מַה שֶּׁיְּכוֹלִים וּמְבִיאִים אוֹתָם בְּשִׁבְיָה אֶל אֶרֶץ אַחֶרֶת:


**and bring them back to the land** For when they fall and are struck down before their enemies, they [their enemies] will seize what they can and will bring them into captivity to another land.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:26:1)


**בְּהֵעָצֵר הַשָּׁמַיִם.** כְּדִכְתִיב ״וְעָצַר אֶת הַשָּׁמַיִם וְלֹא יִהְיֶה מָטָר״ (דברים יא:יז):


**When heaven is shut up** as it is written (Deut. 11:17): “and He will shut up the heaven, and there will be no rain.”



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:27:1)


**כִּי תוֹרֵם אֶל הַדֶּרֶךְ.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁעָשִׂיתָ כְּשֶׁהָיָה רָעָב בִּימֵי דָוִד שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים (שמואל ב כא:א), וְהוֹרֵיתָ וְאָמַרְתָּ לָהֶם: ״אֶל שָׁאוּל וְאֶל בֵּית הַדָּמִים״ (שמואל ב כא:ב), כְּמוֹ כֵן תּוֹרֵם אֵיזֶה חֵטְא גּוֹרֵם לָהֶם עַל מָה נֶעֶצְרוּ הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**for You will teach them the good way** as You did when there was a famine in David’s time for three years (II Sam. 21:1), and You taught them and said to them, “(It is) for Saul and (also) for his bloody house.” Likewise, so shall You teach them which sin causes them [their distress], for what reason the heaven was shut up.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:28:1)


**כָּל נֶגַע.** פִּתְרוֹן: הֲרֵי זָכַרְתִּי לְךָ דֶּבֶר, **שִׁדָּפוֹן וְיֵרָקוֹן**, נְגִיפָה, אַף בַּעֲצִירַת גְּשָׁמִים, וּמָה עוֹד לְהַרְבּוֹת? אֶלָּא **כָּל נֶגַע וְכָל מַחֲלָה כִּי יִהְיֶה** וְהִתְפַּלֵּל:


**whatsoever plague, whatsoever sickness (there be)** The meaning is: I have mentioned to You pestilence, blast and mildew, being struck down [in battle], and lack of rainfall, and what else can I include, but whatsoever plague, whatsoever sickness there may be, and he pray and spread forth his hands toward this House, and this is what it says.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:29:1)


**וּפָרַשׂ כַּפָּיו אֶל הַבַּיִת הַזֶּה.** וְזֶה שֶׁאָמַר: **כָּל תְּפִלָּה** וְ**כָל תְּחִנָּה** שֶׁיִּתְפַּלֵּל וְיִתְחַנֵּן עַל זֹאת מִכָּל אֶחָד הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה:


**Any prayer, any supplication** that he will pray and supplicate about, concerning any of these matters.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:29:2)


**אֲשֶׁר יֵדְעוּ אִישׁ נִגְעוֹ.** כְּלוֹמַר: אִם יִתְפַּלֵּל עַל נִגְעוֹ וְעַל **מַכְאֹבוֹ** לַהֲסִירוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ:


**every man shall know his plague** i.e., if he pray about his affliction or about his sickness to remove it from him.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:30:1)


**וְסָלַחְתָּ וְנָתַתָּה לָאִישׁ כְּכָל דְּרָכָיו וְגוֹ׳.** לָרָשָׁע כְּרִשְׁעָתוֹ וְלַצַּדִּיק כְּצִדְקָתוֹ (דברים ז:י); שֶׁאִם לֹא יְשַׁלֵּם לָרָשָׁע כְּפִי רוֹעַ מַעַלְלֵיו, יַטִּיחַ דְּבָרִים כְּלַפֵּי מַעְלָה וְיֹאמַר: ״אֵין אֱלֹהִים״ (תהלים יד:א) (אָז) יוֹדֵעַ לְבָבוֹ וְיִהְיֶה חִלּוּל הַשֵּׁם; כְּמוֹ כֵן תַּעֲשֶׂה לַצַּדִּיק כְּצִדְקָתוֹ, שֶׁתַּעֲשֶׂה לוֹ כְּשֶׁיִּתְפַּלֵּל לְפָנֶיךָ:


**and forgive and give to each man according to his ways, etc.** to the wicked according to his wickedness and to the righteous according to his righteousness. For if He does not repay the wicked according to his wickedness, he will speak insolently toward Heaven, and he will say, “There is no God.” Then [the thoughts of] his heart will be known, and there will be a profanation of the Name [of God]. Likewise shall You do to the righteous according to his righteousness, which You shall do for him when he prays to You.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:31:1)


**לְמַעַן יִירָאוּךָ.** וְיָשׁוּב הָרָשָׁע מִדַּרְכּוֹ, וּלְכָךְ אֲנִי מִתְפַּלֵּל לָתֵת לוֹ לָאִישׁ ״כִּדְרָכָיו״ (ירמיה לב:יט):


**That they may fear You** and the wicked will repent of his way; therefore, I pray to give each man according to his ways.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:33:1)


**וְעָשִׂיתָ כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָא אֵלֶיךָ הַנָּכְרִי.** בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲנִי מִתְפַּלֵּל לָתֵת לוֹ כִּדְרָכָיו (לעיל, ל), אֲבָל לְעוֹבֵד גִּלּוּלִים: כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָא לְךָ. לְפִי שֶׁיִּשְׂרָאֵל מַכִּירִין בְּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וְיוֹדְעִין שֶׁהַיְּכֹלֶת בְּיָדוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת, אִם אֵין תְּפִלָּתוֹ נִשְׁמַעַת, תּוֹלֶה בְּעַצְמוֹ בַּחֲטָאָיו וּמְפַשְׁפֵּשׁ בְּמַעֲשָׂיו, אֲבָל הָעוֹבֵד גִּלּוּלִים קוֹרֵא תִּגָּר וְאוֹמֵר: שָׁמַעְתִּי שִׁמְעוֹ בְּכָל הָעוֹלָם, וְנִתְיַגַּעְתִּי בַּדְּרָכִים הַרְבֵּה עַד שֶׁבָּאתִי וְהִתְפַּלַּלְתִּי בַּמָּקוֹם הַזֶּה, וְלֹא מָצָאתִי בּוֹ מַמָּשׁ כִּשְׁאָר אֱלָהוּת; לְפִיכָךְ: וְעָשִׂיתָ כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָא אֵלֶיךָ הַנָּכְרִי:


**and You shall do whatever the stranger calls upon You** Concerning an Israelite, I pray to give him according to his ways, but concerning a heathen, according to all the stranger calls upon You, because Israelites recognize the Holy One, blessed be He, and they know that He has the ability to do, and if his prayer is not heard, he assigns the cause to himself, to his sins, and he searches his deeds, but the heathen will complain of injustice and say, “I heard His fame throughout the world, and I toiled on many roads until I came and prayed in this place, and I did not find reality in Him any more than the other deities.” Therefore, You shall do whatever the stranger calls upon You.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:34:1)


**דֶּרֶךְ הָעִיר.** פְּנֵיהֶם לְצַד יְרוּשָׁלַיִם:


**toward the city** With their faces toward Jerusalem.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:35:1)


**וְעָשִׂיתָ מִשְׁפָּטָם.** נִקְמָתָם בְּאוֹיְבֵיהֶם. עִנְיָן אַחֵר: צָרְכֵיהֶם; דֻּגְמָא: ״לַעֲשׂוֹת מִשְׁפַּט עַבְדּוֹ״ (מלכים א ח:נט):


**and maintain their cause** their revenge from their enemies. Another meaning is: their necessities. A similar instance is (I Kings 8:59): “that He sustain the cause of His servant.”



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:38:1)


**אֲשֶׁר בָּנִיתִי לִשְׁמֶךָ.** פִּתְרוֹן: הַ**בַּיִת** הַזֶּה אֲשֶׁר בָּנִיתִי כְּדֵי לְגַדֵּל שְׁמֶךָ, כִּי אָז, כְּשֶׁתְּקַבֵּל תְּפִלָּתָם, אָז יִתְפַּלְּלוּ לְךָ בַּבַּיִת הַזֶּה אוֹ בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁהֵם וְיִפְנוּ לְבֵיתְךָ זֶה:


**that I built for Your name** The meaning is: this House, which I built in order to aggrandize Your name, for when You accept their prayer, they will pray to You in this House; wherever they are, they will turn to this, Your House.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:39:1)


**וְשָׁמַעְתָּ תְּפִלָּתָם.** וְעַל זֶה יִתְגַּדֵּל שִׁמְךָ בָּעוֹלָם:


**You shall hear, etc.** And through this, Your name will be aggrandized in the world.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:41:1)


**וְעַתָּה קוּמָה ה׳.** שֶׁלֹּא לְהִטַּלְטֵל כַּאֲשֶׁר עַד עַתָּה: מִשִּׁילֹה לְנוֹב וּמִנּוֹב לְגִבְעוֹן:


**And now, arise, O Lord** so as not to have to move as until now, from Shiloh to Nob and from Nob to Gibeon.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:41:2)


**אַתָּה וַאֲרוֹן עֻזֶּךָ.** שֶׁלֹּא יִטַּלְטֵל עוֹד מֵעַתָּה:


**You and the Ark of Your might** that it will no longer move from now on.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:41:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:41:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:41:3)


**עֻזֶּךָ.** עַל שֵׁם כֵּן קָרוּי עֻזֶּךָ, עַל שֶׁעָשָׂה נִפְלְאוֹתָיו וַעֲזוּזוֹת בַּפְּלִשְׁתִּים; וְכֵן כְּתִיב בְּ״הוֹדוּ לַה׳״ (דברי הימים א טז:ח): ״דִּרְשׁוּ ה׳ וְעֻזּוֹ״ (דברי הימים א טז:יא). וְעוֹד אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים (עבודה זרה כד:): ״וַיִּשַּׁרְנָה הַפָּרוֹת״ (שמואל א ו:יב) – מָה שִׁירָה אָמְרוּ? ״הוֹשִׁיעָה לּוֹ יְמִינוֹ וּזְרוֹעַ קָדְשׁוֹ״ (תהלים צח:א):


**Your might** Because of the following it is called “Your might”: because it performed wonders and mighty deeds among the Philistines. And so it is written above (I Chron 16:8-36): “Give thanks to the Lord,” “Search for the Lord and His might.” Our Sages stated further in Tractate *Abodah Zarah* 24b (I Sam. 6:12): “And the cows sang.” What song did they recite? They said, (Ps. 98:1) “His right hand and His holy arm have gained Him the victory.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:41:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:41:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:41:4)


**יִלְבְּשׁוּ תְשׁוּעָה.** מֵחֲמַת שֶׁלֹּא יִטַּלְטְלוּ מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם; כְּמוֹ ״וְהָיָה צֶדֶק אֵזוֹר מָתְנָיו״ (ישעיהו יא:ה):


**shall be attired with salvation** because they will not wander from place to place, like (Isa. 11:5): “And righteousness shall be the girdle of his loins.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:41:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:41:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:41:5)


**וַחֲסִידֶיךָ יִשְׂמְחוּ בַטּוֹב.** בְּרוֹב טוֹבוֹת וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת הַנִּתָּן לַלְוִיִּם בִּשְׂכַר שֶׁשּׁוֹמְרִין אֶת הָאָרוֹן וּמְשׁוֹרְרִים לְפָנָיו:


**and Your pious ones shall rejoice with the goodness** with the abundance of goodness and tithes that will be given to the Levites as reward for guarding the Ark and singing before it.



### Verse 42

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 6:42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/6:42:1)


**אַל תָּשֵׁב פְּנֵי מְשִׁיחֶךָ.** מִלַּעֲשׂוֹת טוֹבָה עִמָּדִי; וְאִם לֹא לְמַעֲנִי, עֲשֵׂה לְמַעַנְךָ:


**do not turn back the face of Your anointed one** by not treating me with goodness, and if not for my sake, do it for Your sake.



## Chapter 7


### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:6:1)


**וְהַכֹּהֲנִים עַל מִשְׁמְרוֹתָם.** כַּאֲשֶׁר חִלְּקָן דָּוִד לְעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה מִשְׁמָרוֹת (דברי הימים א כד:א-יט), לְשָׁרֵת כָּל מִשְׁמָרָה וּמִשְׁמָרָה שָׁבוּעַ שֶׁלָּהּ, וְהַלְוִיִּם כְּמוֹ כֵן עַל עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה מִשְׁמָרוֹת שֶׁלָּהֶם (דברי הימים א כה:א-לא):


**And the priests...on their watches** as David had divided them into twenty-four watches, for each watch to serve its week, and the Levites likewise on their twenty-four watches


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:6:2)


**לְהוֹדוֹת.** בִּכְלֵי שִׁיר, שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹמְרִים: ״הוֹדוּ לַה׳ כִּי טוֹב״ (דברי הימים א טז:לד), **כִּי לְעוֹלָם חַסְדּוֹ**:


**to give thanks** with musical instruments, for they recited (Ps. 136): “Give thanks to the Lord for He is good, for His loving kindness is eternal.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:6:3)


**בְּהַלֵּל דָּוִיד.** הַכְּלִי שִׁיר שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹמְרִין בּוֹ הַלֵּל שֶׁל דָּוִד, וְהַהוֹדָיוֹת **בְּיָדָם** שֶׁל לְוִיִּם:


**with David’s praise** The musical instruments with which they recited David’s praise and the thanksgivings were in the Levites’ hands.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:6:4)


**וְהַכֹּהֲנִים.** מַחְצְרִים נֶגְדָּם, שֶׁעֲלֵיהֶם לְשׁוֹרֵר בַּחֲצוֹצְרוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּבְנֵי אַהֲרֹן הַכֹּהֲנִים יִתְקָעוּ וְגוֹ׳״ (במדבר י:ח):


**and the priests were sounding the trumpets opposite them** For it was incumbent upon them to play music with the trumpets, as it is said (Num. 10: 8): “And the sons of Aaron, the priests, shall blow [with the trumpets].”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:7:1)


**וַיְקַדֵּשׁ שְׁלֹמֹה אֶת תּוֹךְ הֶחָצֵר.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ חוֹלְקִין בְּדָבָר זֶה (זבחים נט.-ס.); רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר: קִדֵּשׁ אֶת רִצְפַּת הָעֲזָרָה בִּקְדֻשַּׁת מִזְבֵּחַ, לְהַקְטִיר עַל הָרִצְפָּה, ״כִּי מִזְבַּח הַנְּחֹשֶׁת... קָטָן מֵהָכִיל״ (מלכים א ח:סד), שֶׁרַבּוּ קָרְבָּנוֹת לְהָבִיא; אָמַר לוֹ רַבִּי יוֹסֵי: וַהֲלֹא כְּבָר נֶאֱמַר (מלכים א ג:ד) ״אֶלֶף עֹלוֹת יַעֲלֶה שְׁלֹמֹה עַל״ מִזְבֵּחַ שֶׁעָשָׂה מֹשֶׁה, וּכְשֶׁאַתָּה מַגִּיעַ לְחֶשְׁבּוֹן אַמּוֹת וּמִנְיַן עוֹלוֹת, תִּמְצָא שֶׁזֶּה שֶׁל אֲבָנִים גָּדוֹל מִשֶּׁל מֹשֶׁה, שֶׁהַמִּזְבֵּחַ שֶׁל מֹשֶׁה מְקוֹם מַעֲרַכְתּוֹ אַמָּה עַל אַמָּה וְשֶׁל שְׁלֹמֹה מְקוֹם מַעֲרַכְתּוֹ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע עַל עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע – הֲרֵי שֶׁל שְׁלֹמֹה חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת וְשִׁבְעִים וְשֵׁשׁ כְּשֶׁל מֹשֶׁה! אִם כֵּן מַה הוּא קִדֵּשׁ הַמֶּלֶךְ אֶת תּוֹךְ הֶחָצֵר? שֶׁקָּבַע בּוֹ מִזְבֵּחַ שֶׁל אֲבָנִים מְחֻבָּר בָּרִצְפָּה:


**And Solomon consecrated the interior of the court** Our Sages differ in this matter (*Zeb.* 59): Rabbi Judah says: He consecrated the floor of the forecourt with the sanctity of the altar to burn offerings on the floor, because the copper altar was too small to contain [the offerings], because they brought many sacrifices. Rabbi Jose said to him: Was it not already stated (1:6): “And he offered up on it a thousand burnt offerings” on the altar that Moses had made? When you compute the number of cubits and the number of burnt offerings, you will find that this one of stones was larger than that of Moses, for on Moses’ altar, the place of its pyre was a cubit by a cubit, whereas on Solomon’s, the place of its pyre was twenty-four by twenty-four. The result is that Solomon’s [altar] was 576 times as large as that of Moses. If so, what is the meaning of (I Kings 8:64): “the king consecrated the interior of the court”? That he set the stone altar into it, fastened to the floor.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:7:2)


**לֹא יָכוֹל לְהָכִיל.** בְּשֶׁל מֹשֶׁה הוּא אוֹמֵר, כְּאָדָם הָאוֹמֵר: נַנָּס פְּלוֹנִי וּפָסוּל לַעֲבוֹדָה:


**could not contain** He is talking about Moses’ [altar], like a person who says, “So and so is a dwarf and is disqualified from performing the sacrificial service.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:8:1)


**מִלְּבוֹא חֲמָת.** שֶׁהוּא בַּצָּפוֹן שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**from the entrance of Hamath** which is in the north of the land of Israel.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:8:2)


**עַד נַחַל מִצְרַיִם.** שֶׁהִיא כְּנֶגְדּוֹ, כְּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּאֵלֶּה מַסְעֵי (במדבר לד:ג-ה; ז-ט):


**to the brook of Egypt** which is opposite it, as is delineated in אֵלֶּה מַסְעֵי (ibid. verse 5).



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:10:1)


**וּבְיוֹם עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁלֹשָׁה לַחֹדֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִי שִׁלַּח אֶת הָעָם.** שֶׁהוּא תְּשִׁיעִי לַחֲנֻכַּת הַבַּיִת; וּבִמְלָכִים (מלכים א ח:סו) כָּתוּב ״וּבְיוֹם הַשְּׁמִינִי״, שֶׁהוּא עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁנַיִם לַחֹדֶשׁ! פִּתְרוֹן: בְּיוֹם שְׁמִינִי נָתַן לָהֶם רְשׁוּת לֵילֵךְ לְאַחַר הֶחָג, וּלְמָחָר כְּשֶׁיָּצָא הֶחָג אָז הָלְכוּ כֻּלָּם יַחַד. עִנְיָן אַחֵר: בְּיוֹם שְׁמִינִי נָתַן לָהֶם רְשׁוּת וְשִׁלַּח אוֹתָם הַיּוֹשְׁבִים בְּתוֹךְ תְּחוּם שַׁבָּת, וְכָל מִי שֶׁיִּרְצֶה לִיסַע וְלֵילֵךְ בְּאוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם בְּאוֹתוֹ הַתְּחוּם, וּלְמָחָר שִׁלַּח כָּל הָעָם כֻּלּוֹ. וּבִבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה לה:ג) מְפָרֵשׁ: נָטְלוּ רְשׁוּת מִמֶּנּוּ וְהִמְתִּינוּ עוֹד שָׁם יוֹם אֶחָד, וְחָזְרוּ וְנָטְלוּ רְשׁוּת עוֹד לְמָחָר פַּעַם שְׁנִיָּה, לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: **בְּיוֹם עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁלֹשָׁה בַּחֹדֶשׁ**:


**And on the twenty-third day of the seventh month, he dismissed the people** which was the ninth [day] of the inauguration of the Temple, but in I Kings (8:66) it is written: “On the eighth day,” which was the twenty-second of the month. The meaning is: On the eighth day he gave them permission to leave after the Feast, and on the morrow, when the Feast was over, they all went [home] together. Another meaning: On the eighth day he gave them permission and dismissed those who lived within the Sabbath limits and whoever wished to travel and go on that day in that limit, and on the following day, he dismissed the entire populace. In *Genesis Rabbah* (35:3) it is explained that they took his leave but waited there one more day and then they repeated [this procedure] and took leave again a second time; therefore it is said: “on the twenty-third day of the month.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:10:2)


**שְׂמֵחִים וְטוֹבֵי לֵב וְגוֹ׳.** לְדָוִיד וְלִשְׁלֹמֹה כִּי מַה שֶׁדִּבֵּר בְּפִיו ״בְּנָךְ שְׁלֹמֹה הוּא יִבְנֶה בַיִת לִשְׁמִי״ (דברי הימים א כח: ג, ו) מִלֵּא בְּיָדוֹ, שֶׁנָּתַן לוֹ בֵּן יוֹשֵׁב עַל כִּסְאוֹ שֶׁבָּנָה הַבַּיִת לְשֵׁם ה׳. וּבִבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה לה:ג) מְפָרֵשׁ: אָמַר רַבִּי לֵוִי כְּתִיב **כִּי חֲנֻכַּת וְגוֹ׳ שְׂמֵחִים** מוּסָב עַל אֲשֶׁר אָמַר ה׳ לְדָוִד, שֶׁקִּיֵּם מַה שֶׁנָּדַר לִשְׁלֹמֹה שֶׁבָּחַר בּוֹ מִכָּל אֶחָיו כִּי חָכָם הוּא:


**rejoicing and delighted of heart, etc. for David, for Solomon** For that which He spoke with His mouth above (I Chron 28:6): “Your son Solomon - he shall build My House,” He fulfilled with His hand, for He gave him a son sitting on his throne, who built a House in the name of the Lord. And in *Genesis Rabbah* it is explained: Said Rabbi Levi: It is written: “for the inauguration of, etc.” “Rejoicing” refers to “that the Lord had wrought for David,” viz. that He fulfilled that which He had vowed to Solomon, that He chose him from all his brothers because he was wise.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:10:3)


**וְלִיִשְׂרָאֵל עַמּוֹ.** כְּדִכְתִיב בִּמְלָכִים ״לֹא נָפַל דָּבָר אֶחָד וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים א ח:נו). עִנְיָן אַחֵר: **וְלִיִשְׂרָאֵל עַמּוֹ** – שֶׁהָיָה לָהֶם מוֹשֵׁל, שֶׁהָיוּ יוֹשְׁבִין בְּבִטָּחוֹן וּבְהַשְׁקֵט, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וַיֵּשֶׁב יְהוּדָה וְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים א ה:ה):


**and for Israel His people** it is written in I Kings (8:56): “there has not failed one word, etc.” Another explanation: “and for Israel His people” - they had a ruler, so that they dwelt safely and tranquilly, as it is written (ibid. 5:5): “And Judah and Israel dwelt, etc.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:12:1)


**וַיֵּרָא ה׳ אֶל שְׁלֹמֹה בַּלָּיְלָה.** בְּחֶזְיוֹן לָיְלָה:


**And the Lord appeared to Solomon at night** in a vision of the night.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:12:2)


**שָׁמַעְתִּי אֶת תְּפִלָּתֶךָ.** בְּלָשׁוֹן שֶׁאָמַר שְׁלֹמֹה: ״וְשָׁמַעְתָּ״ (דברי הימים ב ו:לט), הֵשִׁיב הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: שָׁמַעְתִּי תְּפִלָּתֶךָ; הוּא הִתְפַּלֵּל: ״בְּהֵעָצֵר הַשָּׁמַיִם״ (דברי הימים ב ו:כו), הֵשִׁיב הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: **הֵן אֶעֱצֹר**, הוּא הִתְפַּלֵּל ״רָעָב״ וְ״אַרְבֶּה״ (דברי הימים ב ו:כח), וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְשִׁיבוֹ: **וְהֵן אֲצַוֶּה עַל חָגָב**, הוּא הִתְפַּלֵּל עַל ״דֶּבֶר״ (דברי הימים ב ו:כח), וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְשִׁיבוֹ: **וְאִם אֲשַׁלַּח דֶּבֶר בְּעַמִּי**:


**I have heard your prayer** With the wording that Solomon used, “And You shall hear.” The Holy One, blessed be He, answered him: “I have heard your prayer.” He prayed, (above 6:26): “When heaven is closed up.” And the Holy One, blessed be He, answered him, “If I close up.” He prayed concerning famine and locusts, and the Holy One, blessed be He, answered him, “if I command locusts.” He prayed concerning pestilence, and the Holy One, blessed be He, answered him, “or if I send pestilence upon My people.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:12:3)


**וְיִכָּנְעוּ עַמִּי.** בְּמַה שֶׁיִּתְפַּלְלוּ וִיבַקְשׁוּ פָנַי וְיָשׁוּבוּ מִדַּרְכֵיהֶם הָרָעִים. הֲרֵי שָׁלֹשׁ כְּנֶגֶד שָׁלֹשׁ:


**and My people... humble themselves”** by praying and seeking My presence and repenting of their evil ways. Hence we have three [responses] corresponding to three [entreaties].



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:13:1)


**הֵן אֶעֱצֹר וְהֵן אֲצַוֶּה וְאִם אֲשַׁלַּח.** כְּנֶגֶד שָׁלֹשׁ תְּפִלּוֹת; וַאֲנִי אֶשְׁמַע וְאֶסְלַח לְחַטָּאתָם וְאֶרְפָּא אֶת אַרְצָם:


**If I shut up... if I command...or if I send** corresponding to three prayers, “I shall hear...and forgive their sin, and heal their land.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:16:1)


**וְהָיוּ עֵינַי וְלִבִּי.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּתְהֵי שְׁכִינְתִּי שַׁרְיָא בֵּיהּ אִם רְעוּתִי מִתְעַבְּדָא תַּמָּן כָּל יוֹמַיָּא״; וְהָיוּ עֵינַי **שָׁם**, אִם לִבִּי וְחֶפְצִי שָׁם:


**and My eyes and heart will be** Jonathan renders (I Kings 9:3): “and My presence will rest there if My will is performed there at all times.” My eyes will be there if My heart and My will are there.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:20:1)


**אַשְׁלִיךְ מֵעַל פָּנַי.** כַּתְּנַאי שֶׁבֵּינִי וּבֵינֵיכֶם: ״וְאִם לֹא תִשְׁמְעוּ לִי״ (ויקרא כו:יד) – מַה נֶּאֱמַר? ״וַהֲשִׁמּוֹתִי אֶת מִקְדְּשֵׁיכֶם״ (ויקרא כו:לא):


**I shall cast from before Me** according to the condition between Me and you, viz. (Lev. 26: 14): “But if you do not heed Me.” What does it say there? (verse 31): “And I shall make your sanctuaries desolate.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:20:2)


**וְאֶתְּנֶנּוּ לְמָשָׁל וְלִשְׁנִינָה.** כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁמּוֹשְׁלִים מָשָׁל עַל מִי שֶׁאֵרַע לוֹ דְּבַר פֶּגַע:


**and I shall make it a proverb** as people compose a proverb about someone who was befallen by misfortune.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:20:3)


**וְלִשְׁנִינָה.** כְּמוֹ ״וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ״ (דברים ו:ז): ״וְתַתְנִינוּן״ (תרגום אונקלוס); לְשׁוֹן סִפּוּר רָעוֹת; וְכֵן תַּרְגּוּם ״וַיְסַפֵּר״ (בראשית כד:סו): ״וְאִשְׁתָּעֵי״ (תרגום אונקלוס); ״וּלְשׁוּעֵי״ (תרגום אונקלוס לדברים כח:לז):


**and a byword** - Heb. וְלִשְׁנִינָה, like (Deut. 6:7): וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ, “and you shall teach them.” [It is] an expression of recounting misfortunes. Similarly, the Targum of (Gen. 37:10) וַיְסַפֵּר “And he told,” is וְאִשְׁתָּעֵי, and [the *Targum* of] וְלִשְׁנִינָה is וּלְשׁוּעֵי.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:21:1)


**לְכָל עֹבֵר עָלָיו יִשֹּׁם.** כְּלוֹמַר: יֶחֱרַב הַבַּיִת, וְאָז כָּל עֹבֵר עָלָיו יִשֹּׁם; כְּמוֹ ״עַל יוֹמוֹ נָשַׁמּוּ אַחֲרוֹנִים״ (איוב יח:כ):


**every passerby will be astounded** i.e., The Temple will be destroyed, and every passerby will then be astounded (יִשֹּׁם), like (Job 18:20): “Concerning his day - the later people shall be astonished (נָשַׁמּוּ).”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 7:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/7:21:2)


**בַּמֶּה עָשָׂה ה׳.** כְּמוֹ ״עַל מָה עָשָׂה ה׳״ (דברים כט:כג), וְכֵן כְּתִיב (מלכים א ט:ח):


**Why has the Lord done** בַּמֶּה, like (Deut. 29:23): “Why (עַל מֶה) has the Lord done thus,” and so it is written in I Kings (9:9).



## Chapter 8


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:1:1)


**וַיְהִי מִקֵּץ עֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה.** פִּתְרוֹן: מִקֵּץ עֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה שֶׁנִּגְמַר כָּל הַבִּנְיָן שֶׁל **בֵּית ה׳** שֶׁבַע שָׁנִים **וְאֶת בֵּיתוֹ** שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנִים, כְּדִכְתִיב בִּמְלָכִים (מלכים א ו:לח – ז:א):


**Now it came to pass at the end of twenty years** The meaning is: at the end of twenty years that the entire construction had been completed: that of the House of the Lord seven years and that of his palace thirteen years, as it is written in I Kings (6:38, 7:1).



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:4:1)


**וַיִּבֶן אֶת תַּדְמֹר בַּמִּדְבָּר.** וּבִמְלָכִים (מלכים א ט:יח) כְּתִיב ״אֶת תָּמוֹר״ וְ״תַדְמֹר״ קָרִינָן, וְזֶה שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בִּיבָמוֹת (טז.) אֵין מְקַבְּלִים גֵּרִים מִן הַתַּרְמוֹדִים. וּבִבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה נו:יט) בְּפָרָשַׁת עֲקֵידָה מְפָרֵשׁ, שֶׁעָזְרוּ לְשׂוֹנְאֵיהֶם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל, וּלְפִי שֶׁהֵמִירוּ עַצְמָם וְנִתְחַלְּפוּ לְרָעָה בַּסּוֹף, עַל כֵּן נִכְתַּב בַּמְּלָכִים ״תָּמוֹר״ לְשׁוֹן תְּמוּרָה, שֶׁהָיָה לָהֶם לַעֲשׂוֹת לְיִשְׂרָאֵל חֶסֶד עָשָׂה עִמָּהֶם שְׁלֹמֹה, שֶׁבָּנָה לָהֶם כְּרַךְ תַּדְמֹר. וּמִשּׁוּם שְׁלֹמֹה לֹא נִכְתַּב כָּאן ״תָּמוֹר״ אֶלָּא תַּדְמֹר, כְּלוֹמַר: עִיר חֲשׁוּבָה; הַכֹּל לִכְבוֹד שְׁלֹמֹה, שֶׁאֵין כְּבוֹד שְׁלֹמֹה לוֹמַר כָּאן הָא בָּנָה כְּרַךְ שֶׁמָּרְדוּ בּוֹ וְקִלְקְלוּ בּוֹ; וְעַל כֵּן כָּתַב תַּדְמֹר וְלֹא ״תָּמוֹר״:


**And he built Tadmor in the desert** In I Kings (9:18) it [the name] is written “Tamar,” but read, “Tadmor.” This accounts for that which we learned in Tractate *Jebamoth* (16a): We may not accept proselytes from the Tarmodites; and in *Genesis Rabbah* (56:11), in the portion dealing with the binding [of Isaac] it is explained that they aided Israel’s enemies, and because they changed themselves and became worse, the word תָּמָר is used in Kings, an expression of exchange (תְּמוּרָה), for they should have repaid Israel for the kindness that Solomon bestowed upon them, for he built the city of Tadmor for them. Because of Solomon, it is not written here “Tamar,” but “Tadmor,” i.e., an important city, in deference to Solomon’s honor, for it is no honor to Solomon to say here that he built a city which rebelled against him and sinned against him. Therefore, it is written, “Tadmor,” and not “Tamar.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:5:1)


**עָרֵי מָצוֹר.** לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹמְדִים עַל הַתְּחוּם, הֻצְרְכוּ חֹזֶק כָּל כָּךְ:


**fortified cities** Because they stood on the border, they needed to be fortified.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:9:1)


**לִמְלַאכְתּוֹ.** לִמְלֶאכֶת בִּנְיָנוֹ; לָמָּה? כִּי הַגֵּרִים לִמְלַאכְתּוֹ וְלֹא אַנְשֵׁי יִשְׂרָאֵל, לְפִי **כִּי יִשְׂרָאֵל הֵמָּה** אַנְשֵׁי חַיִל וְ**אַנְשֵׁי מִלְחָמָה וְשָׂרֵי רִכְבּוֹ וּפָרָשָׁיו**:


**for his work** [i.e.] for the work of his building. Why? All the proselytes were [employed] to do his work, but not the men of Israel, because the Israelites served as soldiers and men of war and the officers of his chariots and his horsemen.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:10:1)


**חֲמִשִּׁים וּמָאתַיִם.** וּבִמְלָכִים ״חֲמִשִּׁים וַחֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת״ (מלכים א ט:כג)! אֶלָּא זֶה וָזֶה אֶחָד הוּא, שֶׁמְחַסֵּר לְשָׁם שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת וְשָׁנָה וּמָנָה אוֹתָם שׁוּב, שֶׁהֲרֵי שָׁם כָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה ״לְבַד מִשָּׂרֵי הַנִּצָּבִים וְגוֹ׳ שְׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת הָרֹדִים״ (מלכים א ה:ל) וְכָאן כְּתִיב לְעֵיל ״שְׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים וְשֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת״ (דברי הימים ב ב:א), נִמְצָא מַה שֶּׁחָסֵר בְּפָסוּק אֶחָד הוֹסִיף בְּפָסוּק אַחֵר. וּמְחַלְּקָם, בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁהַשְּׁלוֹשׁ מֵאוֹת הַיְּתֵרִים גֵּרִים הָיוּ, וְהָיוּ מְמֻנִּים עַל אוֹתָן שְׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת הָרוֹדִים עַל שִׁבְעִים אֶלֶף נֹשֵׂא סֵבֶל וּשְׁמוֹנִים אֶלֶף חֹצֵב בָּהָר. וְאֵלּוּ חֲמִשִּׁים וּמָאתַיִם הַנֶּאֱמַר כָּאן – יִשְׂרְאֵלִים הָיוּ, מְמֻנִּים עַל אוֹתָם שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת גֵּרִים:


**two hundred fifty** And in I Kings (9:23): “five hundred fifty,” but [in fact], these two are identical, for there Scripture deducts three hundred and then repeats and counts them again, for there it is written (5:30): “Besides Solomon’s chief officers, etc....three thousand and three hundred, who bore rule,” and here it is written above (2:1): “three thousand six hundred.” We find that what is missing in one verse is added in the other verse. Scripture separates them because the three hundred extra were proselytes, and they were appointed over those three thousand three hundred who ruled over the seventy thousand who bore burdens and the eighty thousand hewers in the mountains (I Kings 5:29), and these two hundred fifty were Israelites, appointed over those three hundred proselytes.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:11:1)


**הֶעֱלָה שְׁלֹמֹה מֵעִיר דָּוִד.** הִיא צִיּוֹן, שֶׁהוֹשִׁיבָהּ בְּעִיר דָּוִד כְּשֶׁלְּקָחָהּ, וְהָיְתָה שָׁם עַד כְּלוֹתוֹ לִבְנוֹת אֶת בֵּיתוֹ:


**Solomon brought up from the City of David** That is Zion, for he let her dwell in the City of David when he married her, and she was there until he completed building his palace.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 8:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:11:2)


**כִּי קֹדֶשׁ הֵמָּה.** כָּל הָעִיר וְכָל הַבָּתִּים:


**because they are holy** The entire city and all the houses.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 8:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:11:3)


**אֲשֶׁר בָּאָה אֲלֵיהֶם.** לְפִי שֶׁבָּאָה אֲלֵיהֶם אֲרוֹן ה׳. **בָּאָה** – לְשׁוֹן נְקֵבָה, דּוּגְמָא ״וַאֲרוֹן בְּרִית ה׳ נִלְקָחָה״ (שמואל א ד:יז):


**since the Ark of the Lord came to them** because the Ark of the Lord had come to them. [The word] בָּאָה is a feminine expression, similar to (I Sam. 4:17): “and the Ark of the Covenant of the Lord (sic) was taken (נִלְקְחָה).”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 8:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:12:1)


**עַל מִזְבַּח ה׳.** זֶה מִזְבַּח הַנְּחֹשֶׁת שֶׁבָּנָה שְׁלֹמֹה (דברי הימים ב ד:א; ז:ז), וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים: מִזְבַּח הַנְּחֹשֶׁת שֶׁעָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה תַּחַת מִזְבַּח הַנְּחֹשֶׁת שֶׁבָּנָה מֹשֶׁה (שמות לח:ל; לט:לט). וְתֵימַהּ בַּדָּבָר, שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּתִיב: ״וְאֶת עֹלַת כָּל עַם וְכָל דַּם עוֹלָה וְגוֹ׳ יִהְיֶה לְבַקֵּר״ (מלכים ב טז:טו), כְּלוֹמַר: מִזְבַּח הַנְּחֹשֶׁת שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם קֹדֶם לָכֵן יִהְיֶה לִי לְבַקֵּר; מִכְּלָל שֶׁהַמִּזְבֵּחַ הָיָה שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת:


**on the altar of the Lord** This was the copper altar that Solomon built. Some explain that this was the copper altar that Solomon made instead of the copper altar that Moses built, but this is astounding, because it is written (II Kings 16:15): “and the burnt offerings of the entire people of the land, etc. and the blood of every burnt offering, etc. shall be for me to visit,” i.e., the copper altar, which was there heretofore, shall be for me to visit. This proves that Moses’ copper altar was still there.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 8:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/8:16:1)


**עַד הַיּוֹם מוּסַד בֵּית.** מִיּוֹם הִוָּסְדוֹ עַד כְּלוֹתוֹ:


**until this day [from] the founding of the House of the Lord** From the day of its founding until its completion.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:1:1)


**לְנַסּוֹת אֶת שְׁלֹמֹה.** אִם אֱמֶת מַה שֶּׁשָּׁמְעָה מֵחָכְמָתוֹ, וּבָאָה לְנַסּוֹתוֹ:


**to test Solomon** whether that which she had heard about his wisdom was true, and she came to test him.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:1:2)


**בְּחִידוֹת.** כְּמוֹ ״אָחוּדָה נָּא לָכֶם חִידָה״ דְּשִׁמְשׁוֹן (שופטים יד:יב):


**with riddles** - Heb. בְחִידוֹת like (Jud. 14:12): “Let me ask you a riddle (אָחוּדָה נָא לָכֶם חִידָה),” [in the story] of Samson.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:2:1)


**וְלֹא נֶעְלַם דָּבָר.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁרָאֲתָה שֶׁהִגִּיד לָהּ הַכֹּל, לֹא נֶעְלַם דָּבָר מִמַּה שֶּׁשָׁאֲלָה **אֲשֶׁר הִגִּיד לָהּ**, אָז הוֹדְתָה עַל הַשְּׁמוּעָה כִּי אֱמֶת שָׁמְעָה. וּבְתַרְגּוּם מְגִלַּת אֶסְתֵּר (תרגום שני א:ב) שָׁם כְּתוּבִים קְצָת מִן הַחִידוֹת אֲשֶׁר שְׁאָלַתּוֹ:


**and nothing was hidden** Since she saw that he told her everything, and nothing that she asked him was hidden, she admitted that the report she had heard was true. In the *Targum* (*Sheni*) of Megillath Esther (1:2), some of the riddles that she asked him are written.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:1)


**וּמַאֲכַל שֻׁלְחָנוֹ.** בַּרְבּוּרִים וּמִינֵי מַעֲדַנִּים הָעוֹלִים עַל שֻׁלְחָנוֹ לָרֹב (מלכים א ה:ג):


**And the food of his table** fowl (I Kings 5:3) and kinds of delicacies that were served on his table in abundance.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:2)


**וּמוֹשַׁב עֲבָדָיו.** שֶׁכָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד הָיָה מַכִּיר אֶת מְקוֹמוֹ כְּשֶׁהָיוּ יוֹשְׁבִים עַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן. וְרוֹאֵי פְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ תָּדִיר – לֹא הָיָה אֶחָד רַשַּׁאי לֵישֵׁב בִּמְקוֹם חֲבֵירוֹ כָּל יְמֵי חַיָּיו:


**and the seating of his servants** That each one recognized his place when they were seated at the table, and of those who constantly saw the king’s face, no one was allowed to sit in his neighbor’s place all the days of his life.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:3)


**וּמַעֲמַד מְשָׁרְתָיו.** כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד הָיָה מַחֲזִיק בְּמַעֲמָד שֶׁלּוֹ וּבְשֵׁירוּת שֶׁלּוֹ, וְלֹא הָיָה מְשַׁנֶּה לְמָחָר:


**and the station of his attendants** Each one would retain his station and his position, and he would not change it the next day.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:4)


**וּמַלְבּוּשֵׁיהֶם.** לֹא כְּמַלְבּוּשֵׁיהֶם שֶׁהֵם לְבוּשִׁים הַיּוֹם לוֹבְשִׁים לְמָחָר:


**and their attire** The attire that they would wear tomorrow was not the same as, the attire that they wore today.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:5)


**וּמַשְׁקָיו.** רוֹב מַשְׁקָיו:


**and his cupbearers** most of his cupbearers.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:6)


**וַעֲלִיָּתוֹ.** מְסִלּוֹת עֲלִיּוֹתָיו אֲשֶׁר יַעֲלֶה עֲלֵיהֶם מִבֵּיתוֹ לְבֵית ה׳:


**and his ascent** the stairway by which he ascended from his palace to the Temple.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:4:7)


**וְלֹא הָיָה עוֹד בָּהּ רוּחַ.** שֶׁהָיְתָה סְבוּרָה שֶׁאֵין חָכְמָה בְּכָל הַמַּלְכִיּוֹת כִּי אִם בְּאַרְצָהּ וּכְמוֹ חֲכָמֶיהָ; כִּי אַרְצָהּ בַּמִּזְרָח, וּלְפִיכָךְ הֵם חֲכָמִים בְּיוֹתֵר, לְפִי שֶׁמִּסְתַּכְּלִין בַּמַּזָּלוֹת; כִּדְאִיתָא בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא דְּרֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה (ראש השנה כ:): לְדִידַן מְכַסֵּי סִיהֲרָא שְׁמוֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁעוֹת דְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמַעֲרַב בָּבֶל. וְזֶהוּ שֶׁכָּתוּב ״וַתֵּרֶב חָכְמַת שְׁלֹמֹה מִכָּל חָכְמַת בְּנֵי קֶדֶם״ (מלכים א ה:י) – שֶׁהֵם הָיוּ בַּמִּזְרָח כְּדִכְתִיב ״וַיְהִי בְּנָסְעָם מִקֶּדֶם״ (בראשית יא:ב), ״וּמִכָּל חָכְמַת מִצְרַיִם״ (מלכים א ה:י) שֶׁהָיוּ מְכַשְּׁפִים, חַרְטֻמִּים וְאַשָּׁפִים; וְחָכָם הָיָה בְּטַיִּיר. כָּךְ כְּתִיב בִּפְסִיקְתָּא. טַיִּיר הוּא לְשׁוֹן עֲרָבִי; וּבְוַיִּקְרָא רַבָּה בְּפֶרֶק בֶּן הַמִּצְרִי; ״כִּי עוֹף הַשָּׁמַיִם יוֹלִיךְ אֶת הַקּוֹל״ (קהלת י:כ) – זֶה עוֹרֵב וְחָכְמַת טוּיאַרִי:


**and she was breathless** because she thought that there was no wisdom in any of the kingdoms but in her own country and like that of her wise men, because her country was in the east; they were wise because they were stargazers, as is stated in the first chapter of Tractate *Rosh Hashanah*, for Eretz Israel is west of Babylon, and that is what is written (I Kings 5:10): “And Solomon’s wisdom was greater than the wisdom of all the children of the east,” for they were in the east, as it is written (Gen. 11: 2): “And it came to pass as they migrated from the east.” (I Kings 5:10) “And all the wisdom of Egypt,” for they were sorcerers, necromancers, and astrologers, and he was wise in divination (טַיִיר). So it is written in *Pesikta (d’Rav Kahana* p. 33b). The word (טַיִיר) is Arabic, and in *Leviticus Rabbah* (32:2), in the chapter dealing with the son of the Egyptian (24:10): (Eccl. 10:20): “for a bird of the air shall carry the voice.” This is the raven and refers to the art of bird divination (טוּיאַרִי).



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:6:1)


**חֲצִי מַרְבִּית.** רִבּוּי חָכְמוֹת:


**half of your abundant [wisdom]** The many wisdoms.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:10:1)


**עֲצֵי אַלְגּוּמִּים.** מְפֹרָשׁ בִּפְסִיקְתָּא (פסיקתא רבתי לג) כָּתוּב אֶחָד אוֹמֵר ״אַלְמֻגִּים״ (מלכים א י:יב) וְכָתוּב אֶחָד אוֹמֵר אַלְגּוּמִּים! אֶלָּא אַלְגּוּמִּים שְׁמָם, וְנִקְרָא ״אַלְמֻגִּים״ לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה מִתְמוֹגֵג וְנִכְפָּף מִתּוֹךְ רְכִיכוּתוֹ. כְּמוֹ כּוֹסוֹת שֶׁהוּא נִכְפָּף מִתּוֹךְ חֲשִׁיבוּתוֹ:


**algom wood** In *Pesikta* it is explained: One verse (I Kings 10:11) says אַלְמֻגִּים, and one verse says אַלְגּוּמִּים. The truth is that its name is אַלְגּוּמִּים, but it is called אַלְמֻגִּים because it melted and bent because of its softness, (like cups, which bend because of their value.)



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:11:1)


**מְסִלּוֹת.** מַדְרֵגוֹת; וּבִמְלָכִים (מלכים א י:יב) כְּתִיב ״מִסְעָד״, כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁעוֹשִׂין לָעוֹלִין בְּדֶרֶךְ מַעֲלוֹת וּמַדְרֵגוֹת. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים (ראה רש״י למלכים א י:יב) ״מִסְעָד״ וּמְסִלָּה אֶחָד הֵם:


**a path** מְסִלּוֹת, stairs. In I Kings (10:12) it is written מִסְעָד, [a support] after the manner that they make for those who ascend by way of elevated places and stairs. Some say that מִסְעָד and מְסִילָה are one and the same.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:11:2)


**וְכִנֹּרוֹת וּנְבָלִים לַשָּׁרִים.** יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים לִבְנֵי לֵוִי הַמְשׁוֹרְרִים בִּכְלֵי שִׁיר. וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר זַצַ״ל פֵּירֵשׁ: **לַשָּׁרִים** הַמְשׁוֹרְרִים לְטַיֵּל לִפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ; דּוּגְמָא ״וַיְהִי שִׁירוֹ חֲמִשָּׁה וָאָלֶף״ (מלכים א ד:לב). הֵם שִׁירוֹת שֶׁל חוֹל שֶׁעָשָׂה לְשׁוֹרֵר לְפָנָיו לְטַיֵּל; וְהוּא שֶׁכָּתוּב ״וַיְקוֹנֵן יִרְמְיָהוּ עַל יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ וַיֹּאמְרוּ כָּל הַשָּׁרִים וְהַשָּׁרוֹת וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב לה:כה):


**and harps and psalteries for the singers** Some say (Jonathan and Rashi to Kings) for the Levites who sing with musical instruments, and Rabbi Eliezer explained it to mean: for the singers who stroll before the king. A similar case is (I Kings 5:12): “and his songs were a thousand and five.” These were secular songs that he composed to sing before him, and that is what is meant (below 35:25): “And Jeremiah lamented over Josiah, and all the male singers and the female singers recited in their lamentations, etc.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:12:1)


**מִלְּבַד אֲשֶׁר הֵבִיאָה.** מִשּׁוּם כְּבוֹד מַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה כְּתִיב כָּאן, דְּמַשְׁמַע שֶׁהִיא נָתְנָה לוֹ יוֹתֵר מִמָּה שֶׁנָּתַן לָהּ; וּבִמְלָכִים (מלכים א י:יג) כְּתִיב ״מִלְּבַד אֲשֶׁר נָתַן לָהּ כְּיַד הַמֶּלֶךְ״ – דְּבָרִים הַמְּצוּיִין בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאֵינָם מְצוּיִין בִּמְקוֹמָהּ:


**aside from that which she brought** This is written here in deference to the honor of the kings of Judah; it means that she gave him more than he gave her, and in I Kings (10:13) it is written: “aside from that which he gave her according to King [Solomon’s] ability.” [He gave her] things that were common in the land of Israel, but were not common in her country.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:14:1)


**לְבַד מֵאַנְשֵׁי הַתָּרִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ דּוֹאֲגִים לַעֲבֹר מִמְּדִינָה לִמְּדִינָה וּמִמַּלְכוּת לְמַלְכוּת בְּלֹא תַּיָּר, וְהָיוּ נוֹתְנִים לוֹ שְׂכַר תַּיָּר:


**Aside from that which the guides** - Heb. הַתָּרִים. They were afraid to pass from province to province or from kingdom to kingdom without a guide, and they would give him the guide’s fee.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:14:2)


**וְהַסּוֹחֲרִים מְבִיאִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְבִיאִים לוֹ עַל יְדֵי עֲלִילָה, כְּדֵי לִרְאוֹת חָכְמָתוֹ:


**and the merchants would bring** They would bring him [gold] on a pretext in order to see his wisdom.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:15:1)


**מָאתַיִם צִנָּה.** צִנָּה מַקֶּפֶת אֶת הָאָדָם מִשָּׁלֹשׁ רוּחוֹתָיו, הוּא שֶׁכָּתוּב ״כַּצִּנָּה רָצוֹן תַּעְטְרֶנּוּ״ (תהלים ה:יג):


**two hundred shields** צִנָּה which encompasses the person from his three sides, as is written (Ps. 5: 13): “As a shield You encompass him with favor” (כַּצִּנָּה).


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:15:2)


**זָהָב שָׁחוּט.** זָהָב רַךְ וְנוֹחַ לְהֵרָקַע מֵרוֹב חֲשִׁיבוּתוֹ, שֶׁנִּטְוָה כְּחוּט:


**malleable gold** gold that is soft and easily flattened out. It had great value because it could be spun like a thread.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:15:3)


**שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת זָהָב.** שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת זְהוּבִים, וְהוּא שֵׁשׁ מָנִים, שֶׁהַזָּהָב הוּא הַדִּינָר:


**six hundred gold pieces** Six hundred zehubim, the equivalent of six manehs, for the zahab is the dinar.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:16:1)


**יַעַר הַלְּבָנוֹן.** בַּיִת גָּדוֹל בָּנָה שְׁלֹמֹה בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, כִּדְמוּכַח לְקַמָּן; וְכָל אוֹתָם מָגִינִים לָקַח שִׁישַׁק (מלכים א יד:כה-כו). וּמֵרִבּוּי עֲצֵי הַלְּבָנוֹן הַגְּדוֹלִים עֲשָׂאָן, כְּדִכְתִיב בִּמְלָכִים ״וַיִּבֶן אֶת בֵּית יַעַר הַלְּבָנוֹן עַל אַרְבָּעָה טוּרֵי אֲרָזִים״ (מלכים א ז:ב), וְשָׁם נָתַן אוֹתָם מָגִינִים; וְהוּא שֶׁכָּתוּב ״וַתַּבֵּט בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא אֶל נֶשֶׁק בֵּית הַיָּעַר״ (ישעיהו כב:ח):


**the forest of Lebanon** Solomon built a huge house in Jerusalem, as is proven further, and Shishak took all those shields (below 12:9), and he made them (sic) from an abundance of the large trees of Lebanon, as it is written in I Kings (7:2): “And he built the house of the forest of Lebanon, etc. on four rows, etc.,” and there he deposited those shields, and that is what is written (Isa. 22:8): “and you looked on that day to the weapons of the house of the forest.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:17:1)


**כִּסֵּא שֵׁן.** שֵׁן שֶׁל פִּיל:


**ivory throne** the ivory of elephants.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:18:1)


**וְשֵׁשׁ מַעֲלוֹת לַכִּסֵּא.** מַדְרֵגוֹת לַעֲלוֹת בּוֹ:


**And there were six steps to the throne** steps by which to ascend.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:18:2)


**וְיָדוֹת מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה עַל מְקוֹם הַשָּׁבֶת.** כְּמִין מַקְלוֹת מִפֵּאָה לְפֵאָה, מִיָּמִין וּמִשְּׂמֹאל, וְלִסְמוֹךְ זְרוֹעוֹתָיו עֲלֵיהֶם:


**with armrests on either side by the place of the seat** like rods from corner to corner, from the right and from the left, upon which to support his arms.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:19:1)


**וּשְׁנֵים אֲרָיוֹת עוֹמְדִים אֵצֶל הַיָּדוֹת.** שֶׁל זָהָב. וְכָל צוּרַת הַכִּסֵּא וְצוּרַת הַחַיּוֹת שֶׁבַּכִּסֵּא מְפֹרָשׁ בְּתַרְגּוּם שֶׁל מְגִלַּת אֶסְתֵּר (תרגום שני אסתר א:ב):


**and two lions were standing beside the armrests** [The lions were made] of gold, and the entire form of the throne and the forms of the beasts that were in the throne are depicted in the *Targum* of the Scroll of Esther (1:2).



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:21:1)


**שֶׁנְהַבִּים.** שֵׁן פִּיל וְפִיל עַצְמוֹ:
