**ivory** the tusks of elephants and the elephants themselves.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:21:2)


**וְקוֹפִים.** מֵעֶ״ר קַאצֶ״ן:


**and apes** In German, *meerkats*, long- tailed monkeys.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:21:3)


**וְתוּכִּיִּים.** טַוָּסִין:


**and peacocks** - Heb. וְתוּכִּיִּים.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:22:1)


**וַיִּגְדַּל הַמֶּלֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹה וגו׳.** לְקַיֵּם מַה שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ: ״וְגַם אֲשֶׁר לֹא שָׁאַלְתָּ אֶתֵּן לָךְ גַּם כָּבוֹד״ (מלכים א ג:יג):


**Now King Solomon transcended, etc.** To fulfill that which He said to him, (I Kings 3:13): “And I have also given you that which you have not asked, etc. and honor.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:23:1)


**וְכָל מַלְכֵי הָאָרֶץ וְגוֹ׳.** לְקַיֵּם מַה שֶּׁאָמַר לוֹ: ״הִנֵּה נָתַתִּי לְךָ לֵב חָכָם וְנָבוֹן אֲשֶׁר כָּמוֹךָ לֹא יִהְיֶה״ (מלכים א ג:יב):


**And all the kings of the earth, etc.** To fulfill that which He said to him, (ibid. verse 12): “Behold I have given you a wise and understanding heart; so that there was none like you before you.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:24:1)


**וְהֵם מְבִיאִים אִישׁ מִנְחָתוֹ.** עַל יְדֵי עֲלִילָה, שֶׁבָּאִים לִשְׁמֹעַ חָכְמָתוֹ; כִּי אֵין כָּבוֹד לָבוֹא לִפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ רֵיקָם, דּוּגְמַת ״וְלֹא יֵרָאֶה אֶת פְּנֵי ה׳ רֵיקָם״ (דברים טז:טז):


**And each one would bring his gift** as a pretext because they were [really] coming to hear [his wisdom], since it is disrespectful to come before a king empty-handed, similar to (Deut. 16:16): “and he shall not appear before the Lord empty-handed.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:24:2)


**נֵשֶׁק.** כְּלֵי זַיִן:


**weapons** - Heb. נֶשֶׁק.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:25:1)


**וַיַּנִּיחֵם בְּעָרֵי הָרֶכֶב וְעִם הַמֶּלֶךְ בִּירוּשָׁלִָם.** מִקְצָתָם, וְעָבַר עַל ״לֹא יַרְבֶּה לּוֹ סוּסִים״ (דברים יז:טז):


**and he placed them in the chariot cities and with the king in Jerusalem.** some of them. He transgressed [the negative commandment of] (Deut. 17:17): “Only he shall not multiply horses to himself.”



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:29:1)


**עַל דִּבְרֵי נָתָן.** ״וַיְהִי בַּלַּיְלָה הַהוּא וַיְהִי דְּבַר ה׳ אֶל נָתָן וְגוֹמֵר״ (שמואל ב ז:ד) עַד ״וַיִּקְרָא שְׁמוֹ יְדִידְיָהּ״ (שמואל ב יב:כה):


**in the words of Nathan the prophet** (II Sam. 12:25): “And He sent by the hand of Nathan the prophet, and he called his name Jedidiah for the Lord’s sake.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:29:2)


**וְעַל נְבוּאַת אֲחִיָּה הַשִּׁילוֹנִי.** שֶׁקָּרַע מִמֶּנּוּ הַמַּלְכוּת וּנְתָנָהּ לְיָרָבְעָם (מלכים א יא:כט-לט):


**and in the prophecy of Ahijah the Shilonite** (I Kings 11:31) who rent the kingdom from him and gave it to Jeroboam.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 9:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/9:30:1)


**עַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה.** מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בְּדָוִד, שֶׁבְּחֶבְרוֹן מָלַךְ שֶׁבַע שָׁנִים וּבִירוּשָׁלַיִם שְׁלֹשִׁים וְשָׁלֹשׁ (שמואל ב ה:ה):


**over all Israel forty years** unlike David, who reigned seven years in Hebron and thirty-three years in Jerusalem.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:1:1)


**כִּי שְׁכֶם בָּאוּ כָל יִשְׂרָאֵל לְהַמְלִיךְ אֹתוֹ.** בִּשְׁכֶם שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם יָרָבְעָם שֶׁבְּחֵלֶק אֶפְרַיִם, ״וְיָרָבְעָם אֶפְרָתִי״ (מלכים א יא:כו). וְכָל זֹאת הָיְתָה סִבָּה מֵאֵת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְקַיֵּם דְּבָרוֹ: ״קָרוֹעַ אֶקְרַע הַמַּמְלָכָה״ (מלכים א יא:יא):


**for all Israel had come to Shechem to make him king** In Shechem, which was Jeroboam’s place, which was in the territory of Ephraim. And Jeroboam was an Ephrathite, and all this was brought about by the Holy One, blessed be He, to fulfill His word, (I Kings 11:11): “I will surely tear the kingdom.”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:2:1)


**וַיְהִי כִּשְׁמֹעַ.** פִּתְרוֹן: וַיְהִי כִּשְׁמֹעַ מִקּוֹדֶם לָכֵן שֶׁבָּאוּ לְהַמְלִיכוֹ בִּשְׁכֶם כְּשֶׁ**הוּא** עוֹדֶנּוּ **בְּמִצְרַיִם**:


**And it was when... heard** The meaning is: And it was when he heard - prior to this, when they came to make him [Rehoboam] king in Shechem, when he [Jeroboam] was still in Egypt.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:3:1)


**וַיִּשְׁלְחוּ וַיִּקְרְאוּ לוֹ וַיָּבֹא.** וְכָל זֶה לְהוֹדִיעֵנוּ רְצוֹנוֹ שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ יָרָבְעָם עַצְמוֹ בָּא לְהַמְלִיכוֹ לִרְחַבְעָם, וְהֵסֵב הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אַחַר דְּבָרָיו עַד שֶׁמָּלַךְ יָרָבְעָם:


**And they sent and called him, and...came** All this was to let us know the will of the Holy One, blessed be He, that even Jeroboam himself came to make Rehoboam king, but the Holy One, blessed be He, reversed his words [i.e., Jeroboam’s intention,] until Jeroboam reigned.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:4:1)


**אָבִיךָ הִקְשָׁה אֶת עֻלֵּנוּ.** כִּי אִישׁ טוֹרֵחַ בִּמְלָאכָה הָיָה, וְעָלֵינוּ הִטִּיל לְפַרְנֵס פּוֹעֲלָיו וְהַמַּס; כְּדִכְתִיב בִּמְלָכִים ״וַיַּעַל הַמֶּלֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹה מַס וְגוֹמֵר״ (מלכים א ה:כז):


**Your father made our yoke hard** because he was a man who toiled at work, and he made it incumbent upon us to sustain his laborers with the tax, as it is written in I Kings (5:27): “And King Solomon raised a levy, etc.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:6:1)


**נוֹעָצִים.** כְּמוֹ יוֹעֲצִים, וְכֵן יֹאמַר מִן יוֹדְעִים – נוֹדָעִים; אַךְ יוֹעֲצִים – לְהַבָּא, נוֹעָצִים – נִתְיַעֲצִים כְּבָר:


**How do you advise** - Heb. נוֹעָצִים, [in the passive voice], like יוֹעֲצִים [in the active voice], and so he says from יוֹדְעִים, נוֹדָעִים. יוֹעֲצִים means the future, and נוֹעָצִים means that which they already counseled.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:10:1)


**קָטָנִּי עָבָה מִמָּתְנֵי אָבִי.** אֶצְבַּע קְטַנָּה שֶׁלִּי גְּדוֹלָה מִמָּתְנֵי אָבִי; כְּלוֹמַר: אֲנִי חָזָק מִמֶּנּוּ:


**My little finger is thicker than my father’s loins** My little finger is larger than my father’s loins; i.e., I am stronger than he.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:11:1)


**הֶעְמִיס.** כְּמוֹ ״וַיַּעֲמֹס אִישׁ עַל חֲמוֹרוֹ״ (בראשית מד:יג):


**burdened** - Heb. הֶעֱמִיס, loaded, like (Gen. 44:13): “and each man loaded (וַיַַַַַּעֲמס) his donkey.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:11:2)


**בָּעֲקְרַבִּים.** בְּרִימְ״ן בִּלְשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַ״ז, שֶׁעוֹקְצִין כַּעֲקְרַבִּים:


**with scorpions** *Brimmen* in German, which sting like scorpions.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:15:1)


**כִּי הָיְתָה נְסִבָּה לְמַעַן הָקִים ה׳ אֶת דְּבָרוֹ.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְפֹרָשׁ בִּמְלָכִים (מלכים א יא:לא) לְהָסִיר מִמֶּנּוּ עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים וְלִיתֵּן לְיָרָבְעָם. וְזֶהוּ ״לֹא יָסוּר שֵׁבֶט מִיהוּדָה״ (בראשית מט:י) – לֹא יֵרֵד מִמַּלְכוּתוֹ, ״עַד כִּי יָבֹא שִׁילֹה״ (בראשית מט:י) – פִּתְרוֹן: עַד כִּי יָבֹא יְהוּדָה שִׁילֹה לְהַמְלִיךְ שָׁם רְחַבְעָם, וְשָׁם ״לוֹ יִקְּהַת עַמִּים״ (בראשית מט:י) לְהַמְלִיכוֹ, וְשָׁם נֶחְלְקָה הַמַּלְכוּת וְאָבַד מַלְכוּת שֶׁל עֲשָׂרָה הַשְּׁבָטִים. וְשְׁכֶם עִיר קְטַנָּה סְמוּכָה לְשִׁילֹה, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וְהִנֵּה חַג לַה׳ בְּשִׁילוֹ מִיָּמִים יָמִימָה... בַּמְסִלָּה הָעוֹלָה מִבֵּית אֵל שְׁכֶמָה״ (שופטים כא:יט), וּכְתִיב בְּיִרְמְיָה ״מִשְּׁכֶם״ וּ״מִשִּׁלֹה״ (ירמיה מא:ה). וְלִכְבוֹד מִשְׁכַּן שִׁילֹה נֶאֱסָפִים לִשְׁכֶם, שֶׁהוּא רָאוּי לַאֲסִיפַת בְּנֵי אָדָם וּסְמוּכָה לְשִׁילֹה, וּכְשֶׁנֶּאֱסָפִים בִּשְׁכֶם, אָז ״יָסוּר שֵׁבֶט מִיהוּדָה״ (בראשית מט:י), שֶׁשָּׁם הַמַּלְכוּת נֶחְלְקָה:


**because it was brought about, etc. so that the Lord God might establish His word** as is delineated in I Kings (11:31–37), to remove from him the ten tribes and to give them to Jeroboam, and this is the meaning of (Gen. 49:10): “The scepter will not depart from Judah” [meaning that] he will not be banished from his kingdom, “until he comes to Shiloh,” meaning until Judah comes to Shiloh to crown Rehoboam there, and there “peoples will gather to him” to crown him, and there the kingdom was divided, and the reign over the ten tribes was lost. Now Shechem is a small city adjacent to Shiloh, as it is written at the end of Judges (21:19): “Behold, there is a sacrifice before the Lord in Shiloh from time to time, etc. on the east side of the highway that goes up from Beth-El to Shechem,” and it is written in Jeremiah (41:5): “from Shechem (and) from Shiloh,” and in honor of the Tabernacle of Shiloh, they would gather in Shechem, which was fit for the people to assemble in, and it adjoined Shiloh. And when they would assemble in Shechem, the scepter would depart from Judah, for there the kingdom was divided.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:16:1)


**לְאֹהָלֶיךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** מוּסָב עַל **מַה לָּנוּ חֵלֶק בְּדָוִיד**: מַה לַעֲזֹב אָהֳלֵינוּ וְלֵילֵךְ אַחֲרָיו, לֹא כֵן, אֶלָּא לְכוּ **כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל** אִישׁ **לְאֹהָלָיו**:


**Each man to your homes, O Israel** This refers back to, “What share do we have in David?” Why should we leave our homes and follow him? Not so, but all Israel, return each man to his home.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:16:2)


**עַתָּה רְאֵה בֵיתְךָ דָּוִיד.** מוּסָב עַל **וְלֹא נַחֲלָה בְּבֶן יִשַׁי**, כְּלוֹמַר: אֵין לָנוּ בּוֹ נַחֲלָה אֶלָּא בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ שֶׁבִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, שֶׁאָנוּ זוֹבְחִים בּוֹ וְלִרְאוֹת בּוֹ בְּשָׁלֹשׁ רְגָלִים:


**Now see your house, David** This refers back to, “And no heritage in Jesse’s son,” i.e., we have no heritage in him, but in the Temple in Jerusalem, where we sacrifice and appear on the three Pilgrimage Festivals.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:16:3)


**רְאֵה בֵיתְךָ דָּוִד.** אֵין אָנוּ צְרִיכִין לוֹ לֵילֵךְ שָׁם, יִהְיֶה לְךָ לְבַדְּךָ (וְכָל זֶה לֹא נִרְאֶה לִי), אֶלָּא רְאֵה בֵיתְךָ דָּוִד, אִי אִפְשָׁנוּ לֹא בְּךָ וְלֹא בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ שֶׁלְּךָ:


**see your house, David** We do not need it to go there. Let it be yours alone. (But all this does not appeal to me) but, “See your house, David we have no desire either in you or in your Temple.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:18:1)


**הֲדוֹרָם.** הוּא ״אֲדוֹנִירָם״ (מלכים א ד:ו) שֶׁבִּימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה גְּדֻלָּתוֹ גְּדוֹלָה, וּבִימֵי רְחַבְעָם נִשְׁפְּלָה גְּדֻלָּתוֹ, עַל כֵּן נִקְרָא הֲדוֹרָם:


**Hadoram** He is identical with Adoniram (I Kings 4:6), for during Solomon’s time, he enjoyed great eminence, but in Rehoboam’s time his greatness was humbled. Therefore, he was called Hadoram.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:18:2)


**וַיִּרְגְּמוּ בוֹ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶבֶן.** וּלְפִי שֶׁרְחַבְעָם לֹא יָדַע הַסִּבָּה וְהַקֶּשֶׁר שֶׁהֵסַבּוּ מִמֶּנּוּ, עַל כֵּן נֶהֱרַג כְּשֶׁשָּׁאַל הַמַּס שֶׁקָּצַב עֲלֵיהֶם שְׁלֹמֹה:


**and the Children of Israel pelted him with stones** and because Rehoboam did not know the cause of the rebellion in which they turned away from him, he was slain when he requested the levy that Solomon had levied upon them.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 10:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/10:19:1)


**וַיִּפְשְׁעוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּבֵית דָּוִיד.** לְשׁוֹן מַרְדּוּת, וְדֻגְמָא: ״בְּיָמָיו פָּשַׁע מוֹאָב וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים ב א:א), ״אָח נִפְשָׁע מִקִּרְיַת עֹז״ (משלי יח:יט), ״אָז תִּפְשַׁע לִבְנָה״ (דברי הימים ב כא:י):


**And the Israelites rebelled against the house of David** - Heb. וַיִפְשְׁעוּ, an expression of rebellion. Examples are (II Kings 1:1): “Moab rebelled (וַיִפְשַׁע) against Israel”; (Prov. 18:19): “A rebellious (נִפְשַׁע) brother”; (below 27:19): “then Libnah rebelled (תִּפְשַׁע).”



## Chapter 11


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:1:1)


**וַיַּקְהֵל אֶת בֵּית יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִן.** שֶׁהָיוּ בִּתְחוּם יַחַד, וְגַם יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הָיְתָה שֶׁל שְׁנֵיהֶם, וּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ עַל תְּחוּם שְׁנֵיהֶם בָּנוּי, וּבַגְּבוּלִים בִּיהוֹשֻׁעַ מְפֹרָשׁ הֵיאַךְ הָיָה תְּחוּמָן יַחַד. וְגַם רַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (יומא יב.): רְצוּעָה יוֹצְאָה מֵחֵלֶק בִּנְיָמִין וְנִכְנֶסֶת בְּחֶלְקוֹ שֶׁל יְהוּדָה וְעָלֶיהָ מִזְבֵּחַ בָּנוּי; לְכָךְ לֹא הָיוּ יְכוֹלִין לְהִפָּרֵד זֶה מִזֶּה, וְגַם כָּתוּב בְּסָמוּךְ:


**and he assembled the house of Judah and Benjamin** who were together on the border. Jerusalem belonged to both of them, and the Temple was built on their common border. In the section dealing with boundaries in Joshua, it explains how their border was shared. Our Rabbis also said (*Yoma* 12a, *Meg.* 26a, *Zeb.* 53b): A strip protruded from the territory of Benjamin and entered the territory of Judah, upon which the altar was built. Therefore, they were inseparable, and they are also mentioned close to one another.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:4:1)


**עִם אֲחֵיכֶם.** וַהֲלֹא אֲחֵיכֶם בְּנֵי אִישׁ אֶחָד?


**with your brothers** Are they not your brothers, the sons of one man?



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:5:1)


**וַיִּבֶן עָרִים לְמָצוֹר.** עָרִים קְטַנִּים בָּנָה וְסִבֵּב כָּל סְבִיבוֹתָם מַיִם מִשּׁוּם חֹזֶק, וְזֶה מָצוֹר, כְּדַאֲמְרִינָן ״אֲנִי קַרְתִּי וְשָׁתִיתִי מַיִם וְאַחֲרִיב בְּכַף פְּעָמַי כָּל יְאוֹרֵי מָצוֹר״ (ישעיהו לז:כה); וְעַתָּה הֻצְרַךְ לְכָךְ, כִּי פָּשְׁעוּ בוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and he built cities for fortification** - Heb. לְמָצוֹר. He built small cities and surrounded them with water for strength, and this is [called] מָצוֹר as it is written in Isaiah (37:25): “I dug and drank water, and I dried up with the soles of my feet all rivers of fortification (מַָצוֹר).” And this was now necessary because Israel rebelled against him.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:11:1)


**וַיִּתֵּן בָּהֶם נְגִידִים.** שָׂרִים וּנְצִיבִים לִשְׁמֹר הֶעָרִים וְהַכְּרַכִּים:


**leaders** chiefs and officers to guard the towns and the large cities.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:11:2)


**וְאוֹצְרוֹת מַאֲכָל וְשֶׁמֶן וָיָיִן.** לְפִי שֶׁיְּהֵא לָהֶם מַאֲכָל מְזֻמָּן אִם יַעֲלוּ בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמָצוֹר:


**and storehouses of food, oil, and wine** in order to have food prepared for them should the house of Israel march upon them to besiege them.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:14:1)


**כִּי עָזְבוּ הַלְוִיִּם אֶת מִגְרְשֵׁיהֶם.** שֶׁבְּכָל אֶרֶץ עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים:


**For the Levites had abandoned their open land** that was in the entire land of the ten tribes.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:14:2)


**כִּי הִזְנִיחָם יָרָבְעָם.** הִזְנִיחָם מִלַּעֲבֹד וּמִכַּהֵן לַה׳:


**because Jeroboam...had coerced them to abandon** He coerced them to abandon serving and ministering to the Lord.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:15:1)


**כֹּהֲנִים לַבָּמוֹת.** כְּלוֹמַר: לָמָּה הִזְנִיחַ יָרָבְעָם הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם? לְפִי שֶׁהֶעֱמִיד כֹּהֲנִים כּוֹמְרִים **לַשְּׂעִירִים**:


**priests for the high places** i.e., Why did Jeroboam coerce them to abandon [their positions]? Because he stationed pagan priests for the satyrs.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:16:1)


**וְאַחֲרֵיהֶם.** שֶׁל לְוִיִּם וְכֹהֲנִים:


**And after them** [i.e., after] the Levites and the priests.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:18:1)


**אֲבִיהַיִל בַּת אֱלִיאָב.** כְּמוֹ וַאֲבִיהַיִל. דֻּגְמָא: ״כְּכֶבֶשׂ אַלּוּף״ (ירמיה‏ יא:יט). וְזֶה שֶׁטָּעָה שְׁמוּאֵל הָרוֹאֶה כְּשֶׁהֶעֱבִיר יִשַׁי אֶת אֱלִיאָב לִפְנֵי שְׁמוּאֵל וְאָמַר (שמואל א יז:לא) ״אַךְ נֶגֶד ה׳ מְשִׁיחוֹ״, זֶה שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עָתִיד לוֹמַר ״קוּם נָא מְשָׁחֵהוּ כִּי הוּא זֶה״ (שמואל א יז:יב); וְטָעָה בּוֹ שְׁמוּאֵל, שֶׁרָאָה בִּתּוֹ שֶׁל אֱלִיאָב **אֵשֶׁת רְחַבְעָם** מַלְכָּה. וְעַל זֶה הַדָּבָר נֶעֱנַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל, כִּי שֶׁשָּׁאַל לוֹ שָׁאוּל: ״אֵיזֶה בֵּית הָרוֹאֶה״ ״וַיֹּאמֶר״ שְׁמוּאֵל ״אָנֹכִי הָרוֹאֶה״ (שמואל א ט:יח-יט), שֶׁלֹּא עָנָהוּ דֶּרֶךְ עֲנָוָה, לוֹמַר: מַה תִּרְצֶה. אָמַר לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: חַיֶּיךָ, אַתָּה עָתִיד לִרְאוֹת וְלֹא תֵדַע מַה תִּרְאֶה; כִּי טָעָה בֶּאֱלִיאָב וּבִתּוֹ הַמַּלְכָּה:


**Abihail the daughter of Eliab** Like: and Abihail, similar to (Jer. 11:19): “And I was like a lamb and a bull (כְּכֶבֶשׂ אַלּוּף).” The following was the reason that Samuel the seer erred when Jesse passed Eliab before Samuel, and he [Samuel] said, (I Sam. 16:6): “Surely before the Lord is His anointed.” This is the one about whom the Holy One, blessed be He, is destined to say, “Arise, anoint him, for this is he.” But Samuel erred about him, for he saw Eliab’s daughter as Rehoboam’s wife, the queen, and because of the following matter, Samuel was punished: When Saul asked him, (ibid. 9: 18) “Which is the house of the seer?” Samuel replied, “I am the seer,” in which case he did not answer him with humility, saying, “What do you wish.” [Therefore,] the Holy One, blessed be He, said to him, “By your life, you are destined to see, and you will not know what you see,” for he erred concerning Eliab and his daughter the queen.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:22:1)


**לְנָגִיד בְּאֶחָיו.** כְּדֵי **לְהַמְלִיכוֹ**:


**as a leader among his brothers** in order to make him king.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 11:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/11:23:1)


**וַיִּפְרֹץ.** פִּזֵּר **בָּנָיו לְכָל אַרְצוֹת יְהוּדָה**, דּוּגְמָא: ״וּבֵית אֲבִיהֶם פָּרְצוּ לָרֹב״ (דברי הימים א ד:לח):


**and he dispersed** He scattered his sons to all the lands of Judah. A similar expression is (I Chron. 4:38): “And their fathers’ houses spread (פָּרְצוּ) exceedingly.”



## Chapter 12


### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:7:1)


**נִכְנְעוּ לֹא אַשְׁחִיתֵם.** דֻּגְמָא לְמַטָּה: ״עוֹדֶנּוּ הָאָרֶץ לְפָנֵינוּ כִּי דָרַשְׁנוּ ה׳ אֱלֹהֵינוּ דָּרַשְׁנוּ וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב יד:ו); כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ הַפָּסוּק: כְּשֶׁמַּפְסִיק בֵּנְתַיִם, כּוֹפֵל עוֹד הַתֵּיבָה לְאוֹמְרָהּ; דֻּגְמָא: ״וְהָיָה כִּי תֵלֵךְ עִמָּנוּ וְהָיָה הַטּוֹב וְגוֹ׳״ (במדבר י:לב):


**They have humbled themselves; I shall not destroy them** A similar instance is found below (14: 6): “The land is still before us for we have sought, etc. we have sought.” So is the custom of the Scriptures, that when it interrupts in the middle, it repeats the word. An example is (Num. 10:32): “And it will be, if you go with us, it will be that the good, etc.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 12:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:8:1)


**כִּי יִהְיוּ לוֹ לַעֲבָדִים.** לְשִׁישַׁק וְלִשְׁאָר עוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים, כַּמְפֹרָשׁ:


**For they will be his slaves** [i.e., slaves] of Shishak and the other nations, as is explained.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 12:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:8:2)


**וְיֵדְעוּ עֲבוֹדָתִי.** כְּשֶׁיָּשׁוּבוּ, לֹא יִהְיֶה עֲלֵיהֶם שׁוּם מוֹשֵׁל מִמַּלְכֵי עוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים, אֲבָל כְּשֶׁתָּשׁוּבוּ מֵאַחֲרֵי תּוֹרָתִי, תֵּדְעוּ **עֲבוֹדַת** מַלְכֵי **הָאֲרָצוֹת** וְתַעַבְדוּ לָהֶם:


**and they will know My service** When they repent, no ruler of the kings of the nations will be over them, but when you abandon My Law, you will experience servitude to the kings of the lands, and you will serve them.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 12:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:10:1)


**וְהִפְקִיד עַל יַד שָׂרֵי הָרָצִים.** לָקַח הַמָּגִנִּים מִבֵּית יַעַר הַלְּבָנוֹן (מלכים א י:יז) וְהִפְקִידָם לְשָׂרֵי הָרָצִים, וְאַחַר כָּךְ הֱשִׁיבָם אֶל ״תָּא הָרָצִים״ (מלכים א יד:כח), חֶדֶר הָרָצִים, כְּלוֹמַר: אֶל מְקוֹמָם. עִנְיָן אַחֵר מִפִּי מוֹרִי: לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה דּוֹאֵג מִמֶּלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם וּמִמֶּלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל, הָיוּ נוֹשְׂאִין אוֹתָן לְפָנָיו:


**and he entrusted them in the hands of the chiefs of the runners** He took them from the house of the forest of Lebanon and entrusted them to the chiefs of the runners and afterwards they returned them **to the chamber of the runners** The room of the runners, i.e., to their place. Another explanation I heard from my mentor is that because he was afraid of the king of Egypt and the king of Israel, they would carry them [the shields] before him.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 12:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:12:1)


**וּבְהִכָּנְעוֹ שָׁב מִמֶּנּוּ חֲרוֹן אַף ה׳.** הָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר לְמַעְלָה מִיָּד כְּשֶׁאָמַר **וַיַּעַל שִׁישַׁק וְגוֹמֵר**, אֶלָּא שֶׁהִפְסִיק בֵּנְתַיִם לְצֹרֶךְ **וַיַּעַשׂ תַּחְתֵּיהֶם מָגִנֵּי נְחֹשֶׁת**:


**And when he humbled himself, the Lord’s wrath returned from him** He should have stated this above immediately when he said, “And Shishak... marched,” but he interrupted in between because he had to mention, “And...made instead of them shields of copper.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 12:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:13:1)


**וַיִּתְחַזֵּק הַמֶּלֶךְ רְחַבְעָם.** וּמְפָרֵשׁ לָמָּה, שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּשֶׁהוּמְלַךְ תְּחִלָּה הָיָה **בֶן אַרְבָּעִים וְאַחַת**, לְפִיכָךְ הָיָה בּוֹ כֹּחַ וְחוֹזֶק לְהִתְחַזֵּק מֵעַצְמוֹ, כִּי הָיָה בֶּן שָׁנָה כְּשֶׁמֵּת דָּוִד, שֶׁהֲרֵי שְׁלֹמֹה מָלַךְ אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה (דברי הימים ב ט:ל), וּשְׁלֹמֹה עֲדַיִן לֹא מָלַךְ כְּשֶׁנּוֹלַד רְחַבְעָם, נִמְצָא שֶׁרְחַבְעָם הָיָה **בֶן אַרְבָּעִים וְאַחַת** כְּשֶׁמָּלַךְ:


**And King Rehoboam strengthened himself in Jerusalem** And he explains why, because when he was first made king, he was forty-one years old; therefore, he had the strength and the power to strengthen himself. He was a year old when David died, for Solomon reigned forty years, and Solomon had not yet reigned when Rehoboam was born. We find that Rehoboam was forty-one years old when he reigned.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 12:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:13:2)


**הָעִיר אֲשֶׁר בָּחַר ה׳ לָשׂוּם אֶת שְׁמוֹ שָׁם מִכֹּל שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** אֶלָּא שֶׁנִּתְקַלְקְלוּ בְּמַעֲשֵׂיהֶם, כְּדִכְתִיב **וַיַּעַשׂ הָרָע**, כִּי לֹא הֵכִין לִבּוֹ לִדְרוֹשׁ אֶת ה׳, וְגָרְמוּ שֶׁנִּסְתַּלְּקָה שְׁכִינָה מֵהֶם; וּכְאִלּוּ קוֹבֵל וּמְפִיגִין עַל כָּךְ:


**the city that the Lord had chosen wherein to place His name, out of all the tribes of Israel** but they deteriorated in their deeds, as it is written: “And he did that which was evil, for he did not set his heart to seek the Lord,” and they caused the Shechinah to leave them, and it was as if they were complaining and demonstrating about that.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 12:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/12:15:1)


**בְּדִבְרֵי שְׁמַעְיָה הַנָּבִיא.** כָּל נָבִיא וְנָבִיא הָיָה כּוֹתֵב סִפְרוֹ מַה שֶּׁהָיָה מִתְנַבֵּא, וְזֶהוּ שְׁמַעְיָה שֶׁאָמַר לְעֵיל (דברי הימים ב יא:ב); וּלְקַמָּן מוֹכִיחַ: ״וְיֶתֶר דִּבְרֵי אֲבִיָּה וּדְרָכָיו וּדְבָרָיו כְּתוּבִין בְּמִדְרַשׁ הַנָּבִיא עִדּוֹ״ (דברי הימים ב יג:כג) – סִפְרוֹ הָיָה נִקְרָא מִדְרָשׁ:


**in the words of Shemaiah the prophet** Every prophet would write his book, containing that which he prophesied, and this is the Shemaiah who was mentioned above (11:2, 12:5). And the verse written further (13:22), “And the rest of the deeds of Abijah and his ways and his words are written in the midrash of the prophet Iddo,” proves this, [that each prophet wrote a book of his prophecies, and] his [Iddo’s] book was called midrash.



## Chapter 13


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:2:1)


**שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים מָלַךְ בִּירוּשָׁלִַם.** וְלֹא יוֹתֵר, כְּדְמוֹכַח בִּמְלָכִים ״וּבִשְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים לְיָרָבְעָם מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל מָלַךְ אָסָא מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה״ (מלכים א טו:ט), נִמְצָא שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה שָׁנִים שְׁלֵמִים לֹא מָלַךְ:


**Three years he reigned in Jerusalem** and no more, as is proven in I Kings (15:9): “And in the twentieth year of Jeroboam, king of Israel, Asa reigned over Judah.” We find that he did not reign even three whole years.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:2:2)


**מִיכָיָהוּ בַת אוּרִיאֵל.** וּלְמַעְלָה (דברי הימים ב יא:כ) קוֹרֵא לָהּ שֵׁם אַחֵר, ״מַעֲכָה בַּת אַבְשָׁלוֹם״, וּבִמְלָכִים כְּתִיב כְּמוֹ כֵן ״מַעֲכָה בַּת אֲבִישָׁלוֹם״ (מא טו:ב)! מִיכָיָהוּ בַת אוּרִיאֵל הָיָה שְׁמָהּ, וְכָאן קוֹרֵא אוֹתָהּ כֵּן, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא סֵפֶר הַיַּחַס שֶׁל יְהוּדָה; וּבִמְלָכִים, שֶׁהוּא סֵפֶר מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהוּדָה, קְרָאָהּ בְּשֵׁם כִּנּוּיָהּ ״מַעֲכָה בַּת אֲבִישָׁלוֹם״; וּלְפִיכָךְ כִּנּוּ שְׁמָהּ מִשֵּׁם גָּמוּר שֶׁלָּהּ מִיכָיָהוּ – ״מַעֲכָה״, וְשֵׁם אָבִיהָ אוּרִיאֵל – ״אֲבִישָׁלוֹם״. לְפִי שֶׁעַל שֵׁם כַּלָּתָהּ נִקְרֵאת, שֶׁהָיְתָה אֵשֶׁת חַיִל (משלי לא:י), גְּבַרְתָּנִית, כַּכָּתוּב ״וְגַם מַעֲכָה אֵם אָסָא הֱסִירָהּ מִגְּבִירָה״ (דברי הימים ב טו:טז); וּמִשּׁוּם כְּבוֹדָהּ שֶׁל כַּלָּתָהּ קָרְאָה לַחֲמוֹתָהּ עַל שֵׁם כַּלָּתָהּ. כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בִּירוּשַׁלְמִי (עבודה זרה ג:ז מב:ד). וְהַמְּסוֹרֶת הַזֶּה תִּקַּח בְּיָדְךָ בְּשֵׁם רַבֵּינוּ אֶלְעָזָר, וְכֵן אָמַר לִי בְּשֵׁם אָבִיו. וְכֵן מָצָאתִי בְּפֵירוּשׁ אָבִיו: בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁהוֹסִיף וָי״ו בְּשֵׁם אִשָּׁה – זוֹ מִשּׁוּם שִׁבְחָהּ, כְּמוֹ ״עֲתַלְיָה״ (מב יא:יג) – ״וַעֲתַלְיָהוּ מוֹלֶכֶת עַל הָאָרֶץ״ (דברי הימים ב כב:יב), כִּי אֵשֶׁת חַיִל הָיְתָה. וְכֵן ״יְכָלְיָה״ (דברי הימים ב כו:ג) ״יְכָלְיָהוּ״ (מלכים ב טו:ב) לְפִי שֶׁהָיְתָה גְּבַרְתָּנִית, כִּי אֲמַצְיָהוּ בַּעְלָהּ נָס לָכִישָׁה (דברי הימים ב כה:כז) וַיְהִי שָׁם חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה (דברי הימים ב כה:כה) עַד שֶׁמֵּת, וְהִיא שָׁפְטָה אֶת הָעָם כָּל אוֹתָן חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה הַשָּׁנִים; וְשׁוּב כְּשֶׁמֵּת, הִמְלִיכוּ אֶת בְּנוֹ עֻזִּיָּהוּ אַחֲרָיו ״בֶּן שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה״ (דברי הימים ב כו:א):


**Micaiah the daughter of Uriel** And above (11:20), he calls her by a different name, Maacah the daughter of Absalom, and in I Kings (15:10), it is likewise written “Maacah the daughter of Absalom.” Micaiah the daughter of Uriel was her [real] name, and here he calls her thus because this is the genealogical record of Judah. In Kings, which is the book of the kings of Israel and Judah, he calls her by her nickname, Maacah the daughter of Absalom. Now, for this reason, they nicknamed her Maacah, rather than call her by her real name, Micaiah, (and the name of her father was Uriel Abishalom). She was called by the name of her daughter-in-law, who was a valiant woman, a heroic woman, as it is written (below 15:16): “And also Maacah the mother of King Asa he removed from being queen,” and in honor of her daughter-in-law, they called the mother-in-law by the daughter-in-law’s name. So it is explained in *Yerushalmi* (unknown). Now this tradition is in the name of Rabbi Eliezer, and he told me this in the name of his father, and so I found in his father’s commentary: Wherever a “vav” is added to the name of a woman, this is to her praise, like Athaliah (below 22: 12): “and Athaliah reigned over the land,” for she was a valiant woman; similarly Jecaliah (ibid. 26:3) and Jecaliahu (II Kings 15:2), because she was a heroic woman, for Amaziah her husband fled to Lachish and was there fifteen years until he died, and she judged the people all those fifteen years, and later, when he died, they crowned his son Uzziah after him at the age of sixteen.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:4:1)


**וַיָּקָם אֲבִיָּה.** שֶׁהָלַךְ אֲבִיָּה לְמִלְחָמָה עַל יָרָבְעָם בְּמָחוֹז שֶׁל יָרָבְעָם **בְּהַר אֶפְרָיִם**:


**And Abijah arose** For Abijah went to war against Jeroboam in Jeroboam’s territory on Mount Ephraim.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:5:1)


**בְּרִית מֶלַח.** בְּקִיּוּם וֶאֱמוּנָה, וְעַתָּה:


**a covenant of salt** with endurance and permanence. And now -



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:6:1)


**וַיָּקָם יָרָבְעָם עֶבֶד שְׁלֹמֹה וְגוֹ׳.** וְעַל זֹאת יֵשׁ בִּזָּיוֹן גָּדוֹל:


**Jeroboam, etc. the servant of Solomon the son of David, arose, etc.** and a great stigma is attached to this.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:7:1)


**וַיִּקָּבְצוּ עָלָיו אֲנָשִׁים רֵקִים.** רְשָׁעִים:


**And there gathered to him worthless** wicked.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:7:2)


**וַיִּתְאַמְּצוּ עַל רְחַבְעָם בֶּן שְׁלֹמֹה וּרְחַבְעָם הָיָה נַעַר וְרַךְ לֵבָב.** וּלְפִי שֶׁמָּצְאוּ בּוֹ שִׁפְלוּת וּכִיכוּת לֵבָב הִתְאַמְּצוּ עָלָיו:


**and they overwhelmed Rehoboam the son of Solomon and Rehoboam was young and soft-hearted** and since they found in him humility and soft- heartedness, they overwhelmed him.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:7:3)


**נַעַר.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָיָה בֶּן אַרְבָּעִים וְאַחַת שָׁנָה כְּשֶׁמָּלַךְ, קוֹרֵהוּ נַעַר; וּפֵירֵשׁ (דברי הימים א כב:ה) עַל שְׁלֹמֹה:


**young** - Heb. נַעַר. Although he was forty-one years old when he reigned, he is called a youth (נַעַר), as I explained above (I Chron. 22:5) in regards to Solomon.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:9:1)


**כְּעַמֵּי הָאֲרָצוֹת כָּל.אַרְבָּעִים וְאַחַת הָרוֹצֶה לָבוֹא וּלְמַלֵּא יָדוֹ וְלִהְיוֹת כֹּהֵן לְעֵגֶל זָהָב, יָבִיא עִמּוֹ <b>פַּר בֶּן בָּקָר וְאֵילִים שִׁבְעָה** וִיקַבְּלוּהוּ:


**like the peoples of the lands** Whoever wishes to come and be initiated to be a priest to a golden calf, shall bring a young bull and seven rams, and be accepted.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:9:2)


<b>וְהָיָה כֹהֵן לְלֹא אֱלֹהִים. לְמַלֵּא יָדוֹאַרְבָּעִים וְאַחַת לְהַבְדִּיל בֵּין טֻמְאָה לְטָהֳרָה; דֻּגְמָא: ״לְמָשְׁחָה בָהֶם וּלְמַלֵּא בָם אֶת יָדָם״ (שמות כט:כט):


**to initiate himself** - Heb. לְמַלֵּא יָדוֹ. To differentiate between impure and pure, similar to (Exod. 29:29): “to be anointed and initiated (וּלְמַלֵּא בָם אֶת יָדָם) through them.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:10:1)


**וְהַלְוִיִּם בַּמְלָאכֶת.** כְּלוֹמַר: בִּמְלַאכְתָּם, זֶה לְשִׁיר וְזֶה לְשׁוֹעֵר, כָּל אִישׁ לִמְלַאכְתּוֹ כַּאֲשֶׁר הֵכִין דָּוִד (דברי הימים א כד-כח):


**engage in the work** i.e., in their work; this one to sing, and that one to guard the gates, every man to his work, as David prepared it.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:20:1)


**וְלֹא עָצַר כֹּחַ וְגוֹ׳ וַיִּגְּפֵהוּ ה׳.** לְיָרָבְעָם:


**And Jeroboam could muster no more strength, etc., and the Lord smote him** i.e., Jeroboam.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 13:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/13:20:2)


**וַיָּמֹת.** לֹא שֶׁמֵּת מִיָּד, אֶלָּא הוֹלֵךְ וְחָסֵר בַּעֲבוּר הַמִּלְחָמוֹת; וּבִבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה עג:ה) מְפֹרָשׁ: **וַיִּגְּפֵהוּ ה׳** לַאֲבִיָּה:


**and he died** He did not die immediately, but continuously deteriorated because of the wars, and in *Genesis Rabbah* (65:20), it is explained that the Lord smote Abijah.



## Chapter 14


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 14:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:3:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר לִיהוּדָה לִדְרוֹשׁ.** וְלֹא כַּעֲשָׂרָה שְׁבָטִים שֶׁדָּרְשׁוּ לֵאלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים, חֲדָשִׁים מִקָּרוֹב בָּאוּ (דברים לב:יז), וְאֶת אֱלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתָם לֹא דָרְשׁוּ:


**And he commanded Judah to seek** unlike the Ten Tribes, who sought other, new Gods, which came up of late, and did not seek the God of their fathers.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 14:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:6:1)


**עוֹדֶנּוּ הָאָרֶץ לְפָנֵינוּ.** בְּעוֹד שֶׁהַמַּלְכוּת נָכוֹנָה לְפָנֵינוּ, שֶׁאִם נִצְרְכוּ לָהֶם יִהְיוּ לָנוּ לְמָנוֹס. וְכָל אֵלֶּה הָיָה בְּ״עֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים״ שֶׁכָּתוּב ״בְּיָמָיו שָׁקְטָה הָאָרֶץ״ (דברי הימים ב יג:כג), וּלְאַחַר אוֹתָן עֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים הָיְתָה מִלְחָמָה בֵּינוֹ וּבֵין בַּעְשָׁא כָּל יְמֵיהֶם, כִּי בִּשְׁנַת שָׁלֹשׁ לְאָסָא מָלַךְ בַּעְשָׁא, וְשֶׁבַע שָׁנִים הָיוּ שָׁלוֹם בֵּינֵיהֶם. וְכֵן מוּכָח אַף כָּאן, אֵין עִמּוֹ מִלְחָמָה בַּשָּׁנִים הָאֵלֶּה, אֲבָל בְּשָׁנִים אֲחֵרִים הָיְתָה עִמּוֹ מִלְחָמָה. וּלְמַטָּה כְּתִיב ״וּמִלְחָמָה לֹא הָיָתָה עַד שְׁנַת שְׁלֹשִׁים וְחָמֵשׁ לְמַלְכוּת אָסָא״ (דברי הימים ב טו:יט). אֲבָל יְכוֹלִים אָנוּ לְפָרֵשׁ: אוֹתָהּ מִלְחָמָה שֶׁהָיְתָה לוֹ עִם הַכּוּשִׁים לֹא הָיְתָה עַד שְׁנַת שְׁלֹשִׁים וְחָמֵשׁ לְמַלְכוּתוֹ, אֲבָל זֶה קָשֶׁה לִי, שֶׁכָּתוּב לְמַטָּה ״בִּשְׁנַת שְׁלֹשִׁים וָשֵׁשׁ לְמַלְכוּת אָסָא עָלָה בַּעְשָׁא וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב טז:א), עַד ״נִסְכַּלְתָּ עַל זֹאת כִּי מֵעַתָּה יֶשׁ עִמְּךָ מִלְחָמוֹת״ (דברי הימים ב טז:ט) – מַשְׁמָע שֶׁקֹּדֶם לָכֵן לֹא הָיוּ לוֹ מִלְחָמוֹת, וְהוּא לְאַחַר כֵּן כִּי אִם שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים. וּבִמְלָכִים כְּתִיב ״וּמִלְחָמָה הָיְתָה בֵּין אָסָא וּבֵין בַּעְשָׁא כָּל יְמֵיהֶם״ (מלכים א טו:לב), וּבְסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה טז) מְפָרֵשׁ הֵיטֵב:


**as long as the land is before us** As long as the kingdom is established before us, so that if we need them, they will be a refuge for us. And all this happened in the ten years about which it is written (above 13:23): “in his time the land was tranquil.” But after those ten years, there was a war between him and Baasha all their lives, for in the third year of Asa, Baasha reigned, and seven years there was peace between them, and so it is proven here as well (verse 5): “and there was no war with him in these years.” But in the other years there was war with him, and below (15:19) it is written: “And there was no war until the thirty-fifth year of Asa’s reign.” We can, however, explain that the war that he had with the Cushites was not until the thirty-fifth year of his reign, but this is difficult to me, for it is written below (16:1): “In the thirty-sixth year of Asa’s reign, Baasha...advanced,” until (verse 9): “you have dealt foolishly concerning this, for from now on there will be wars with you.” It appears from here that prior to this, he did not have wars, and he lived after that only five years, and in I Kings 15:32, it is written: “And there was war between Asa and Baasha, etc. all their lives.” In *Seder Olam* (ch. 16) it is explained clearly.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 14:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:6:2)


**כִּי דָרַשְׁנוּ אֶת ה׳ אֱלֹהֵינוּ דָּרַשְׁנוּ וְגוֹ׳.** לְפִי שֶׁדְּרַשְׁנוּהוּ הִנִּיחַ לָנוּ מִסָּבִיב:


**for we sought the Lord our God; we sought [Him]** Since we sought Him, He gave us peace from round about.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 14:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:6:3)


**וַיִּבְנוּ.** עָרִים:


**So they built** cities.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 14:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:6:4)


**וַיַּצְלִיחוּ.** וְדֻגְמָא: ״וּבִרְאוֹת ה׳ כִּי נִכְנַע וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב יב:ז):


**and prospered** And a similar instance is (above 12:7): “And when the Lord saw that they had humbled themselves, etc.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 14:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:10:1)


**אֵין עִמְּךָ לַעְזוֹר.** כְּלוֹמַר: אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאָנוּ מְעַטִּים וְהֵם רַבִּים כְּחוֹל הַיָּם, וְאֵין כֹּחַ לָנוּ בִּפְנֵיהֶם, עִמְּךָ לַעְזוֹר **בֵּין רַב לְאֵין כֹּחַ**; כְּלוֹמַר: בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לְךָ כֹּחַ לַעְזוֹר, לְפִיכָךְ **עָזְרֵנוּ ה׳ אֱלֹהֵינוּ כִּי עָלֶיךָ נִשְׁעַנּוּ**:


**it makes no difference to You to aid** i.e., even though we are few and they are as numerous as the sand by the sea, and we have no strength before them, You can help both the great and the powerless; it is known that You have strength to aid; therefore, help us, O Lord our God, for we have relied on You.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 14:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/14:10:2)


**אַל יַעְצֹר עִמְּךָ אֱנוֹשׁ.** אַל יִהְיֶה לְאוֹיְבֵינוּ, לְאוֹתוֹ אֱנוֹשׁ – כִּי אֱנוֹשׁ מוֹשֵׁל – עוֹצֵר יְדֵי עַמְּךָ נֶגְדְּךָ לְנַצְּחֵנוּ, שֶׁלֹּא יֹאמַר: נִצַּחְנוּ אֱלֹהֵיהֶם. דָּבָר אַחֵר מִפִּי הָרַב: אַל יְהִי לָנוּ אֱנוֹשׁ, שֶׁאָנוּ אֲנוּשִׁים, עוֹצֵר יָד מִלְּנַצְּחָם; דּוּגְמָא ״וְעָצַר אֶת הַשָּׁמַיִם״ (דברים יא:יז); ״כִּי עָצֹר עָצַר ה׳״ (בראשית כ:יח); כְּמוֹ ״אִם תַּעְצְרֵנִי״ (שופטים יג:טז):


**let no man rule against You** Let not our enemies - man - have human strength to rule (עִמְּךָ) with You, i.e., against You, to be victorious, so that they should not say, “We have defeated their God.” Another explanation from the Rabbi: Let not the weak one (אֱנוֹשׁ) be held back (יַעְצר), for we are weak, deprived of strength, keeping us back (עוֹצְרֵי יָד) from defeating them. And an example is (Deut. 11:17): “and He will hold back (וְעָצַר) the heaven”; (Gen. 20:18): “For the Lord had held back (וְעָצר עָצַר),” and like (Jud. 13:16): “If you take me in (תַּעְצְרֵנִי).”



## Chapter 15


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 15:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:3:1)


**וְיָמִים רַבִּים לְיִשְׂרָאֵל לְלֹא אֱלֹהֵי אֱמֶת.** עַד שֶׁבָּאתָ אַתָּה:


**Now there were many days for Israel without a true God** until you came.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 15:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:3:2)


**וּלְלֹא כֹהֵן מוֹרֶה.** שֶׁאֵין מִי שֶׁשּׁוֹאֵל לָהֶם שׁוּם דְּבַר תּוֹרָה, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי לֹא יוֹרוּ אֶתְכֶם הַכֹּהֲנִים:


**and without an instructing priest** Since no one asks them words of Torah, the priests will not instruct you.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 15:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:4:1)


**וַיָּשָׁב בַּצַּר לוֹ עַל ה׳ אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** כְּלוֹמַר: עַתָּה כְּשֶׁבָּאת צָרָה לָהֶם, בִּקְּשׁוּ עַל ה׳ אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל **וַיְבַקְשֻׁהוּ**, לְפִיכָךְ **וַיִּמָּצֵא לָהֶם** הַמָּצוּי, וְהוֹשִׁיעֲכֶם בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר הֲלַכְתֶּם:


**And they returned to the Lord, the God of Israel, when they were in distress** He means to say that now, when they were beset with troubles, they beseeched the Lord, the God of Israel, and they sought Him; therefore, He was found by them, and He saved you (sic) wherever you went.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 15:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:5:1)


**וּבָעִתִּים הָהֵם אֵין שָׁלוֹם.** בְּאוֹתָן הַ**יָּמִים רַבִּים לְיִשְׂרָאֵל לְלֹא אֱלֹהֵי אֱמֶת, אֵין שָׁלוֹם לַיּוֹצֵא וְלַבָּא וְגוֹ׳**, בִּרְחַבְעָם כְּתִיב עָלָיו ״עָלָה שִׁישַׁק מֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם כִּי מָעֲלוּ בַּה׳״ (דברי הימים ב יב:ב) וְכָל הַפָּרָשָׁה, וְאַף לַאֲבִיָּה הָיוּ מְהוּמוֹת רַבּוֹת (דברי הימים ב יג:ב):


**And in those times** in those numerous days for Israel, [when they were] without a true God **there was no peace for anyone going or coming, etc.** Concerning Rehoboam it is written (above 12: 2): “Shishak, the king of Egypt, marched against Jerusalem, for they had betrayed the Lord.” Abijah also experienced many turmoils.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 15:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:6:1)


**וְכֻתְּתוּ גוֹי בְּגוֹי.** דֻּגְמָא: ״וַיַּכְּתוּם״ (במדבר יד:מה):


**And nation was crushed by nation** - Heb. וְכֻתְּתוּ. Another instance of this is (Num. 14:45): “and they struck them and crushed them (וַיַּכְּתוּם).”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 15:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:7:1)


**וְאַתֶּם חִזְקוּ.** לִדְרוֹשׁ בֵּאלֹהִים:


**But you be strong** to seek God.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 15:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:11:1)


**וַיִּזְבְּחוּ לַה׳ בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא מִן הַשָּׁלָל הֵבִיאוּ.** פִּתְרוֹן: וּמִן הַשָּׁלָל שֶׁהֵבִיאוּ זָבְחוּ **בָּקָר שְׁבַע מֵאוֹת וְצֹאן שִׁבְעַת אֲלָפִים** שֶׁשָּׁבוּ, וּמִמֶּנּוּ הֵבִיאוּ; כְּדִלְמַעְלָה ״וְגַם אָהֳלֵי מִקְנֶה הֵבִיאוּ וַיִּשְׁבּוּ צֹאן לָרֹב״ וְגוֹמֵר (דברי הימים ב יד:יד). דָּבָר אַחֵר: נִרְאֶה לִי: **וַיִּזְבְּחוּ לַה׳ בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא** שֶׁנִּצְּחוּ וּבָאוּ בְשָׁלוֹם בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם; **מִן הַשָּׁלָל הֵבִיאוּ**, כְּלוֹמַר: מִן הַשָּׁלָל אֲשֶׁר הֵבִיאוּ, **הֵבִיאוּ בָּקָר שְׁבַע מֵאוֹת וְצֹאן שִׁבְעַת אֲלָפִים**:


**And they sacrificed to the Lord on that day of the plunder they brought** The meaning is: And the plunder that they brought, they sacrificed seven hundred cattle and seven thousand sheep which they had captured, and from that they brought, as above: (14:14): “And they also smote the tents of the livestock, and they captured many sheep. etc.” Another possible explanation is: “And they sacrificed to the Lord on that day” - when they were victorious and came in peace to Jerusalem. “from the plunder they brought” - from the plunder that they had brought, i.e., seven hundred cattle and seven thousand sheep.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 15:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:16:1)


**וְגַם מַעֲכָה אֵם אָסָא הַמֶּלֶךְ הֱסִירָהּ מִגְּבִירָה.** לְפִי שֶׁ**עָשְׂתָה לַאֲשֵׁרָה מִפְלָצֶת** (עבודה זרה מד.): מַפְלִיא לֵיצָנוּתָא, זִכְרוּת עָשְׂתָה לוֹ לַצֶּלֶם וְהָיְתָה נִבְעֶלֶת לוֹ:


**And also Maacah, King Asa’s mother he deposed from [being] the queen** because she had made a frightful image for an asherah to gratify her lust. [מִפְלֶצֶת means] מַפְלֵא לֵצוּתָא. It carried lasciviousness to an extreme. She made herself a phallus for the image, and would copulate with it.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 15:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:17:1)


**וְהַבָּמוֹת לֹא סָרוּ.** בָּמוֹת יָחִיד שֶׁהוּרְגְּלוּ לְהַקְרִיב עֲלֵיהֶם לַשָּׁמַיִם מִשֶּׁחָרְבָה שִׁילֹה עַד שֶׁנִּבְנָה הַבַּיִת, וְהָיוּ עוֹנְשִׁים עֲלֵיהֶם כָּרֵת:


**The high places, however, were not removed** private high places, upon which they had become accustomed to sacrificing to Heaven from the time that Shiloh was destroyed until the Temple was built. [Those who worshipped there] were punishable by כָּרֵת, premature death.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 15:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/15:19:1)


**וּמִלְחָמָה לֹא הָיְתָה וְגוֹ׳.** תַּנְיָא בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה טז): ״בִּשְׁנַת שְׁלֹשִׁים וָשֵׁשׁ לְמַלְכוּת אָסָא עָלָה בַּעְשָׁא״ (דברי הימים ב טז:א) – אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר כֵּן, וַהֲלֹא מֵת כְּבָר בַּעְשָׁא בִּשְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים וָשֵׁשׁ לְמַלְכוּת אָסָא, כְּדִכְתִיב ״בִּשְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים וָשֵׁשׁ לְאָסָא מָלַךְ אֵלָה בֶּן בַּעְשָׁא״ (מלכים א טז:ח)! אֶלָּא מַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר: ״בִּשְׁנַת שְׁלֹשִׁים וָשֵׁשׁ לְמֶלֶךְ אָסָא״? כְּנֶגֶד שְׁלֹשִׁים וָשֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים שֶׁנִּתְחַתֵּן שְׁלֹמֹה לְבַת פַּרְעֹה, שֶׁהֲרֵי כָּתַב כֵּן בִּמְלָכִים ״וַיִּבְרְחוּ שְׁנֵי עֲבָדִים לְשִׁמְעִי״ (מלכים א ב:לט) עַד ״וַיְצַו הַמֶּלֶךְ אֶת בְּנָיָהוּ וַיֵּלֶךְ וַיִּפְגַּע בּוֹ״, וּכְתִיב בְּסָמוּךְ ״וַיִּתְחַתֵּן שְׁלֹמֹה בְּבַת פַּרְעֹה״ (מלכים א ג:א) – כְּלוֹמַר: מִיָּד אַחַר אוֹתָן שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים, וּבִשְׁנַת אַרְבַּע לְקָחָהּ, וְהוּא מָלַךְ אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנִים, נִמְצָא שֶׁהָיְתָה עִמּוֹ שְׁלֹשִׁים וָשֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים. וּכְנֶגְדָּן שְׁלֹשִׁים וָשֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים נִגְזְרָה גְּזֵרָה עַל מַלְכֵי אֲרָם לִהְיוֹת שׂוֹטְנִים עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל, וּבָאַחֲרוֹנָה לִיפֹּל בְּיַד בְּנֵי דָוִד, לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר ״בִּשְׁנַת שְׁלֹשִׁים וָשֵׁשׁ לְמֶלֶךְ אָסָא״; וּבִשְׁנַת שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה לְמַלְכוּתוֹ, מֵעֵת שֶׁנָּפַל זֶרַח הַכּוּשִׁי בְּיָדוֹ, הִיא שְׁנַת שְׁלֹשִׁים וָשֵׁשׁ מִשֶּׁמֵּת שְׁלֹמֹה, וּבִשְׁנַת שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה לְאָסָא כָּרְתוּ בְּרִית מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמֶלֶךְ אֲרָם לַעֲלוֹת וּלְהִתְגָּרוֹת בְּאָסָא. וְקִלְקֵל אָסָא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר **וַיּוֹצֵא אָסָא כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב לֵאמֹר** כְּרוֹת **בְּרִית בֵּינִי וּבֵינֶךָ וַיִּשְׁמַע בֶּן הֲדַד.** וְלֹא פָּסְקוּ מַלְכֵי אֲרָם מִלִּהְיוֹת שׂוֹטְנִים לְיִשְׂרָאֵל עַד שֶׁמֵּת אָחָז, וּבִשְׁנַת שָׁלֹשׁ לְאָחָז כָּרְתוּ בְּרִית מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמֶלֶךְ אֲרָם לַעֲלוֹת וּלְהִתְגָּרוֹת בְּאָחָז, וְלֹא הָיָה זְכוּת בְּאָחָז שֶׁיִּפְּלוּ בְיָדוֹ, וְנָפְלוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם בְּיַד תִּגְלַת פִּלְאֶסֶר מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר:


**And there was no war, etc.** We learned in Seder Olam (ch. 16): “In the thirty-sixth year of Asa’s kingdom, Baasha, the king of Israel, advanced.” Is it possible to say so? Had not Baasha already died in the twenty-sixth year of Asa’s reign, as it is written (I Kings 16:8): “In the twenty-sixth year of Asa, king of Judah, Elah the son of Baasha reigned”? What then is the meaning of “In the thirty-sixth year of Asa’s kingdom”? This corresponds to the thirty-six years from the time that Solomon married Pharaoh’s daughter, for so it is written (I Kings 2:39-46): “and two of Shimei’s slaves ran away... And the king commanded Benaiah etc. and he smote (Sic) and fell upon him,” and immediately following this Scripture writes about Solomon’s marriage to Pharaoh’s daughter, i.e., immediately after those three years, and in the fourth year he married her, and he reigned forty years. We find that she was with him thirty-six years, and corresponding to them were the thirty-six years that the decree was issued upon the kings of Aram to be adversaries to Israel and finally to fall into the hand[s] of David’s sons. Therefore, it is stated: “In the thirty-sixth year of Asa’s kingdom.” In the sixteenth year of his reign, after Zerah the Cushite fell into his hand[s], that is the thirty-sixth year from Solomon’s death, and in the sixteenth [year] of Asa, the king of Israel and the king of Aram made a treaty to advance and provoke Asa. Asa sinned, as it is stated (16:2f.): “And Asa brought out silver and gold... saying, ‘Make (sic) a treaty between me and you...’ And Ben Hadad heeded.” And the kings of Aram did not cease being adversaries to Israel until Ahaz died, and in the third year of Ahaz, the king of Israel and the king of Aram made a treaty to advance and provoke Ahaz, and Ahaz had no merit that they should fall into his hand[s]; so they both fell into the hand [s] of Tiglath-Pileser, the king of Assyria.



## Chapter 16


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 16:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:2:1)


**בְּדַרְמֶשֶׂק.** וּבִמְלָכִים (מלכים א טו:יח) כְּתִיב ״דַּמֶּשֶׂק״, וּבְפָסוּק אַחֵר (מלכים ב טז:י) ״דּוּמֶשֶׂק״, וְכֻלָּן אֶחָד הָיָה, וְדֻגְמָא שׁוֹט שֵׁבֶט שַׁרְבִיט:


**in Damascus** And in I Kings (15:18) it is written, “Damesek,” and in another verse (II Kings 16: 10) it is written, “Dumesek.” It is all the same, an example being שׁוֹט, שֵׁבֶט, שַׁרְבִיט, whip, rod, scepter.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 16:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:7:1)


**עַל כֵּן נִמְלַט.** שֶׁלֹּא יָכֹלְתָּ לוֹ, לָמָּה? שֶׁלֹּא בָטַחְתָּ בְּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא. כִּי אִם בָטַחְתָּ בְּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, כְּשֶׁנִּשְׁבְּעוּ וְכָרְתוּ בְרִית יַחַד מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמֶלֶךְ אֲרָם עָלֶיךָ, כַּאֲשֶׁר בָּטַחְתָּ בּוֹ כְּשֶׁבָּאוּ עָלֶיךָ הַכּוּשִׁים (דברי הימים ב יד:ח-יד), לֹא נִמְלְטוּ מִיָּדֶךָ, וְהָיִיתָ נוֹצֵחַ לְכֻלָּם; אַף לֹא הָיִיתָ מְאַבֵּד מָמוֹנְךָ, שֶׁנָּתַתָּ לוֹ כְּדֵי לְהָפֵר בְּרִיתוֹ עִם בַּעְשָׁא (דברי הימים ב טז:ג); וְעַתָּה שֶׁלֹּא בָטַחְתָּ בְּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא – אָבַדְתָּ מָמוֹנְךָ וְגַם מֶלֶךְ אֲרָם נִמְלַט מִיָּדֶךָ:


**therefore...escaped** You did not prevail against them. Why not? Because you did not trust in the Holy One, blessed be He. For had you trusted in the Holy One, blessed be He, when the king of Israel and the king of Aram made a treaty against you, as you trusted in Him when the Cushites attacked you, they would not have escaped from your hand, and you would have defeated them all, and you would not have lost your money, which you gave him in order that he abrogate his treaty with Baasha. Now that you did not trust in the Holy One, blessed be He, you lost your money, and the king of Aram escaped from your hand.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 16:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:10:1)


**בֵּית הַמַּהְפֶּכֶת.** בֵּית הַסֹּהַר שֶׁעָשׂוּי מְלֶאכֶת הַמַּהְפֶּכֶת; גְּוֶועלְבְּ״ט בִּלְשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַז:


**into the prison house** - Heb. בֵּית הַמַּהְפֶּכֶת. A prison made of vaultwork, *gewelb(e)t* in Middle High German, vaulted (prison).



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 16:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:12:1)


**עַד לְמַעְלָה חָלְיוֹ.** נֶפַח שֶׁבְּרַגְלָיו עָלָה מִכַּף רַגְלוֹ עַד קָדְקֳדוֹ:


**until his ailment spread upward** The swelling in his feet spread from the sole of his foot to the top of his head.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 16:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:14:1)


**וּזְנִים.** מִינֵי בְשָׂמִים הֵם. וְהַבְּשָׂמִים הָיוּ **מְרֻקָּחִים** בְּמֶרְקָחָה **מַעֲשֵׂה** רוֹקֵחַ **מִרְקַחַת**. וְזֶה פִּתְרוֹן: **מְרֻקָּחִים** בְּמַעֲשֵׂה רוֹקֵחַ (שמות ל:כה):


**and divers kinds** These are kinds of spices, and the spices were prepared with the perfumer’s art, a mixture. The meaning is: prepared with the perfumer’s art.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 16:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/16:14:2)


**וַיִּשְׂרְפוּ לוֹ שְׂרֵפָה גְדוֹלָה.** שָׂרְפוּ לוֹ בְּשָׂמִים טוֹבִים לְאֵפֶר, וְאוֹתוֹ אֵפֶר יִזְרוּ עָלָיו. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁין (עבודה זרה יא.): כְּמוֹ שֶׁשּׂוֹרְפִין עַל הַמְּלָכִים; וּמַהוּ שׂוֹרְפִין וְכוּ׳ וְעוֹקְרִין עַל הַמְּלָכִים:


**and they kindled an exceedingly great fire** They burned good spices for him into ashes, and they scattered those ashes on him. Others explain this like (*Abodah Zarah* 11a): “We may burn for the kings.” Now what did they burn? Their biers and the utensils they used.



## Chapter 17


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:3:1)


**בְּדַרְכֵי דָוִיד אָבִיו הָרִאשֹׁנִים.** וְלֹא הָאַחֲרוֹנִים שֶׁפָּשַׁע בְּמַעֲשֵׂה בַת שֶׁבַע, וְגַם הֵסִית אוֹתוֹ שָׂטָן לִמְנוֹת אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל (דברי הימים א כא:א). וְעִיקָּר וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים: **רִאשֹׁנִים** רֹאשׁ לִשְׁלֹמֹה וְרַחְבְעָם; שְׁלֹמֹה – נָשָׁיו הִטּוּ לְבָבוֹ בְּאַחֲרִית יָמָיו וּבָנָה בָּמָה לִכְמוֹשׁ תּוֹעֲבַת מוֹאָב וּלְמוֹלֶךְ שִׁקּוּץ בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן (מלכים א יא:ז), וְרַחְבְעָם – כְּתִיב ״וַיְהִי כְּהָכִין מַלְכוּת רְחַבְעָם וּכְחֶזְקָתוֹ עָזַב אֶת תּוֹרַת ה׳״ (דברי הימים ב יב:א). וְלֹא כְּאָבִיו שֶׁכָּתוּב בּוֹ בִּמְלָכִים ״וְלֹא הָיָה לְבָבוֹ שָׁלֵם עִם ה׳ אֱלֹהָיו״ (מלכים א טו:ג), וְלֹא כְּאָסָא שֶׁלֹּא נִשְׁעַן בַּה׳, אֶלָּא כְּדָוִד אָבִיו, שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לוֹ אֶלָּא לֵב אֶחָד לְאָבִיו שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַיִם:


**in the first ways of David, his father** But not the last ways, when he sinned in the incident of Bathsheba, and when the Adversary enticed him to count Israel. This is the most accurate interpretation, but others explain “first” as meaning superior to Solomon and Rehoboam. Regarding Solomon, it is written (I Kings 11:4): “his wives turned away his heart” at the end of his days, and he built a high place for Chemosh, the abomination of Moab, and for Molech, the abomination of the children of Ammon.” Regarding Rehoboam, it is written (above 12:1): “And when he became strong, he abandoned the Law of the Lord.” [Jehoshaphat was also not] like Abijam, about whom it is written (I Kings 15:2): “and his heart was not complete with the Lord, his God,” or like Asa, who did not rely on the Lord; [Jehoshaphat] resembled his father, David, who had only one heart for his Father in heaven.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:3:2)


**וְלֹא דָרַשׁ לַבְּעָלִים.** כְּמַעֲשֵׂה יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁבְּיָמָיו, כִּי אַחְאָב הָיָה בְּיָמָיו, שֶׁעָבַד אֶת הַבְּעָלִים הוּא וְיִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁבְּיָמָיו:


**and did not inquire of the baalim** like the practice of Israel in his time, for Ahab, who was in his time, worshipped the baalim, as did Israel in his time.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:6:1)


**וַיִּגְבַּהּ לִבּוֹ בְּדַרְכֵי ה׳.** גָּבַהּ לִבּוֹ בְּדָבָר זֶה שֶׁלֹּא הָלַךְ כִּי אִם בְּדַרְכֵי ה׳; דּוּגְמָא: ״אֲשֶׁר נָשָׂא לִבָּן אֹתָנָה בְּחָכְמָה״ (שמות לה:כו):


**And his heart was uplifted in the ways of the Lord** His heart was lifted up in this matter, that he went only in the ways of the Lord. An example of this is (Exod. 35:26): “whose hearts had uplifted them in wisdom.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:7:1)


**שָׁלַח לְשָׂרָיו לְבֶן חַיִל.** כָּךְ הָיָה שְׁמוֹ. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים: **שָׁלַח לְשָׂרָיו**, כָּל שַׂר **בֶּן חַיִל**; וְקַשְׁיָא לִי: אִם כֵּן לֹא הָיָה לוֹמַר אֶלָּא לְעֹבַדְיָה, אֶלָּא מִמַּה שֶׁאָמַר **וּלְעֹבַדְיָה**, מִכְּלָל **לְבֶן חַיִל** שֵׁם אָדָם הוּא:


**Ben Hail** That was his name. Others explain: he sent to his officers, every officer a valiant man. But I have difficulty with this interpretation because, if so, it should have only said, “Obadiah.” The fact that it says, “to Obadiah” proves that Ben Hail was a person’s name.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:7:2)


**לְלַמֵּד בְּעָרֵי יְהוּדָה.** תּוֹרָה וּלְהוֹרוֹת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**to teach in the cities of Judah** [i.e., to teach] the Torah and to instruct the Children of Israel.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:8:1)


**וְעִמָּהֶם.** מִן הַלְוִיִּם **שְׁמַעְיָהוּ וּנְתַנְיָהוּ וְגוֹמֵר**:


**And with them** of the Levites were Shemaiah, Nethaniah, etc.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:8:2)


**וְעִמָּהֶם אֱלִישָׁמָע וִיהוֹרָם הַכֹּהֲנִים.** כִּי עַל הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם לְלַמֵּד וּלְהוֹרוֹת, כְּדִכְתִיב ״כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר יוֹרוּ אֶתְכֶם הַכֹּהֲנִים״ וְ״הַלְוִיִּם״ (דברים כד:ח). וְהַשָּׂרִים הָלְכוּ עִמָּהֶם, לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא יַמְרוּ אֶת פִּיהֶם וּלְהַכְרִיחָם לִשְׁמֹעַ לָהֶם, וְלִשְׁמֹר לַעֲשׂוֹת כְּמִצְוַת הַשּׁוֹפְטִים; דּוּגְמַת ״שׁוֹפְטִים וְשׁוֹטְרִים תִּתֶּן לְךָ״ (דברים טז:יח) – שׁוֹפְטִים לִשְׁפֹּט אֶת הָעָם, וְשׁוֹטְרִים לְהַכְרִיחָם וְלַעֲשׂוֹת צִוּוּי הַשּׁוֹפְטִים:


**and with them were Elishama and Jehoram, the priests** because it is incumbent upon the priests and the Levites to teach and to instruct, as it is written (Deut. 24:8): “in accordance with everything the priests, the Levites teach you.” The officers went with them because they [the people] would not disobey their orders, and [the officers went] to coerce them [the people] to heed them [the priests] and to observe [and] to do in accordance with the order of the judges. An example (ibid. 15:18): “Judges and law-enforcing officials shall you appoint for yourself”: Judges to judge the people, and law-enforcing officials to coerce them to comply with the orders of the judges.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:10:1)


**וַיְהִי פַּחַד ה׳ עַל כָּל מַמְלְכוֹת הָאֲרָצוֹת.** בִּזְכוּת שֶׁלָּמְדוּ תּוֹרָה:


**And the fear of the Lord was upon all the kingdoms of the lands** in the merit of having studied Torah.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:11:1)


**וְכֶסֶף מַשָּׂא.** מַשָּׂאוֹת שֶׁל כֶּסֶף, דֻּגְמָא: ״הָיוּ נְשׂוּאֹתֵיכֶם עֲמוּסוֹת מַשָּׂא״ (ישעיה מו:א):


**and loads of silver** - Heb. וְכֶסֶף מַשָּׂא, loads of silver. An example (Isa. 46:1): “נְשֻׂאתֵיכֶם עֲמוּסוֹת מַשָּׂא: the things that you carried are made a load.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:12:1)


**וַיְהִי יְהוֹשָׁפָט הוֹלֵךְ וְגָדֵל.** שְׁלֹשָׁה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר כְּלָשׁוֹן זֶה: יִצְחָק (בראשית כו:יג), שְׁמוּאֵל (שמואל א ב:כו), יְהוֹשָׁפָט. אֲבָל מֹשֶׁה (שמות יא:ג), דָּוִד (דברי הימים א יא:ט), מָרְדֳּכַי (אסתר ט:ד; י:ג) נֶאֱמַר בָּהֶם ״וְגָדוֹל״. אֲבָל יִצְחָק (בראשית כו:יג), שְׁמוּאֵל (שמואל א ב:כו), יְהוֹשָׁפָט כָּתוּב בָּהֶם וְגָדֵל, שֶׁאֵין נָכוֹן לוֹמַר וְגָדוֹל; בִּשְׁמוּאֵל כְּתִיב ״וַיִּגְדַּל הַנַּעַר שְׁמוּאֵל עִם ה׳״ (שמואל א ב:כא), וְחַס וְחָלִילָה לוֹמַר גָּדוֹל עִם ה׳! וְכָאן כְּמוֹ כֵן: **גָּדֵל עַד לְמָעְלָה**, לָכֵן לֹא הָגוּן לוֹמַר גָּדוֹל, וְכֵן בְּיִצְחָק:


**was becoming greater** - Heb. וְגָדֵל. Scripture uses] a similar expression in reference to three people: Isaac, Samuel, and Jehoshaphat, but concerning Moses, David, and Mordecai, it says וְגָדוֹל. Concerning Isaac, Samuel, and Jehoshaphat, it says, וְגָדֵל because it is not proper to say וְגָדוֹל, great [in the context in which the word appears in the verse]. In regards to Samuel, it says, (I Sam. 2: 21): “And the lad Samuel grew up with the Lord.” God forbid that it should say, גָדוֹל עִם ה', he was great with the Lord. Here too, he [Jehoshaphat] became great to an unusual degree, גָדֵל עַד לְמָעְלָה. It is therefore improper to say וְגָדוֹל, great. The same applies to Isaac.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:12:2)


**בִּירָנִיּוֹת.** מִגִּזְרוֹת בִּירָה, מִגְדָּלִים גְּדוֹלִים לְחוֹזֶק:


**towers** בִּירָנִיוֹת, derived from בִּירָה, huge towers for strength.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:12:3)


**וְעָרֵי מִסְכְּנוֹת.** עָרֵי אוֹצָרוֹת לָתֵת בָּהֶם תְּבוּאָה וְיַיִן וְדָגָן:


**and store cities** storage cities, to put in them wheat, wine and grain.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:16:1)


**וְעַל יָדוֹ.** אֶצְלוֹ; דֻּגְמָא: ״חֶלְקַת יוֹאָב עַל יָדִי״ (שמואל ב יד:ל):


**And beside him** Heb. וְעַל יָדוֹ beside him; an example is (II Sam. 14:30): “Joab’s field is adjoining mine (אֶל יָדִי).”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 17:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/17:16:2)


**הַמִּתְנַדֵּב לַה׳.** דֻּגְמָא: ״הַמִּתְנַדְּבִים בָּעָם בָּרְכוּ ה׳״ (שופטים ה:ט):


**who gave generously to the Lord** - Heb. הַמִּתְנַדֵּב. An example is (Jud. 5:2) “when the people offer themselves willingly (בְּהִתְנַדֵּב עַם), bless the Lord”



## Chapter 18


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:2:1)


**וַיֵּרֶד לְקֵץ שָׁנִים.** וּבִמְלָכִים (מלכים א כב:ב) כְּתִיב ״וַיְהִי בַּשָּׁנָה הַשְּׁלִישִׁית וַיֵּרֶד יְהוֹשָׁפָט וְגוֹמֵר״, בַּשָּׁנָה הַשְּׁלִישִׁית מִשֶּׁשָּׁלַח אֶת בֶּן הֲדַד (מלכים א כ:לד):


**And he went down at the end of years** And in I Kings (22:2) it is written: “And it was in the third year, that Jehoshaphat came down, etc.” This refers to the third year since he had released Ben Hadad (ibid. 20:34).


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:2:2)


**וַיְסִיתֵהוּ לַעֲלוֹת אֶל רָמַת גִּלְעָד.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב שָׁם בְּסֵפֶר מְלָכִים: ״הַיְדַעְתֶּם כִּי לָנוּ רָמוֹת גִּלְעָד״ (מלכים א כב:ג); כִּי כְּשֶׁהִצִּיב יַעֲקֹב אֲבָנָיו שָׁם אֶת הַגַּל (בראשית לא:מו-מז), וְקָרָא לוֹ גַּלְעֵד, הֻחְזַק בּוֹ. וַאֲבוֹתֵינוּ לָקְחוּ גִּלְעָד וְהָרָמוֹת מְשׁוּכִים וּמֻחְזָקִים אַחֲרֵי הָעִיר; וַאֲבוֹתֵינוּ אָז בִּימֵי מֹשֶׁה, כְּשֶׁתָּפְשׂוּ גִּלְעָד שָׁתְקוּ אָז מִלְּקַחַת אוֹתָהּ כִּי לֹא חָשְׁשׁוּ בָּהֶם, אוֹ שֶׁמָּא בִּימֵי מֹשֶׁה לְקָחוּהָ, וְאַחֲרֵי כֵן לְקָחוּהָ הָאֻמּוֹת מִיַּד יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְשֶׁלָּנוּ הֵם:


**and he enticed him to go up to Ramoth Gilead** as is written in the Book of I Kings (22: 3): “Do you know that Ramoth Gilead is ours,” because when Jacob built the mound [of stones] there and called it Galead, he took possession of it, and our forefathers took Gilead and the heights (רָמוֹת), which are a continuation and an attachment to the city. But when our forefathers in Moses’ time seized Gilead, they were silent and refrained from taking it, because they did not care for it, or perhaps in Moses’ time, they did take it, but later the nations took it from Israel’s hands, but it is [in fact] ours.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:4:1)


**דְּרָשׁ נָא כַיּוֹם אֶת דְּבַר ה׳.** וְלֹא מִנְּבִיאֵי הַבְּעָלִים אֲשֶׁר אַתָּה בּוֹטֵחַ בָּהֶם:


**Please inquire today the word of the Lord** and [do not inquire] of the prophets of the baalim in whom you trust.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:5:1)


**וַיִּקְבֹּץ מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַנְּבִיאִים אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת.** וְכֻלָּם נְבִיאֵי אֱמֶת הָיוּ, כִּי מַה שֶּׁשָּׁאַל מִמֶּנּוּ יְהוֹשָׁפָט קִבֵּץ לוֹ. כֵּן מוֹכִיחַ בְּסָמוּךְ **וַיֵּצֵא הָרוּחַ וַיֹּאמֶר אֲנִי אֲפַתֶּנּוּ וְגוֹ׳**, עַד **וְהָיִיתִי לְרוּחַ שֶׁקֶר בְּפִי כָּל נְבִיאָיו**, שֶׁאוֹמֵר לָהֶם לֵאמֹר לְאַחְאָב **עֲלֵה וְיִתֵּן הָאֱלֹהִים בְּיַד הַמֶּלֶךְ**. מִכְּלָל שֶׁהָיוּ נְבִיאֵי ה׳, שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ מִתְנַבְּאִים אֶלָּא דְּבַר ה׳. שֶׁאִם הָיוּ נְבִיאֵי שֶׁקֶר אֵין הַפִּתּוּי נוֹפֵל עֲלֵיהֶם, שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ מִתְנַבְּאִים אֶלָּא מַה שֶּׁהָיוּ בּוֹדִים מִלִּבָּן. וְעוֹד שֶׁאָמַר יְהוֹשָׁפָט שׁוּב **הַאֵין פֹּה נָבִיא לַה׳ עוֹד** – וַדַּאי יוֹדֵעַ אֲנִי שֶׁהֵם נְבִיאֵי ה׳, אֶלָּא מְקֻבָּלְנִי מִבֵּית אָבִי אַבָּא, שֶׁאֵין שְׁנֵי נְבִיאִים מִתְנַבְּאִים בְּסִגְנוֹן אֶחָד וְלָשׁוֹן אֶחָד, וְהַלָּלוּ כֻּלָּם מִתְנַבְּאִים: ״עֲלֵה וְהַצְלַח״ (מלכין א כב:טו):


**And the king of Israel gathered the prophets, four hundred** And they were all true prophets, for he gathered for Jehoshaphat that which he had requested of him. And this is proven in the closely following text (verse 20f.): “And a spirit came forth, etc. and said: ‘I will entice him...and I will be a lying spirit in the mouth of all his prophets,’ for I will inspire them to say to Ahab: Ascend, and the Lord will deliver it into the hands of the king.” From here we can deduce that they were prophets of the Lord, for they prophesied only the word of the Lord, for if they were false prophets, they would not entice, for they would prophesy only that which they were inventing from their minds. Moreover, Jehoshaphat later said, “Is there no other prophet of the Lord?” I know that they are prophets of the Lord, but I have a tradition from my grandfather’s house that two prophets do not prophesy in the same style and with the same language, but all of these [prophets] are [unanimously] prophesying, “Ascend and prosper.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:7:1)


**כִּי כָל יָמָיו לְרָעָה.** כִּי זֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר: ״יַעַן אֲשֶׁר שִׁלַּחְתָּ אִישׁ חֶרְמִי וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים א כ:מב):


**but always bad** For this is what it says (I Kings 20:42): “Because you have released the man whom I designated for destruction, etc.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:8:1)


**מַהֵר מִיכָהוּ.** כְּתִיב חָסֵר יוֹ״ד; מִשּׁוּם בִּזָּיוֹן כִּנָּה לוֹ שֵׁם אַחֵר: מִיכָהוּ:


**Bring Micaiah at once** [Micaiah] is written with the “yud” missing. He called him by a different name, Micahu, in order to degrade him.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:9:1)


**מְלֻבָּשִׁים בְּגָדִים.** בִּגְדֵי מַלְכוּת, וְיֹשְׁבִין **בְּגֹרֶן פֶּתַח שַׁעַר שֹׁמְרוֹן**, בָּא לְהוֹדִיעֵנוּ, כִּי כְּשֶׁנִּתְנַבְּאוּ הַנְּבִיאִים לִפְנֵיהֶם הָיוּ יוֹשְׁבִים חוּץ לָעִיר, וְשָׁם בָּאוּ מְרַגְּלִים שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ אֲרָם, וְשָׁמְעוּ שֶׁהוֹדִיעַ לְמֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהוּא לְבַדּוֹ יִפּוֹל בַּמִּלְחָמָה. הוּא שֶׁצִּוָּה מֶלֶךְ אֲרָם לְחֵילוֹתָיו: **לֹא תִּלָּחֲמוּ אֶת הַקָּטֹן וְאֶת הַגָּדוֹל כִּי אִם אֶת מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְבַדּוֹ**, לְקַיֵּם דִּבְרֵי מִיכָיְהוּ. לְפִיכָךְ כְּתִיב **בְּגֹרֶן**, שֶׁהָיָה מָקוֹם מְרַגְּלִים לְרַגֵּל, אֲבָל אִם הוּעַד בְּתוֹךְ הָעִיר, לֹא הָיוּ הַמְּרַגְּלִים שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ אֲרָם יְכוֹלִין לָבֹא שֶׁמָּא יַרְגִּישׁוּ בָּהֶם שֶׁהֵם מְרַגְּלִים, אֲבָל כְּשֶׁהֵם חוּץ לָעִיר, אֲפִלּוּ יַרְגִּישׁוּ בָּהֶם וְיָבִינוּ כִּי מְרַגְּלִים הֵם, יְכוֹלִים הֵם לוֹמַר: לְמָקוֹם אַחֵר אָנוּ הוֹלְכִים, וּמַה שֶּׁקָּרַבְנוּ פֹּה אֶל הַגֹּרֶן – בִּשְׁבִיל הֶרְגֵּשׁ גָּדוֹל וְקִבּוּץ חֲיָילוֹת הֵנָּה פֶּתַח הָעִיר, תָּמַהְנוּ וּפָנִינוּ מִן הַדֶּרֶךְ לִרְאוֹת מַה זֶּה. וְהַמְּרַגְּלִים הֵם הוֹדִיעוּ לְמֶלֶךְ אֲרָם, וְהוּא צִוָּה שֶׁלֹּא לַהֲרֹג שׁוּם אָדָם כִּי אִם מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְבַדּוֹ, שֶׁאִם לֹא כֵן, מַה עָלָה בְלִבּוֹ שֶׁצִּוָּה שֶׁלֹּא לַהֲרֹג מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל רַק הַמֶּלֶךְ לְבַדּוֹ? אֶלָּא מְרַגְּלִים הָיוּ לוֹ וְשָׁמְעוּ אֶת מִיכָיְהוּ אוֹמֵר: **רָאִיתִי אֶת כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל נְפוֹצִים וְגוֹ׳**:


**dressed in [their] attire** the royal raiment **and seated in a threshing floor** He is telling us that when the prophets prophesied before them, they were sitting outside the city, and spies of the king of Aram came there and heard that he [the prophet] informed the king of Israel that he alone would fall in battle. That is the reason that the king of Aram commanded his forces (v. 30): “Attack neither small nor great; only the king of Israel,” to fulfill the words of Micaiah. That is why it is written, “in a threshing floor,” for it was a place where spies could spy. If the meeting had been inside the city, the spies of the king of Aram would not have been able to infiltrate, for they would have detected that they were spies; but when they were outside the city, even if they [the Israelites] became aware of them, they could say, “We are going elsewhere, and the reason we drew near to the threshing floor is because of the great tumult and gathering of the armies here at the gate of the city; we were amazed, and turned off the road to see what was going on.” Consequently, the spies informed the king of Aram [what they had heard], who commanded to slay no one but the king of Israel. Otherwise, why did it enter his mind to command not to slay anyone of Israel, but the king? But he had spies who had heard Micaiah saying, (verses 16f.): “I saw all the Israelites scattered, etc. And the Lord said: These have no master.” This was a sign that the king would be slain, and that the Israelites would return each one to his house in peace. And they heard that he said, (v. 27): “If you return in peace...” The spies heard all and informed the king of Aram. The king of Aram then commanded to act in accordance with the word of Micaiah, and to slay only the king.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:14:1)


**עֲלוּ וְהַצְלִיחוּ וְיִנָּתְנוּ בְיֶדְכֶם.** כְּלוֹמַר: הַלְוַאי שֶׁתַּצְלִיחוּ וְיִנָּתְנוּ בְיֶדְכֶם:


**Go up and triumph, and may they be delivered into your hand** i.e., May you only prosper, and may they be delivered into your hands.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:16:1)


**נְפוֹצִים עַל הֶהָרִים.** כִּי כְּשֶׁבּוֹרְחִים מִן הַמִּלְחָמָה בּוֹרְחִים עַל הֶהָרִים לְבֵית מָנוֹס, כְּמוֹ ״וְהַנִּשְׁאָרִים הֶרָה נָּסוּ״ (בראשית יד:י); וְעוֹד, שֶׁהַמִּלְחָמָה הָיְתָה בְּרָמוֹת גִּלְעָד, מְקוֹם הָרִים וּגְבָעוֹת:


**scattered over the mountains** For when they flee from battle, they flee on the mountains to a refuge, like (Gen. 14:10): “and those who remained fled to the mountains.” In addition, the battle was on the heights of Gilead, a mountainous and hilly terrain.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:16:2)


**וַיֹּאמֶר ה׳ לֹא אֲדֹנִים לָאֵלֶּה.** סִימָן שֶׁיֵּהָרֵג הַמֶּלֶךְ, אֲבָל יִשְׂרָאֵל **יָשׁוּבוּ אִישׁ לְבֵיתוֹ בְּשָׁלוֹם**. וְשָׁמְעוּ אֶת זֶה שֶׁאָמַר **אִם שׁוֹב תָּשׁוּב בְּשָׁלוֹם**. הַכֹּל שָׁמְעוּ הַמְרַגְּלִים וְהִגִּידוּ לְמֶלֶךְ אֲרָם, וְאָז צִוָּה מֶלֶךְ אֲרָם לַעֲשׂוֹת כִּדְבַר מִיכָיְהוּ לַהֲרֹג הַמֶּלֶךְ לְבַדּוֹ:


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:16:3)


**וַיֹּאמֶר ה׳ לֹא אֲדוֹנִים לָאֵלֶּה.** לְפִיכָךְ **נְפוֹצִים כַּצֹּאן עַל הֶהָרִים** שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם **רֹעֶה**, שֶׁמַּלְכָּם יֵהָרֵג:


**And the Lord said: These have no master** They are scattered like sheep on the mountains, because they have no shepherd, for their king will be slain.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:16:4)


**יָשׁוּבוּ אִישׁ לְבֵיתוֹ בְּשָׁלוֹם.** שֶׁאַחְאָב לְבַדּוֹ יֵהָרֵג, אֲבָל שְׁאָר יִשְׂרָאֵל יָשׁוּבוּ אִישׁ לְבֵיתוֹ בְּשָׁלוֹם, כַּאֲשֶׁר צִוָּה אֲרָם לְעַמּוֹ (להלן, ל):


**Let them return each one to his house in peace** Ahab alone will be killed, but the rest of the Israelites will return each one to his house in peace, as the king of Aram commanded his people.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:18:1)


**וְכָל צְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם עֹמְדִים עַל יְמִינוֹ וּשְׂמֹאלוֹ.** אֵלּוּ מַלְכֵי הַשְׁחָתָה (ס״א מַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּּׁרֵת) עוֹמְדִים לְפָנָיו:


**and all the host of heaven were standing on His right and His left** These are the angels of destruction who stand before Him.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 18:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/18:20:1)
