**כִּי עֲתַלְיָהוּ הַמִּרְשַׁעַת בָּנֶיהָ פָרְצוּ.** הֻצְרַךְ לוֹמַר, כִּי מֵחָדָשׁ בְּנָאוֹ שְׁלֹמֹה בִּנְיָן חָזָק, כִּי מִשֶּׁבְּנָאוֹ שְׁלֹמֹה עַד יוֹאָשׁ לֹא הָיוּ רַק מֵאָה עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנָה וּכְבָר הָיָה נִפְרָץ, שֶׁאִם לֹא שֶׁפָּרְצוּ עֲתַלְיָהוּ וּבָנֶיהָ, עֲדַיִן הָיָה קַיָּם, שֶׁהֲרֵי מִיּוֹאָשׁ עַד יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ יוֹתֵר מִמָּאתַיִם שָׁנָה, וְלֹא הֻצְרְכוּ לְחַזְּקוֹ עַד יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ; וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵּן לֹא עֲשָׂאוֹ יוֹאָשׁ חָדָשׁ כְּבַתְּחִלָּה. וּמִגְּמַר הַבַּיִת עַד יוֹאָשׁ מֵאָה עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ כֵּיצַד? שְׁלֹמֹה מָלַךְ אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה וְאַרְבַּע שָׁנָה לְמַלְכוּתוֹ הִתְחִיל לִבְנוֹתוֹ, וְשֶׁבַע שָׁנָה עִכֵּב בְּבִנְיָנוֹ, הֲרֵי עֶשֶׂר שָׁנָה, וְנִשְׁאַר שְׁלוֹשִׁים; וְרְחַבְעָם מָלַךְ שְׁבַע עֶשְׂרֵה, הֲרֵי אַרְבָּעִים וָשֶׁבַע; וַאֲבִיָּה שָׁלוֹשׁ, הֲרֵי חֲמִשִּים; וְאָסָא אַרְבָּעִים וְאַחַת, הֲרֵי תִּשְׁעִים וְאַחַת; וִיהוֹשָׁפָט עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ, הֲרֵי מֵאָה וְשֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה; יְהוֹרָם שְׁמוֹנֶה, הֲרֵי מֵאָה עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע; וַאֲחַזְיָה אַחַת, הֲרֵי מֵאָה עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ. וּמִיּוֹאָשׁ עַד יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ כֵּיצַד? אֲמַצְיָה מָלַךְ עֶשְׂרִים וְתֵשַׁע שָׁנִים, עֻזִּיָּהוּ חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם, יוֹתָם שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה, אָחָז שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה, חִזְקִיָּה עֶשְׂרִים וְתֵשַׁע, מְנַשֶּׁה חֲמִשִּׁים וְחָמֵשׁ וְאָמוֹן שְׁתַּיִם, הֲרֵי מָאתַיִם חָסֵר אַחַת. וְאוֹתָן שָׁנִים יוֹתֵר שֶׁחָיָה יוֹאָשׁ לְאַחַר חֶזְקַת הַבַּיִת, נִמְצָא שֶׁלֹּא הֻצְרְכוּ לְחַזֵּק אֶת בֶּדֶק הַבַּיִת אִם לֹא עֲתַלְיָהוּ הַמִּרְשַׁעַת וּבָנֶיהָ פָּרְצוּהוּ:


**For the wicked Athaliah—her sons had breached** He had to say this because Solomon had built it as a new sturdy edifice. Only 125 years elapsed from the time that Solomon built it until the days of Joash and it was already breached. Had Athaliah and her sons not breached it, it would have continued to endure, for more than 200 years elapsed from Joash to Josiah and they did not have to repair it until Josiah, although Joash did not make it new as it was originally. One hundred and twenty-five years elapsed from the completion of the Temple until Joash. How so? Solomon reigned 40 years, and in the fourth year of his reign he commenced to build it. It took 7 years to build, totaling 10 years, leaving him 30. Rehoboam reigned 17, totaling 47; Abijah 3, totaling 50; Asa 41, totaling 91; Jehoshaphat 25, totaling 116; Jehoram 8, totaling 124, and Ahaziah one, totaling 125. And from Joash until Josiah were 200 years. How so? Amaziah reigned 29 years, Uzziah 52, Jotham 16, Ahaz 16, Hezekiah 29, Manasseh 55, Amon 2, totaling 200 less 1, in addition to those years that Joash lived after he had repaired the Temple. We find that they would not have had to repair the damage of the Temple had not the wicked Athaliah and her sons breached it.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:7:2)


**כִּי עֲתַלְיָהוּ וְגוֹ׳.** לָכֵן אֵין תֵּימַהּ אִם צָרִיךְ מְהֵרָה כָּל כָּךְ חִזּוּק. וְגַם אַל תִּתְמַהּ שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב שֶׁל הַקְדָּשׁוֹת בְּאוֹצְרוֹת בֵּית ה׳, עַד שֶׁהֻצְרְכוּ לִיתֵּן **קוֹל בִּיהוּדָה וּבִירוּשָׁלַיִם לְהָבִיא מַשְׂאַת, כִּי גַם כָּל קָדְשֵׁי בֵית ה׳ עָשׂוּ לַבְּעָלִים**:


**For Athaliah, etc.** It is therefore no wonder that it needed repairs so soon, and also no wonder that there was no hallowed silver and gold in the treasuries of the House of the Lord, so that they had to make an announcement in Judah and Jerusalem to bring the tax, for they had also used all the hallowed things of the House of the Lord for the baalim.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:8:1)


**וַיַּעֲשׂוּ אֲרוֹן אֶחָד.** וּבִמְלָכִים כְּתִיב ״וַיִּקֹּב חוֹר בְּדַלְתּוֹ״ (מלכים ב יב:י); עַל יְדֵי אוֹתוֹ חוֹר הָיוּ זוֹרְקִין בָּאָרוֹן הַהֶקְדָּשׁוֹת וְלֹא לִפְתּוֹחַ הַדֶּלֶת, פֶּן יָבֹא רַמַּאי וְיִגְנוֹב מִמֶּנּוּ:


**and they made one chest** and in II Kings (12:10), it is written: “and he bored a hole in its door,” and through that hole they would cast the hallowed things, but they did not open the door lest a thief come and steal from it.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:8:2)


**וַיִּתְּנֻהוּ בְּשַׁעַר בֵּית ה׳ חוּצָה.** כְּדֵי שֶׁיּוּכְלוּ לָבֹא אֵלָיו כָּל נְדִיב לֵב (שמות לה:כב) לָתֵת בּוֹ אֶת תְּרוּמַת ה׳:


**and they placed it in the gate of the House of the Lord, to the outside** so that all the generous people would be able to come and deposit the donation to the Lord therein.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:11:1)


**וְכִרְאוֹתָם כִּי רַב הַכֶּסֶף.** אָז בָּא סוֹפֵר הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**and when they saw that there was much money** then the king’s scribe came.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:11:2)


**וּפְקִיד כֹּהֵן רֹאשׁ.** מְשָׁרֵת הַמְּמֻנֶּה תַּחַת כֹּהֵן הָרֹאשׁ, וְהוּא סְגַן כְּהֻנָּה, עִם סוֹפֵר הַמֶּלֶךְ. וּבִמְלָכִים כְּתִיב ״וַיַּעַל סוֹפֵר הַמֶּלֶךְ וְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל״ (מלכים ב יב:יא) – פִּתְרוֹן ״וַיַּעַל סוֹפֵר הַמֶּלֶךְ״ וְאַף ״כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל״ שָׁלַח פְּקִידוֹ, הוּא הַסּוֹפֵר הַכּוֹתֵב מַה שֶּׁכֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל מְצַוֶּה לוֹ; כִּי אֵין הָגוּן וְכָבוֹד לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל לֵילֵךְ עִם עַבְדֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**and the appointee of the head priest** The servant appointed under the head priest, who was the assistant to the high priest, together with the king’s scribe. In II Kings (12:11), it is written: “the king’s scribe and the high priest went up.” The meaning is that the king’s scribe went up, and the high priest also sent his appointee, who was the scribe who wrote what the high priest commanded him, because it was unfitting and degrading for the high priest to go with the king’s servants.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:11:3)


**וַיְעָרוּ אֶת הָאָרוֹן.** דֻּגְמָא: ״וַתְּעַר כַּדָּהּ״ (בראשית כד:כ):


**empty the chest** - Heb. וִיעָרוּ. A similar instance is (Gen. 24:20): “and emptied (וַתְּעַר) her pitcher.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:11:4)


**וַיִּשָּׂאֻהוּ.** בְּכָל יוֹם וָיוֹם כְּשֶׁרוֹאִין כִּי רַב הַכֶּסֶף שֶׁבּוֹ, מְבִיאִין אוֹתוֹ לִפְקִיד הַמֶּלֶךְ בְּתוֹךְ הָעֲזָרָה, מְעָרִין אוֹתוֹ וְנוֹשְׂאִין אוֹתוֹ וּמַחֲזִירִין **אֶל מְקוֹמוֹ בְּשַׁעַר ה׳ חוּצָה**:


**carry it** Every day, when they saw that there was much money in it, they would bring it to the king’s appointee within the forecourt, empty it, carry it, and return it to its place outside the gate of the Lord.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:13:1)


**וַתַּעַל אֲרוּכָה לַמְּלָאכָה.** דֻּגְמָא: ״הַיְחַיּוּ הָאֲבָנִים״ דְּעֶזְרָא (נחמיה ג:לד), וְגַם לְעֵיל ״יוֹאָב יְחַיֶּה אֶת שְׁאָר הָעִיר״ (דברי הימים א יא:ח):


**and the work was improved** A similar instance is (Neh. 3:34): “Will they revive the stones” of Ezra. Also, above (I Chron. 11:8): “and Joab sustained.’


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:13:2)


**עַל מַתְכֻּנְתּוֹ.** עַל מְכוֹנוֹ:


**on its base** - Heb. עַל מַתְכֻּנְתּוֹ, on its base.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:14:1)


**כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת וְהַעֲלוֹת.** שְׁטוּשִׁיל בִּלְשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַז, הוּא שֶׁכּוֹתְשִׁין אֶת הַבְּשָׂמִים בְּמַכְתֶּשֶׁת; דּוּגְמָא: ״אִם תִּכְתּוֹשׁ אֱוִיל בַּמַּכְתֵּשׁ״ (משלי כז:כב). כֵּן אָמַר לִי רַבִּי יִצְחָק בַּר שְׁמוּאֵל מִנַּרְבּוֹנָא. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: **וְהַעֲלוֹת** אֵלּוּ הַנְּסָרִים שֶׁחוֹתְכִין עֲלֵיהֶם בָּשָׂר; וְדוּגְמָא בְּבֵיצָה (יא.): אֵין מְטַלְטְלִין אֶת הָעֱלִי לְקַצֵּב עָלָיו בָּשָׂר:


**service vessels and pestles** (*Stessel* in German.) This is [the implement] with which they crush spices in the mortar. A similar instance is (Prov. 27:22): “If you crush a fool in a mortar [among grain with a pestle (בַּעֱלִי)”]. Rabbi Isaac the son of Samuel of Narbonne told me this. Others say that וְהַעֲלוֹת refers to boards upon which meat is cut, and an example of this appears in Bezah (1:5): We may not move a board (עלי) to cut meat upon it.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:14:2)


**וְכַפּוֹת וּכְלֵי זָהָב וָכָסֶף.** וּבִמְלָכִים (מלכים ב יב:יד) כָּתִיב ״אַךְ לֹא יֵעָשֶׂה בֵּית ה׳ סִפּוֹת כֶּסֶף״, הָא כֵּיצַד? מְפֹרָשׁ בִּכְתוּבוֹת בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא (כתובות קו.-קו:): בְּעָא מִינֵּיהּ רַב הוּנָא מֵרַב: כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת מַהוּ שֶׁיַּעֲשׂוּ מִקָּדְשֵׁי בֶּדֶק הַבַּיִת וְכוּ׳:


**and spoons and gold and silver vessels** But in II Kings (12:14) it is written: “But there would not be made for the House of the Lord silver pitchers.” How is this possible? It is explained in *Kethuboth*, chapter 13 (106): Rav Huna inquired of Rav: May vessels of ministry be made of material hallowed for the repair of the Temple, etc.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:20:1)


**מֵעַל לָעָם.** גָּבוֹהַּ מִכָּל הָעָם כְּדֵי לְהַשְׁמִיעַ קוֹלוֹ. וּבְאֵיכָה רַבָּתִי (איכה רבתי פתיחתא כג) דּוֹרֵשׁ: סָמַךְ עַל עַצְמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה שַׂר גָּבוֹהַּ מִכָּל הָעָם, נָבִיא וְכֹהֵן, וּבְיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים וַחֲתַן הַמֶּלֶךְ, וְלֹא נִתְיָירֵא מִלּוֹמַר נְבוּאָה; וְעַל זֶה נֶאֱמַר ״אִם יֵהָרֵג בְּמִקְדַּשׁ ה׳ כֹּהֵן וְנָבִיא״ (איכה ב:כ):


**above the people** Higher than the people, in order to make his voice heard. In *Lamentations Rabbah* (Proem 23), it is interpreted as meaning that he relied on himself; he was a prince higher than all the people, a prophet and a priest, and it was Yom Kippur, and he was the king’s son-in-law, and he was not afraid to say the prophecy. Concerning this incident, it was stated (Lam. 2:20): “shall priest and prophet be slain in the Sanctuary of the Lord?”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:22:1)


**וּבְמוֹתוֹ אָמַר יֵרֶא ה׳ וְיִדְרוֹשׁ.** כִּי לֹא הֲרָגוּנִי אֶלָּא שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי שְׁלִיחוּתוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם, וּמֵאַחַר שֶׁנֶּהֱרַגְתִּי עָלָיו, מִן הַדִּין וְהָגוּן וְכְדַאי הוּא שֶׁיִּדְרוֹשׁ דָּמִי; וְכֵן הָיָה, כִּי מִיָּד נִדְרַשׁ דָּמוֹ, כִּי **לִתְקוּפַת הַשָּׁנָה וְגוֹ׳**:


**and upon his death, he said, “May the Lord see and requite.”** For they have slain me only because I spoke as the Omnipresent’s medium, and since I have been slain for His sake, it is proper and fitting that He requite my blood. And so it was: his blood was immediately requited, for “it came to pass at the turn of the year, etc.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:23:1)


**וַיְהִי לִתְקוּפַת הַשָּׁנָה.** לְפִי שֶׁבְּנֵי יְהוּדָה חָטְאוּ יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר שְׁבָטִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְעֵיל **וַיַּעַזְבוּ אֶת בֵּית ה׳ אֱלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם**:


**And it came to pass at the turn of the year** Because the sons of Judah sinned more than the other tribes, as it is written above (verse 18): “And they forsook the House of the Lord, the God of their fathers.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:23:2)


**וַיַּשְׁחִיתוּ אֶת כָּל שָׂרֵי הָעָם.** מוּסָב לְמַעְלָה **וְאַחֲרֵי מוֹת יְהוֹיָדָע בָּאוּ שָׂרֵי יְהוּדָה וַיִּשְׁתַּחֲווּ לַמֶּלֶךְ**, וְהֶחֱטִיאוּ אֶת יוֹאָשׁ אַחֲרֵיהֶם; עַל זֶה נִפְרַע לָהֶם גְּמוּלָם, שֶׁבָּאוּ חֵיל אֲרָם וְהִשְׁחִיתוּ אֶת כָּל שָׂרֵי הָעָם:


**and they destroyed all the princes of the people** This refers back to the above (verse 17): “And after the death of Jehoiada, the princes of Judah came and prostrated themselves to the king,” and caused Joash to sin after them. Because of that, they were paid in kind: the army of Aram came and destroyed all the princes of the people.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:23:3)


**מֵעָם.** מִהְיוֹת עָם:


**from among the people** from being a people.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:25:1)


**בִּדְמֵי בְּנֵי יְהוֹיָדָע.** בִּשְׁבִיל דְּמֵי זְכַרְיָה שֶׁהָיָה בֶּן יְהוֹיָדָע, כִּי הָיָה לוֹ לִזְכֹּר טוֹבָה וָחֶסֶד שֶׁגָּמַל עִמּוֹ יְהוֹיָדָע, וּבִשְׁבִיל אוֹתוֹ עָוֹן נִתְקַשְּׁרוּ **עָלָיו עֲבָדָיו וַיַּהַרְגֻהוּ עַל מִטָּתוֹ** – מָדְדוּ לוֹ מִדָּה כְּנֶגֶד מִדָּה: עַל שֶׁנֶּהֱרַג זְכַרְיָה שָׁקֵט וְשָׁלֵיו וּבוֹטֵחַ, שֶׁאֵין לוֹ לִדְאֹג, כִּי בֶּן יְהוֹיָדָע הוּא, וְלִכְבוֹדוֹ יִנְהֲגוּ בּוֹ כָּבוֹד, וְשָׁפְכוּ דָּמָיו לָאָרֶץ – וְעַל כֵּן נֶהֱרַג גַּם הוּא בְּשָׁלוֹם וְהַשְׁקֵט (ישעיה לב:יז) **עַל מִטָּתוֹ**:


**for the blood of the sons of Jehoiada the priest** Because of the blood of Zechariah, who was the son of Jehoiada, for he should have remembered the favors and the loving-kindness that Jehoiada had bestowed upon him, and because of that iniquity, his servants conspired against him and slew him in his bed. They meted out to him a measure corresponding to a measure. Because Zechariah was slain, tranquil and secure, with nothing to worry about, for he was the son of Jehoiada, and out of respect for him [Jehoiada], they respected him [Zechariah], and spilled his blood onto the ground, therefore, he [Jehoash] too was slain in tranquility and security in his bed.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 24:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/24:26:1)


**הַמּוֹאָבִית.** מָה לִי מִי הָיוּ וּמִמִּי יָצְאוּ, אִם מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹ מִמּוֹאָבִים? אֶלָּא יָבֹאוּ כְּפוּיֵי טוֹבָה, שֶׁכָּפוּ טוֹבָתוֹ שֶׁל אַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ לְלוֹט אֲבִיהֶם, שֶׁנִּלְחַם עִם הַמְּלָכִים כְּשֶׁשָּׁמַע שֶׁנִּשְׁבָּה לוֹט (בראשית יד:יד-טז), וְהֵם שָׂכְרוּ אֶת בִּלְעָם לְקַלֵּל בָּנָיו (במדבר כב:ה-ו), וְיִפָּרְעוּ מִיּוֹאָשׁ שֶׁכָּפָה טוֹבָתוֹ שֶׁל יְהוֹיָדָע וְהָרַג אֶת זְכַרְיָה בְּנוֹ; כֵּן מְפֹרָשׁ בַּמְּכִילְתָּא (מכילתא בשלח עמלק א):


**the Moabitess** What difference does it make who they were and from whom they were descended? However, [Scripture means that] those ingrates who did not show their gratitude for the favor of Abraham our father to Lot their father, who fought with the kings when he heard that Lot was captured, and they hired Balaam to curse his sons; [they were chosen] to punish Joash, who [similarly] did not show gratitude to Jehoiada and slew his son Zechariah. It is explained this way in *Mechilta* (Exod. 17:8).



## Chapter 25


### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:7:1)


**כִּי אֵין ה׳ עִם יִשְׂרָאֵל כֹּל בְּנֵי אֶפְרָיִם.** לְפִי שֶׁיָּרָבְעָם מִשֵּׁבֶט אֶפְרַיִם, וְהוּא הָיָה הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁהֶחֱטִיא אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, וּבְכָל דּוֹר וָדוֹר אַחֲרָיו הָיָה לָהֶם לְמוֹקֵשׁ, שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹבְדִין לְעֶגְלֵי זָהָב שֶׁל יָרָבְעָם (דברי הימים ב יג:ח):


**for God is not with Israel, all the sons of Ephraim** because Jeroboam was of the tribe of Ephraim, and he was the first to cause Israel to sin. And in every generation thereafter he remained a stumbling block for them, for they worshipped the golden calves of Jeroboam.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:8:1)


**כִּי אִם בֹּא אַתָּה.** פִּתְרוֹן: אִם לֹא תַאֲמִין בִּדְבָרַי, הֲלֹא תְנַסֶּה; בֹּא אַתָּה וַ**עֲשֵׂה וַחֲזַק לַמִּלְחָמָה**:


**But if you come** The meaning is: If you do not believe my words, will you not try [to put effort into your military campaign?] Come, do, and strengthen yourself for war.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:8:2)


**לַעְזֹר וּלְהַכְשִׁיל.** פִּתְרוֹן: **יֵשׁ** לוֹ **כֹּחַ** וּגְבוּרָה לַעְזֹר לְךָ בְּלֹא חַיִל שֶׁל אֶפְרַיִם, וּלְהַכְשִׁיל אוֹתְךָ אִם תּוֹלִיכֵם עִמְּךָ:


**to help or to cause to stumble** The meaning is: He has the strength to help you without the army of Ephraim and to cause you to stumble if you take them with you.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:12:1)


**וְכֻלָּם נִבְקָעוּ. וַיְהִי אַחֲרֵי בֹא וְגוֹ׳.** בְּאֵיכָה רַבָּתִי (איכה רבה פתיחתא יד) עָלָיו נֶאֱמַר ״וְרָגַז וְשָׂחַק וְאֵין נָחַת״ (משלי כט:ט):


**and they were all split open, etc...after [Amaziah] had come, etc.** In Lam. Rabbah it says about him, “whether he is angry or he laughs” (Prov. 29:9).



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:13:1)


**וַיִּפְשְׁטוּ בְּעָרֵי יְהוּדָה מִשֹּׁמְרוֹן וְעַד בֵּית חוֹרוֹן.** לְאַחַר שֶׁחָזְרוּ לִמְקוֹמָם, כְּדִלְעֵיל **וַיִּחַר אַפָּם מְאֹד בִּיהוּדָה וַיָּשׁוּבוּ לִמְקוֹמָם**; שֶׁאִם לֹא כֵן, אֶלָּא מִיָּד שֶׁנִּבְדְּלוּ מֵאֲמַצְיָה פָּשְׁטוּ בְּעָרֵי יְהוּדָה, הָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר: וַיִּפְשְׁטוּ בְּעָרֵי יְהוּדָה מִבֵּית חוֹרוֹן עַד שׁוֹמְרוֹן:


**raided the cities of Judah from Samaria to Beth-Horon** after they returned to their place, as above, “they were very angry with Judah and returned to their place.” For if it were not so, i.e., if they had raided the cities of Judah as soon as they left Amaziah, Scripture would have said, “and they raided the cities of Judah from Beth-Horon to Samaria,” [i.e., going from Judah to Samaria].



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:16:1)


**כִּי יָעַץ אֱלֹהִים לְהַשְׁחִיתֶךָ.** כְּלוֹמַר: אִם אֵין אַתָּה שׁוֹמֵעַ לַעֲצָתִי לֵילֵךְ בְּדֶרֶךְ יְשָׁרָה, דַּע לְךָ, אַתָּה הַמֶּלֶךְ, כִּי יָעַץ ה׳ לְהַשְׁחִיתֶךָ, הוֹאִיל **כִּי עָשִׂיתָ זֹּאת וְלֹא שָׁמַעְתָּ לַעֲצָתִי**. וּמַה הִיא הָעֵצָה? שֶׁהִשִּׂיאֲךָ לְהִתְגָּרוֹת בְּמֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלִיפּוֹל לְפָנָיו, כְּדִכְתִיב **וַיִּוָּעֵץ אֲמַצְיָה מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה וַיִּשְׁלַח אֶל יוֹאָשׁ וְגוֹ׳**:


**that God has given counsel to destroy you** i.e., If you do not hearken to My advice to follow the straight path, you should know, O king, that the Lord has given counsel to destroy you because you have done this, and have not hearkened to the counsel. Now what is the counsel? That he enticed you to provoke the king of Israel and to fall before him, as it is written: “And Amaziah the king of Judah took counsel, and he sent to Joash, etc.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:17:1)


**לְךָ נִתְרָאֶה פָנִים.** לְהַרְאוֹת גְּבוּרָתֵינוּ עַל סוּסִים בָּרְמָחִים:


**Come, let us confront each other** to show our might on horses with spears.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:18:1)


**הַחוֹחַ אֲשֶׁר בַּלְּבָנוֹן.** שֶׁהוּא שְׁפַל רוּחַ:


**The thistle that was in Lebanon** which is of short stature.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:18:2)


**שָׁלַח אֶל הָאֶרֶז.** שֶׁהוּא שַׂר גָּדוֹל מִכָּל הָאִילָנוֹת:


**sent to the cedar** which is the greatest prince of all the trees.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:18:3)


**וַתַּעֲבֹר וַתִּרְמוֹס.** וְלֹא רָצָה הָאֶרֶז לְהָרְגוֹ, כִּי זִילוּת הוּא בְּעֵינָיו לִטְרֹף הַחוֹחַ, אֶלָּא עָבְרָה עָלָיו וּרְמָסַתּוֹ לְבִזָּיוֹן, כִּי הֵיאַךְ נְשָׂאוֹ לִבּוֹ לִשְׁלֹחַ בִּזָּיוֹן גָּדוֹל כָּזֶה לְאֶרֶז גָּבוֹהַּ; אַף אַתָּה כְּחוֹחַ, שֶׁאֵין לְךָ רַק שְׁנֵי שְׁבָטִים, וְעַתָּה עֲבָדַי יְקוּמוּן עָלֶיךָ וְיִרְמְסוּךָ כְּשֶׁיִּשְׁמְעוּ הַבִּזָּיוֹן הַזֶּה:


**passed by and trampled the thistle** The cedar did not wish to kill it because it was degrading in his eyes to cut down the thistle, but a beast of the field passed by and trampled it in contempt, for how did he dare to send such a great insult to the lofty cedar? You too are like the thistle, for you have only two tribes, and now my servants will rise up against you and trample you when they hear of this disgrace.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:19:1)


**וּנְשָׂאֲךָ לִבְּךָ.** לִרְדֹּף אַחַר הַכָּבוֹד, וְרוֹצֶה אַתָּה לְהִתְכַּבֵּד אַף בִּי; וּבִמְלָכִים: ״הִכָּבֵד וְשֵׁב בְּבֵיתֶךָ״ (מלכים ב יד:י):


**and your heart has made you haughty** to pursue glory, and you wish to be glorified also on my account. In II Kings (14:10), the reading is: “Retain your honor by staying home.”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:20:1)


**כִּי מֵהָאֱלֹהִים הִיא לְמַעַן תִּתָּם בְּיָד.** כְּלוֹמַר: בִּידֵיהֶם; כְּדִלְעֵיל **יָדַעְתִּי כִּי יָעַץ אֱלֹהִים לְהַשְׁחִיתֶךָ**:


**because this was from God in order to deliver them into their hands** i.e., into their hands, as above (verse 16): “I know that God has given counsel to destroy you.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:21:1)


**בְּבֵית שֶׁמֶשׁ.** הָיְתָה הַמִּלְחָמָה, וְהֻצְרַךְ לוֹמַר, שֶׁלֹּא תֹאמַר: כְּשֶׁתְּפָשׂוּ אֲמַצְיָה, לָמָּה הֱבִיאוֹ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם וְלֹא לְשׁוֹמְרוֹן? לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה בֵּית שֶׁמֶשׁ סָמוּךְ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**in Beth-Shemesh** the war took place. It was necessary to say this lest you ask why they brought Amaziah to Jerusalem and not to Samaria when they seized him. [The answer is] that Beth-Shemesh was near Jerusalem.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:23:1)


**וַיִּפְרֹץ בְּחוֹמַת.** שֶׁאָמַר: מֵעַתָּה לֹא יִמְרֹד בִּי, כִּי לֹא גָבַהּ לִבּוֹ (דברי הימים ב כו:טז) כָּל כָּךְ לִמְרֹד, הוֹאִיל וְנִפְרְצָה הַחוֹמָה:


**and breached the wall of...** He said, “From now on, he will not rebel against me, because his heart will not be so haughty since the wall has been breached”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:24:1)


**הַנִּמְצָאִים בְּבֵית הָאֱלֹהִים עִם עֹבֵד אֱדוֹם.** כִּי הוּא וּבָנָיו נְגִידִים עַל הָאוֹצָרוֹת כָּל יְמֵיהֶם, כְּדִלְעֵיל (דברי הימים א כו:טו):


**that were found in the House of God with Obed-Edom** for he and his sons were keepers of the treasures all their lives.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:24:2)


**וְאֶת בְּנֵי הַתַּעֲרֻבוֹת.** שֶׁלֹּא הִנִּיחוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת אֲמַצְיָהוּ לִהְיוֹת עוֹד מֶלֶךְ בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, עַד שֶׁנָּתַן בְּנֵי הַשָּׂרִים עֵרָבוֹן שֶׁלֹּא יִמְרְדוּ עוֹד בּוֹ; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן אוֹתוֹ בִּמְלָכִים (מלכים ב יד:יד): ״בְּנֵי רַבְרְבַיָּא״. וְרַבֵּינוּ יוֹסֵף פֵּרֵשׁ **תַּעֲרֻבוֹת** – אוֹתָן הַתַּעֲרֻבוֹת שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ לַמֶּלֶךְ נְתוּנִים בְּעֵרָבוּת שֶׁלֹּא יִמְרְדוּ אֲבוֹתָם בּוֹ. וְלֹא נִרְאֶה לִי, כִּי לֹא מָצִינוּ שֶׁגָּבְרָה יַד יְהוּדָה כָּל כָּךְ עַל אֲחֵרִים שֶׁיְּהוּ צְרִיכִים לִיתֵּן לִיהוּדָה בְּנֵיהֶם לְעֵרָבוֹן:


**and the hostage children** For the Jews did not permit Amaziah to be king any longer in Jerusalem until he made the children of the officers hostages so that they would no longer rebel against him, and so did Jonathan translate it in II Kings (14:14): “the children of the officers.” Rabbi Joseph (Kara) explained “the hostage children” to mean those hostages that the king had taken as security so that their fathers would not rebel against him. This, however, does not appear correct to me because we do not find that Judah had such power over others that they could have [forced them] to give Judah their children as hostages.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:27:1)


**וּמֵעֵת אֲשֶׁר סָר אֲמַצְיָה מֵאַחֲרֵי ה׳.** וְנָפַל לִפְנֵי מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל מִיָּד **וַיִּקְשְׁרוּ עָלָיו קֶשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלִַם** לְהָרְגוֹ, כִּי הָיָה לִבָּם דָּוֶה אִישׁ עַל בְּנוֹ וְעַל אָחִיו וְעַל קְרוֹבוֹ שֶׁנֶּהֶרְגוּ עַל יָדוֹ, לְפִי שֶׁנִּתְגָּרָה בְּמֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And from the time that Amaziah turned away from following the Lord** and was defeated by the king of Israel, immediately **they conspired against him in Jerusalem** to kill him, because each one was grieved over his son, his brother, and his kinsman who were slain because of him, because he provoked the king of Israel.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 25:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/25:27:2)


**וַיָּנָס לָכִישָׁה.** עִיר מִבְצָר אֲשֶׁר נִלְחַם עָלֶיהָ סַנְחֵרִיב (מלכים ב יח:יד), וְנָס לָכִישָׁה וַיְהִי שָׁם ט״ו שָׁנִים, עַד שֶׁשָּׁלְחוּ אַחֲרָיו **לָכִישָׁה וַיְמִיתֻהוּ שָׁם**. וְאוֹתָן חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה שֶׁהָיָה בְלָכִישׁ מָלְכָה יְכָלְיָהוּ אֵם עֻזִּיָּהוּ תַּחְתָּיו. אַךְ קָשֶׁה לִי; וּבְפֵירוּשׁ ר׳ יוֹסֵף זַצַ״ל (מלכים ב יד:יז-כב) רָאִיתִי: גַּם אוֹתָן חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנִים שֶׁהָיָה בְלָכִישׁ מָלַךְ עֻזִּיָּהוּ בְּנוֹ תַּחְתָּיו – וְלֹא נָכוֹן לְפוֹתְרוֹ כֵּן, שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּתִיב בְּסָמוּךְ: ״וְהוּא בֶּן שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה״ (דברי הימים ב כו:ג) – נִמְצָא שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בִּזְמַן שֶׁבָּרַח אָבִיו אֶלָּא בֶּן שָׁנָה; וְעוֹד, שֶׁאִם כֵּן, הָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר: וַיִּקְחוּ אֶת עֻזִּיָּהוּ וַיַּמְלִיכוּ תַּחַת אָבִיו בְּחַיָּיו; אֶלָּא מַשְׁמַע שֶׁלֹּא מָלַךְ בְּחַיֵּי אָבִיו:


**and he fled to Lachish** a fortified city against which Sennacherib waged war. He fled to Lachish, where he remained fifteen years until they followed him to Lachish and assassinated him there. And during those fifteen years that he was in Lachish, Jecoliah, Uzziah’s mother, reigned in his stead. This is how it appears to me. In the commentary of Rabbi Joseph (Kara), I saw that during those fifteen years that he was in Lachish, Uzziah his son reigned in his stead, but it is incorrect to interpret it in this manner, for it is written immediately following (26:1): “who was sixteen years old.” We find that when his father fled, he was only one year old. Moreover, if this is the case, it should say, “took Uzziah and made him king instead of his father during his lifetime.” Rather, Scripture intimated that he did not reign during his father’s lifetime.



## Chapter 26


### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:4:1)


**וַיַּעַשׂ הַיָּשָׁר בְּעֵינֵי ה׳ כְּכָל.** הַיָּשָׁר שֶׁ**עָשָׂה אָבִיו** עָשָׂה, וְלֹא עָשָׂה כְּרִשְׁעָתוֹ:


**And he did what was right in the eyes of the Lord** like all the proper things that his father did, but not like his wickedness.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:5:1)


**בִּימֵי זְכַרְיָהוּ.** זֶה עֻזִּיָּהוּ, וּשְׁלֹשָׁה שֵׁמוֹת נִקְרְאוּ לוֹ: עֻזִּיָּהוּ, זְכַרְיָהוּ, עֲזַרְיָהוּ. וְדֻגְמָא: יְהוֹאָחָז, אֲחַזְיָהוּ (מלכים ב יד:יג):


**in the days of Zechariah** This refers to Uzziah, and he was called by three names: Uzziah, Zechariah, and Azariah, similar to (II Kings 14): Yehoahaz/Ahaziah.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:5:2)


**הַמֵּבִין בִּרְאוֹת הָאֱלֹהִים.** שֶׁהֵבִין בְּיִרְאַת אֱלֹהִים שֶׁטּוֹבָה הִיא, וְנִתְיָרֵא מֵה׳:


**who understood the visions of God** who understood that the fear of God is good, and feared the Lord.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:9:1)


**וַיִּבֶן עֻזִּיָּהוּ מִגְדָּלִים וְגוֹ׳.** הֻצְרַךְ לְחַזֵּק מַה שֶּׁפָּרְצוּ בַחוֹמָה בִּימֵי אָבִיו (דברי הימים ב כה:כג):


**And Uzziah built towers, etc.** He had to strengthen the parts of the wall they breached in his father’s days.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:10:1)


**אִכָּרִים.** אַרַאטוּ״רִי בְּלַעַ״ז:


**plowmen** - Heb. אִכָּרִים, *aratkori* in Italian, plowmen.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:10:2)


**וּכְרָמִים.** דֻּגְמָא: ״לְכֹרְמִים וּלְיֹגְבִים״ (מלכים ב כה:יב); שׁוֹמְרֵי בְּהֵמוֹת הָיָה לוֹ:


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:10:3)


**כִּי אוֹהֵב אֲדָמָה הָיָה.** מְפֹרָשׁ בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה כב:ג): שְׁנַיִם לְהוּטִים אַחֲרֵי אֲדָמָה הָיוּ, וְלֹא נִמְצָא בָם תּוֹעֶלֶת, הוּא וְקַיִן:


**for he loved the soil** It is explained in *Genesis Rabbah* (See 22:3): Two had a passion for agriculture, and no good was found in them: he and Cain.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:15:1)


**חִשְּׁבֹנוֹת מַחֲשֶׁבֶת חוֹשֵׁב.** דֻּגְמָא ״מַעֲשֵׂה חוֹשֵׁב״ (שמות כו:א), כְּלֵי מִלְחָמוֹת עָשָׂה בְאוּמָנוּת, כְּגוֹן דִּיק וְאֵילִים הַמְנַגְּחִים בַּחוֹמָה, כְּדִכְתִיב ״כִּי בְתַחְבֻּלוֹת תַּעֲשֶׂה לְךָ מִלְחָמָה״ (משלי כד:ו):


**engines, invented by skillful men** - Heb. חִשְּׁבֹנוֹת מַחֲשֶׁבֶת חוֹשֵׁב, similar to (Exod. 28:6): “expert work (מַעֲשֵׂה חֹשֵׁב.)” He made war engines with craftsmanship, such as siege mounds and battering rams, as it is written (Prov. 24:6): “For you shall wage war with strategies.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:16:1)


**וַיִּמְעַל בַּה׳ אֱלֹהָיו.** כָּאן נוֹפֵל לוֹמַר מְעִילָה, כִּי בִּשְׁגָגָה עָשָׂה; דּוּגְמָא: ״נֶפֶשׁ כִּי תִמְעֹל מַעַל וְחָטְאָה בִּשְׁגָגָה״ (ויקרא ה:טו):


**and he trespassed against the Lord, his God** - Heb. וַיִּמְעַל. Here it is appropriate to say מְעִילָה (trespass), for he sinned inadvertently, similar to (Lev. 5:15): “If a person commits a trespass (תִמְעל מַעַל) and sins inadvertently.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:16:2)


**וַיָּבֹא אֶל הֵיכַל ה׳.** שֶׁאָמַר: נָאֶה לַמֶּלֶךְ לְשָׁרֵת לְמֶלֶךְ הַכָּבוֹד:


**and he came into the Temple of the Lord** for he said, “It is proper for a king to serve the King of glory.”



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:19:1)


**וּבְיָדוֹ מִקְטֶרֶת לְהַקְטִיר.** וְרָצָה לְהַכּוֹת בּוֹ הַכֹּהֵן הַמּוֹנְעוֹ מִלְּהַקְטִיר קְטֹרֶת:


**and in his hand was a censer to burn** and he wished to strike the priest who prevented him from burning incense.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:21:1)


**בֵּית הַחָפְשִׁית.** וּבִירוּשַׁלְמִי (כלאים ט, ג לב, א) מְפֹרָשׁ: עָשָׂה לוֹ בַּיִת בְּבֵית הַקְּבָרוֹת; עַל שֵׁם ״בַּמֵּתִים חָפְשִׁי״ (תהלים פח:ו):


**in a house of retirement** Heb. בֵּית הַחָפְשִׁית. And in *Yerushalmi* (unknown) it is explained that he made himself a house in the cemetery, reminiscent of (Ps. 88:6): “Among the dead, a free man (חָפְשִׁי) .”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:21:2)


**כִּי נִגְזַר.** לוֹ מִלָּבֹא עוֹד בֵּין הַבְּרִיּוֹת, דֻּגְמָא (אסתר ב:א): ״וְאֵת אֲשֶׁר נִגְזַר עָלֶיהָ״ (מגילה יב:):


**for it had been decreed** upon him to come no longer among people, similar to (Esther 2:1): “and that which was decreed upon her.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 26:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/26:23:1)


**בִּשְׂדֵה הַקְּבוּרָה.** וְלֹא בַּמְּעָרָה עִם הַמְּלָכִים:


**in the cemetery** but not in the cave with the kings.



## Chapter 27


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 27:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/27:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/27:2:1)


**כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה עֻזִּיָּהוּ אָבִיו.** הַטּוֹבוֹת שֶׁעָשָׂה אָבִיו; וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן יוֹחַאי בְּמַסֶּכֶת סוּכָּה (מה, ב): אִלְמָלֵא יִהְיֶה אַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ מְקַבֵּל עָלָיו כָּל עֲוֹנוֹת הַדּוֹרוֹת עַד לוֹ, אֲנִי הָיִיתִי מְקַבֵּל עָלַי חֲטָאֵי הַדּוֹרוֹת מֵאַבְרָהָם עַד לִי, וְאִם הָיָה יוֹתָם בֶּן עֻזִּיָּהוּ עִמִּי, הָיִינוּ מְקַבְּלִין עָלֵינוּ מֵאַבְרָהָם וְעַד סוֹף הַדּוֹרוֹת. שֶׁבְּכָל הַמְּלָכִים שֶׁלְּפָנָיו וּלְאַחֲרָיו מָצִינוּ בָהֶם חֵטְא, חוּץ מִיּוֹתָם: דָּוִד – ״רַק בְּאוּרִיָּה הַחִתִּי״ (מלכים א טו:ה); שְׁלֹמֹה – ״נָשָׁיו הִטּוּ לְבָבוֹ״ (מלכים א יא:ד), וּרְחַבְעָם – ״עָזַב אֶת תּוֹרַת ה׳״ (דברי הימים ב יב:א); אֲבִיָּה – וַיֵּלֶךְ בְּכָל חַטֹּאת אָבִיו (מלכים א טו:ג); אָסָא – הוֹצִיא ״כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב מֵאוֹצְרוֹת בֵּית ה׳״ (דברי הימים ב טז:ב), וְנָתַן הַנָּבִיא ״בֵּית הַמַּהְפֶּכֶת״ (דברי הימים ב טז:י); יְהוֹשָׁפָט – נִתְחַבֵּר לָרָשָׁע (דברי הימים ב כ:לה); יְהוֹרָם – הָרַג אֶחָיו (דברי הימים ב כא:ד); אֲחַזְיָהוּ – ״אִמּוֹ יוֹעַצְתּוֹ לְהַרְשִׁיעַ״ (דברי הימים ב כב:ג); יוֹאָשׁ – הָרַג זְכַרְיָה וְנַעֲשָׂה קָדֵשׁ (דברי הימים ב כד); אֲמַצְיָה – הִשְׁתַּחֲוָה לִפְסִילֵי שֵׂעִיר (דברי הימים ב כה:יד); עֻזִּיָּהוּ – נִכְנַס לַהֵיכָל לְהַקְטִיר (דברי הימים ב כו:טז-יט); אָחָז – הָלַךְ בְּדַרְכֵי מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגַם עָשָׂה מַצֵּבוֹת לַבְּעָלִים (דברי הימים ב כח:ב); חִזְקִיָּה – ״גָּבַהּ לִבּוֹ וַיְהִי עָלָיו קֶצֶף״ (דברי הימים ב לב:כה), וְעַל שְׁלֹשָׁה לֹא הוֹדוּ לוֹ (משנה פסחים ד,ט); מְנַשֶּׁה – עָשָׂה ״הָרַע בְּעֵינֵי ה׳״ (דברי הימים ב לג:ב); יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ – לֹא שָׁמַע דִּבְרֵי נְבוּאָה ״מִפִּי אֱלֹהִים״ (דברי הימים ב לה:כב); צִדְקִיָּהוּ – עָשָׂה הָרַע בְּעֵינֵי ה׳ וְלֹא נִכְנַע מִפְּנֵי יִרְמְיָה (דברי הימים ב לו:יב); אֲבָל יוֹתָם – לֹא נִמְצָא בּוֹ שׁוּם דֹּפִי (כָּךְ אָמַר לִי רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בַּר מֹשֶׁה):


**as all that his father Uzziah** the good deeds that his father had done. That is what R. Simeon ben Yohai said in Tractate *Sotah* (sic. Should be *Sukkah* 45b): If Abraham would take upon himself all the sins of the generations until his time, I would take upon myself the sins of the generations from Abraham until my time. And if Jotham the son of Uzziah were with me, we would take upon ourselves the sins of all the generations from Abraham until the end of all generations, for in all the kings before him and after him we find sin, except in Jotham. Concerning David it is written, (I Kings 15:5): “Only concerning the matter of Uriah the Hittite”; concerning Solomon it is written, (ibid. 11:4): “His wives turned his heart away”; Rehoboam forsook the Lord’s Torah; Abijah (ibid. 15:3): “went in all his father’s sins”; Asa took silver and gold from the treasuries of the House of the Lord and put the prophet in prison; Jehoshaphat associated with a wicked man (Ahab and his son Ahaziah); Joram killed his brother; Ahaziah’s mother advised him to sin; Joash killed Zechariah and was [later punished by being] sexually molested (*Yer. Kid.* 1:7); Amaziah prostrated himself before the graven images of Seir; Uzziah entered the Temple to burn incense; Ahaz went in the ways of the kings of Israel and also made monuments for the baalim; Hezekiah became haughty and there was anger upon him, and the sages did not concur with him on three things that he did (*Pes.* 4:9); Manasseh did evil in the eyes of the Lord; Josiah did not heed the words of prophecy from God; Zedekiah did evil in the eyes of the Lord and did not humble himself before Jeremiah (below 36:12). In Jotham, however, no trace of sin was found. (R. Eliezer the son of Moses told me this.)


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 27:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/27:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/27:2:2)


**וְעוֹד הָעָם מַשְׁחִיתִים.** כְּדִכְתִיב ״עוֹד הָעָם מְזַבְּחִים וּמְקַטְּרִים״ (מלכים ב טו:לה):


**the people were still acting corruptly** as it is written in II Kings (15:35): “the people were still slaughtering and burning incense [on the high places].”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 27:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/27:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/27:3:1)


**וּבְחוֹמַת הָעֹפֶל.** דֻּגְמָא (ישעיהו לב:יד) ״עֹפֶל וָבַחַן הָיָה״, בּוֹנֶה מְצוּדוֹת:


**and in the wall of the rampart** - Heb. עֹפֶל, similar to (Isa. 32:14): “rampart and tower (עפֶל וָבַחַן) .” He built strongholds.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 27:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/27:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/27:4:1)


**וּבֶחֳרָשִׁים בָּנָה בִּירָנִיּוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן בִּירָה גְּדוֹלָה:


**and in the forests he built palaces** - Heb. בִּירָנִית, an expression of a large palace (בִּירָה).



## Chapter 28


### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 28:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:7:1)


**אֶת מַעֲשֵׂיָהוּ בֶּן הַמֶּלֶךְ.** אָחָז, הָרַג **זִכְרִי גִּבּוֹר אֶפְרַיִם**:


**Maaseiah, the king’s son** [I.e., the son of] Ahaz. Zichri, a mighty man of Ephraim slew [him].


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 28:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:7:2)


**וְאֶת עַזְרִיקָם.** וְאֶת... וְאֶת, דֻּגְמָא: ״וְאַיָּה וַעֲנָה״ (בראשית לו:כד), וְכֵן ״נִרְדָּם וָרֶכֶב וָסוּס״ (תהלים עו:ז):


**and Azrikam** - Heb. וְאֶת וְאֶת, similar to (Gen. 36:24): “and Aiah and Anah (וְאַיָּה וְעַנָה),” and similarly, (Ps. 76:7): “and chariot and horse (וָרֶכֶב וָסוּס) were stunned.”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 28:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:15:1)


**וַיָּקֻמוּ הָאֲנָשִׁים אֲשֶׁר נִקְּבוּ בְשֵׁמוֹת.** לְמַעְלָה מִי הֵם? אוֹתָם אֲנָשִׁים שֶׁהֵם **מֵרָאשֵׁי בְנֵי אֶפְרַיִם**: עֲזַרְיָהוּ בֶּן יְהוֹחָנָן וַחֲבֵירָיו:


**And the men who were mentioned by name arose** above (verse 12). Who were they? Those men who were of the heads of the sons of Ephraim: Azariah the son of Jehohanan and his colleagues.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 28:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:15:2)


**וְכָל מַעֲרֻמֵּיהֶם.** לְשׁוֹן ״עֵרוֹם וְעֶרְיָה״ (יחזקאל טז:ז):


**and all their nakedness** - Heb. מַעֲרֻמֵּיהֶם, an expression of (Ezek. 15: “naked (עֵרם) and bare.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 28:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:18:1)


**וּפְלִשְׁתִּים פָּשְׁטוּ בְּעָרֵי הַשְּׁפֵלָה וְהַנֶּגֶב.** שֶׁהוּא כְּנֶגְדָּם, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וְהַכְּנַעֲנִי יוֹשֵׁב עַל הַיָּם״ (במדבר יג:כט):


**And Philistines raided the cities of the lowlands and the southland** which was opposite them, as it is written about the Philistines, (Zeph. 2:5): “the inhabitants of the seacoast.”



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 28:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:19:1)


**כִּי הִפְרִיעַ בִּיהוּדָה.** דֻּגְמָא: ״כִּי פְרָעֹה אַהֲרֹן״ (שמות לב:כה); ״בִּפְרֹעַ פְּרָעוֹת״ (שופטים ה:ב):


**for he exposed Judah** - Heb. הִפְרִיעַ, similar to (Exod. 32:25): “for Aaron had exposed them (פְּרָעֹה) (Jud. 5:2): “When breaches are made (בִּפְרֹעַ פְּרָעוֹת).”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 28:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:20:1)


**וַיָּבֹא עָלָיו.** שֶׁשָּׁלַח אַחֲרָיו לַעֲזֹר לוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בִּמְלָכִים (מלכים ב טז:ז) ״וַיִּשְׁלַח מַלְאָכִים אֶל תִּגְלַת פִּלְנְאֶסֶר״:


**came upon him** for he had sent after him to aid him, as is written in II Kings (16:7): “And Ahaz sent messengers to Tiglath-Pilneser.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 28:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:20:2)


**וַיָּצַר לוֹ.** מִמַּה שֶׁחָלַק לוֹ **אֶת בֵּית ה׳ וְאֶת בֵּית הַמֶּלֶךְ וְגוֹ׳**:


**and oppressed him** from that which he shared with him [from] the House of the Lord and the palace of the king, etc.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 28:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:21:1)


**וְלֹא לְעֶזְרָה לוֹ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמָּצִינוּ בִּמְלָכִים ״וַיַּעַל מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר עַל דַּמֶּשֶׂק וַיִּתְפְּשֶׂהָ וַיַּגְלֶהָ קִירָה וְאֶת רְצִין הֵמִית״ (מלכים ב טז:ט), לֹא הָיָה לוֹ לְעֶזְרָה כָּל כָּךְ לְחַזְּקוֹ וּלְהָשִׁיב לוֹ עָרִים שֶׁלָּקְחוּ מִמֶּנּוּ פְּלִשְׁתִּים:


**but he was of no help to him** Even though we find in II Kings (ibid. verse 9): “and the king of Assyria went up to Damascus and seized it and exiled it[s inhabitants] to Kir, and slew Rezin,” he was not much of an aid to him to strengthen him and to restore the cities that the Philistines had taken from him.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 28:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/28:24:1)


**וַיִּסְגֹּר אֶת דַּלְתוֹת בֵּית ה׳.** כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יָבוֹאוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים לַעֲבֹד עֲבוֹדָתָם בְּאֹהֶל מוֹעֵד, כִּי אִם לָבוֹא לַ**מִּזְבְּחוֹת** שֶׁעָשָׂה **בְּכָל פִּנָּה בִּירוּשָׁלִַם**; וְחִזְקִיָּה **פָּתַח... דַּלְתוֹת בֵּית ה׳** – לְפִי שֶׁאָבִיו סָגַר **דַּלְתוֹת בֵּית ה׳**:


**and he closed the doors of the House of the Lord** so that the priests would not go to perform their service in the Tent of Meeting, but come to the altars that he erected on every corner in Jerusalem, and Hezekiah “opened the doors of the House of the Lord,” (below 29:3) because his father had closed the doors of the House of the Lord.



## Chapter 29


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:3:1)


**וַיְחַזְּקֵם.** לְפִי שֶׁאָחָז קִצֵּץ אֶת כָּל **כְּלֵי בֵית הָאֱלֹהִים**:


**and reinforced them** because Ahaz had destroyed all the vessels of the House of God.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:5:1)


**וְהוֹצִיאוּ אֶת הַנִּדָּה.** עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים, אֶת הַמֵּת לֹא נֶאֱמַר כָּאן, כִּי אִם נִדָּה, וּמָאוּס יוֹתֵר מִן הַמֵּת הַמֻּטָּל שָׁלֵם; דֻּגְמָא: ״כְּטֻמְאַת הַנִּדָּה״ (יחזקאל לו:יז) – כִּי דַּם נִדָּה מָאוּס וּמְסֹרָח:


**and remove the ‘menstruant’** The *Midrash Aggadah* (source unknown) states: ‘The corpse’ is not stated here, but ‘the menstruant,’ which is more repugnant than the corpse. It is compared to the uncleanness of the menstruant, because menstrual blood is repugnant.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:7:1)


**וַיְכַבּוּ אֶת הַנֵּרוֹת וּקְטֹרֶת לֹא הִקְטִירוּ וְעֹלָה לֹא הֶעֱלוּ בַקֹּדֶשׁ לֵאלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** אֲבָל לֵאלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים עָשׂוּ בָמוֹת לְכָל עִיר וָעִיר לְקַטֵּר לָהֶם (דברי הימים ב כח:כה):


**extinguished the lamps, and they did not burn incense nor offer up burnt offerings in the Sanctuary to the God of Israel** but they made high places to alien gods in every city, to burn incense to them.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:11:1)


**אַל תִּשָּׁלוּ.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁגָגָה:


**do not forget** - Heb. תִּשָּׁלוּ, an expression denoting lack of intention.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:16:1)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים לִפְנִימָה.** כִּי לֹא עַל הַלְוִיִּם לִיכָּנֵס פְּנִימָה:


**And the priests came within** for the Levites may not enter within.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:16:2)


**לַחֲצַר בֵּית ה׳.** מְקוֹם מַעֲמַד הַלְוִיִּם, כְּאוֹמְרוֹ: **וַיְקַבְּלוּ הַלְוִיִּם וְגוֹ׳ וַיָּחֵלּוּ בְּאֶחָד לַחֹדֶשׁ**:


**to the court of the House of the Lord** the place where the Levites stood, as he says: “and the Levites received, etc.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:17:1)


**וַיְקַדְּשׁוּ אֶת בֵּית ה׳ לְיָמִים שְׁמוֹנָה.** לְכָךְ עִכְּבוּ בַּטָּהֳרָה לְטַהֵר כָּל כָּךְ, לְפִי שֶׁאָחָז צִיֵּר כָּל הַכְּתָלִים צַלְמֵי מַשְׂכִּית, כְּדִכְתִיב: ״צַלְמֵי כַשְׂדִּים חֲקוּקִים בַּשָּׁשַׁר״ (יחזקאל כג:יד), וְעִכְּבוּ שְׁמוֹנָה יָמִים עַד שֶׁהִשְׁחִיתוּ דְּמוּת הַצְּלָמִים מִן הַכְּתָלִים, **וּבְיוֹם שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר לַחֹדֶשׁ כִּלּוּ** הַכֹּל, הַבַּיִת וְהָעֲזָרָה:


**They commenced on the first of the first month etc., they sanctified the House of the Lord for eight days** This is why they prolonged the cleansing to cleanse it so long, because Ahaz had painted all the walls with images, as it is written (Ezek. 23:14): “Images of Chaldeans, engraved with types of drawings,” and they prolonged it for eight days until they destroyed the likeness of the images from the walls, and on the sixteenth day of the month, they completed everything, the Temple and the forecourt.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:19:1)


**הֵכַנּוּ וְהִקְדָּשְׁנוּ.** מְפֹרָשׁ בַּעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה בְּפֶרֶק רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל (עבודה זרה נב:): כֵּלִים שֶׁשִּׁמְּשׁוּ בְּבֵית חוֹנְיוֹ מַהוּ וְכוּ׳, עַד אֵיתִיבֵיהּ: אֲשֶׁר הִזְנִיחַ אָחָז הֵכַנּוּ וְהִקְדָּשְׁנוּ; מַאי לָאו הֵכַנּוּ – דְּאַטְבְּלִינְהוּ; הִקְדַּשְׁנוּ – דְּאַקְדְּשִׁינְהוּ. אָמַר לוֹ: בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה לַשָּׁמַיִם שֶׁהֶחֱזַרְתָּ לִי אֲבֵדָתִי: הֵכַנּוּ – שֶׁגְּנָזוּם, וְהִקְדָּשְׁנוּ – שֶׁהִקְדַּשְׁנוּ אֲחֵרִים תַּחְתֵּיהֶם:


**we have prepared and sanctified** This is explained in *Abodah Zarah* (52b) in the chapter entitled “Rabbi Ishmael”: May vessels used in the Onias temple be used in the Temple?...He questioned him, “’That King Ahaz forsook, etc....we have prepared and sanctified.’ Does not ‘we have prepared’ mean that they immersed them and ‘sanctified’ mean that they consecrated them?” He replied, “Blessed are you to Heaven, for you have restored my loss to me. ‘We have prepared’ means that we have concealed them and ‘sanctified’ means that we consecrated others in their stead.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:21:1)


**וּצְפִירֵי עִזִּים וְגוֹ׳.** הוֹרָאַת שָׁעָה הָיְתָה:


**and seven kids, etc.** This was a temporary provision.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:21:2)


**עַל מִזְבַּח ה׳.** וְלֹא עַל אוֹתָן מִזְבְּחוֹת שֶׁעָשָׂה אָחָז אָבִיו (דברי הימים ב כח:כה):


**on the altar of the Lord** but not on those altars that his father Ahaz had made.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:24:1)


**לְכַפֵּר עַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל.** שֶׁחָטְאוּ בַּעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים; כֵּן **אָמַר הַמֶּלֶךְ**: תְּהִי **הָעוֹלָה וְהַחַטָּאת** עֲבוּר כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל. בְּפָרָשַׁת שְׁלַח לְךָ כְּתִיב ״וְעָשׂוּ כָל הָעֵדָה פַּר וְגוֹ׳״ (במדבר טו:כד), וּכְתִיב ״וְהִקְרִיבוּ הַקָּהָל פַּר בֶּן בָּקָר לְחַטָּאת״ (ויקרא ד:יד), וְכָאן כְּתִיב **וַיָּבִיאוּ פָּרִים... וְאֵילִים... וּצְפִירֵי עִזִּים שִׁבְעָה** אֶלָּא הוֹרָאַת שָׁעָה הָיְתָה (ירושלמי הוריות א:ח):


**to atone for all Israel** for they had sinned by [practicing] idolatry. The king said as follows: “Let the burnt offerings and the sin offerings be for all Israel.” In the section שְׁלַח (Num. 16:24) it is written: “the entire congregation shall offer up one bull, etc.,” and it is written: (Lev 4:14): “and the congregation shall bring a young bull for a sin offering,” and here it is written that they brought seven bulls, rams, lambs, and kids! This was a temporary ruling.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:25:1)


**כִּי בְיַד ה׳ הַמִּצְוָה בְּיַד נְבִיאָיו.** לְשׁוֹרֵר הַלְוִיִּם בִּכְלֵי שִׁיר; לֹא כָתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה שֶׁיְּהֵא קָרְבָּן טָעוּן שִׁירָה, אֶלָּא מִצְוַת ה׳ שֶׁצִּוָּה בְּיַד נְבִיאָיו לְשׁוֹרֵר עַל הַקָּרְבָּן. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת עֲרָכִין (יא.): מִנַּיִן שֶׁהַלְוִיִּם טְעוּנִין לְשׁוֹרֵר בִּכְלֵי שִׁיר, דִּכְתִיב ״עֲבוֹדַת עֲבוֹדָה״ (במדבר ד:מז) – אֵיזוֹהִי עֲבוֹדָה שֶׁצְּרִיכָה עֲבוֹדָה אַחֶרֶת עִמָּהּ? הֱוֵי אוֹמֵר: זֶה הַשִּׁיר בִּכְלֵי שִׁיר. ״וַעֲבוֹדַת מַשָּׂא״ (שם) – נָכוֹן לְפָרֵשׁ עַל הַשִּׁיר, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וּכְנַנְיָהוּ שַׂר הַלְוִיִּם בְּמַשָּׂא... כִּי מֵבִין הוּא״ (דברי הימים א טו:כב), שֶׁהָיָה מְמֻנֶּה לְהָרִים קוֹל הַנִּגּוּן שֶׁל שִׁיר:


**for by the hand of the Lord was the commandment, by the hand of His prophets** that the Levites should play musical instruments. It is not written in the Torah that a sacrifice requires musical accompaniment (lit., song), but it is the commandment of the Lord, which He commanded through His prophets to accompany the sacrifice with song. Our Rabbis explained in Tractate *Arachin* (11a): From where do we derive that the Levites are required to play musical instruments? For it is written (Num. 4:47): “the service of the service.” Which is a service that requires another service with it? This is music with a musical instrument. “And the service of raising” may also be correctly explained in reference to song, as it is written (I Chron. 15:22): “And Chenaniah, the leader of the Levites in song (בְּמַשָּׂא) etc. because he was an expert,” for he was appointed to raise the voice of the melody of the song.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:26:1)


**וְהַכֹּהֲנִים בַּחֲצֹצְרוֹת.** כְּכָתוּב ״וּבְנֵי אַהֲרֹן הַכֹּהֲנִים יִתְקְעוּ בַּחֲצוֹצְרוֹת״ (במדבר י:ח):


**and the priests with the trumpets** as it is written (Num. 10:8): “And Aaron’s sons, the priests, shall blow the trumpets.”



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:30:1)


**לְהַלֵּל לַה׳ בְּדִבְרֵי דָוִד.** זֶה ״הוֹדוּ לַה׳ קִרְאוּ בִשְׁמוֹ״ (דברי הימים א טז:ח), כַּכָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה ״בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא אָז נָתַן דָּוִד בָּרֹאשׁ לְהוֹדוֹת וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א טז:ז):


**to praise the Lord with the words of David and Asaph the seer** i.e., “Give thanks to the Lord, call out in His name,” as is written above (I Chron. 16:8f): “On that day, David placed Nathan at the head to give thanks to the Lord by the hand of Asaph and his brethren, etc.”



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:31:1)


**וַיָּבִיאוּ הַקָּהָל זְבָחִים וְתוֹדוֹת.** דָּבָר הַמֻּתָּר לַבְּעָלִים בַּאֲכִילָה:


**and the congregation brought [peace] offerings and thanksgiving offerings** which are permissible for the owner to eat.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:31:2)


**וְכָל נְדִיב לֵב עֹלוֹת.** קְצָת מֵהֶם, מִי שֶׁהִתְנַדֵּב לִבּוֹ הֵבִיאוּ עֹלוֹת, וְלֹא כֻלָּם, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין לַבְּעָלִים הֶתֵּר אֲכִילָה בָּהֶם:


**and every generous-hearted one - burnt offerings** Some of them, whoever was moved by his heart, brought burnt-offerings, but not all of them [i.e., not all the people brought burnt-offerings], because the owner has no permission to eat them.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:33:1)


**וְהַקֳּדָשִׁים.** קָדָשִׁים קַלִּים: זְבָחִים וּשְׁלָמִים, מַה שֶּׁהַבְּעָלִים נֶהֱנִים בָּהֶם:


**And the sacred offerings** sacred offerings of the lower degree - [thanksgiving-] offerings and peace-offerings, from which the owners may benefit.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:34:1)


**רַק הַכֹּהֲנִים הָיוּ לִמְעָט.** כְּלוֹמַר: אוֹתָן שֶׁהִקְדִּישׁוּ הָיוּ עֲדַיִן לִמְעָט, **וַיְּחַזְּקוּם אֲחֵיהֶם הַלְוִיִּם עַד כְּלוֹת הַמְּלָאכָה וְעַד יִתְקַדְּשׁוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים**. וְאִם תֹּאמַר: הֵיאַךְ יִתְקַדְּשׁוּ הַלְוִיִּם וְלֹא יִתְקַדְּשׁוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים?! וּמְפָרֵשׁ, לְפִי **כִּי הַלְוִיִּם יִשְׁרֵי לֵבָב לְהִתְקַדֵּשׁ מֵהַכֹּהֲנִים**, וְיָשְׁרוּ לִבָּם הַלְוִיִּם לְהִתְקַדֵּשׁ יוֹתֵר מֵהַכֹּהֲנִים. וְהָיוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם וְכָל יִרְאֵי הַשֵּׁם מִתְחַפְּשִׂים וּמִתְנַכְּרִים וְאַף מִסְתַּתְּרִים כָּל יְמֵי הַמְּלָכִים הָרְשָׁעִים, וּכְשֶׁבָּא חִזְקִיָּהוּ שֶׁהָיָה צַדִּיק לֹא הָיוּ יְכוֹלִים מִיָּד לְהִתְקַדֵּשׁ וּלְטַהֵר, שֶׁזֶּה בַּשָּׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה הָיָה לְמָלְכוֹ, וְהָיוּ בָּאִים וּמִתְקַדְּשִׁים עַל יָד; כְּדִכְתִיב ״בַּעֲלֹץ צַדִּיקִים רַבָּה תִפְאָרֶת וּבְקוּם רְשָׁעִים יְחֻפַּשׂ אָדָם״ (משלי כח:יב), וּכְתִיב ״בְּקוּם רְשָׁעִים יִסָּתֵר אָדָם וּבְאָבְדָם יִרְבּוּ צַדִּיקִים״ (משלי כח:כח) – כְּלוֹמַר: בְּקוּם רָשָׁע, אָחָז, יִסָּתֵר אָדָם, וּבְאָבְדָם יִרְבּוּ צַדִּיקִים, חִזְקִיָּה וְסִיעָתוֹ:


**Only the priests were few** i.e., Those who had consecrated themselves were still few; so their kinsmen, the Levites, reinforced them until the work was completed and until the priests consecrated themselves. Now, should you ask: How was it that the Levites consecrated themselves and the priests did not consecrate themselves? Scripture explains: “for the Levites were more conscientious than the priests to consecrate themselves”; the Levites were more conscientious than the priests, for the priests and the Levites and all God-fearing people had disguised themselves and made themselves appear unrecognizable, and had even hidden, all the days of the wicked kings, and when Hezekiah - who was righteous - came, they could not immediately consecrate themselves and cleanse themselves, for this was in the first year of his reign; so they came and consecrated themselves gradually, as it is written (Prov. 28:12): “When the righteous rejoice, there is much glory, but when the wicked rise up, a person disguises himself,” and it is written (ibid. verse 28): “When the wicked rise up, people hide; but when they perish, the righteous increase.” When the wicked Ahaz rose up, people hid, but when he perished, the righteous increased, i.e., Hezekiah and his company.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 29:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/29:36:1)


**עַל הַהֵכִין הָאֱלֹהִים לָעָם.** לְעָבְדוֹ וְלַעֲשׂוֹת רְצוֹנוֹ, דּוּגְמָא דִּלְמַטָּה: ״כָּל לְבָבוֹ הֵכִין לִדְרוֹשׁ וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב ל:יט). וְגַם שָׂמְחוּ, כִּי **בְּפִתְאֹם הָיָה הַדָּבָר** עֲלֵיהֶם בְּשָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה לְמָלְכוֹ, לָשׁוּב אֶל ה׳, וְשָׁבוּ מִיָּד:


**over that which God had prepared for the people** to serve Him and to do His will, similar to below (30:19): “His whole heart he set to seek, etc.,” and they also rejoiced because they were suddenly inspired in the first year of his reign to return to the Lord, and they returned immediately.



## Chapter 30


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:1:1)


**וְגַם אִגְּרוֹת כָּתַב עַל אֶפְרַיִם וּמְנַשֶּׁה וְגוֹ׳.** כִּי עֲדַיִן לֹא גָלוּ, כִּי זֶה הָיָה בְּשָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה לְמָלְכוֹ, וְהֵם לֹא גָלוּ כִּי אִם בְּשֵׁשׁ לְמָלְכוֹ, כְּדִכְתִיב ״וַיִּלְכְּדֻהָ בִּקְצֵה שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים בִּשְׁנַת שֵׁשׁ לְחִזְקִיָּה״ וְ״הִיא שְׁנַת תֵּשַׁע לְהוֹשֵׁעַ מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל״ (מלכים ב יח:י), כַּאֲשֶׁר תִּמְצָא כָתוּב בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר:


**and he also wrote letters to Ephraim and Manasseh, etc.** because they were not yet exiled, because this was in the first year of his reign and they were not exiled until the sixth year of his reign as it is written (II Kings 18:10): “And they captured it at the end of three years, in the sixth year of Hezekiah, which was the ninth year of Hoshea, king of Israel,” as you will find at the end of the Book.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:3:1)


**כִּי לֹא יָכְלוּ לַעֲשֹׂתוֹ בָּעֵת הַהִיא.** בַּחֹדֶשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹן בְּנִיסָן:


**For they could not perform it at that time** in the first month, in Nissan.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:3:2)


**כִּי הַכֹּהֲנִים לֹא הִתְקַדְּשׁוּ לְמַדָּי.** הִתְקַדְּשׁוּ לִהְיוֹת דַּי לְיִשְׂרָאֵל בָּהֶם לְהַקְרִיב פִּסְחֵיהֶם; וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ יְכוֹלִים לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ בְּנִיסָן, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא כָּלוּ לְקַדֵּשׁ אֶת הַבַּיִת עַד שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן (דברי הימים ב כט:יז). וְכֵן פִּתְרוֹן **כִּי לֹא יָכְלוּ לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ בָּעֵת הַהִיא** – כְּלוֹמַר: אֲפִילּוּ אִם לֹא עִכְּבוּ טָהֳרַת הַבַּיִת, שֶׁלֹּא כָּלוּ עַד שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן, לֹא הָיִינוּ יְכוֹלִין לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ עַתָּה, לְפִי כִּי הַכֹּהֲנִים לֹא הִתְקַדְּשׁוּ לְמַדָּי:


**for the priests had not consecrated themselves sufficiently** They had not consecrated themselves sufficiently for Israel to offer their Passover sacrifices, and it goes without saying that they could not perform it in Nissan, for they did not finish consecrating the Temple until the sixteenth of Nissan, but this is its meaning: For they could not perform it at that time; i.e., even if they had not delayed the purification of the Temple, which they did not complete until the sixteenth of Nissan, they would not have been able to perform it now [the offering of the Passover sacrifice] because not enough priests had consecrated themselves.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:3:3)


**וְהָעָם לֹא נֶאֶסְפוּ.** עֲדַיִן כֻּלָּם **לִירוּשָׁלָיִם**:


**and the people had not gathered** all of them [had not] yet [gathered] to Jerusalem.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:5:1)


**מִבְּאֵר שֶׁבַע וְעַד דָּן.** זֶהוּ תְּחוּם עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים:


**from Beersheba to Dan** This is the boundary of the Ten Tribes.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:5:2)


**כִּי לֹא לָרֹב.** פִּתְרוֹן: כִּי רֹב שָׁנִים עָבְרוּ שֶׁלֹּא עָשׂוּהוּ כְּהִלְכָתוֹ:


**because for many years** The meaning is that many years had passed in which they had not performed it according to its law.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:6:1)


**וְיָשֹׁב אֶל הַפְּלֵיטָה הַנִּשְׁאֶרֶת לָכֶם מִכַּף מַלְכֵי אַשּׁוּר.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הָגְלוּ עֲדַיִן, קוֹרֵא לָהֶם פְּלֵיטָה, כִּי הָיָה נֶחְשָׁב בְּעֵינֵיהֶם כַּאֲשֶׁר גָּלוּ כְּבָר; וְעוֹד, כִּי מַלְכֵי אַשּׁוּר תָּפְשׂוּ הַכֹּל בִּימֵי אָחָז. וּמַה שֶּׁהָיָה בְּעֵינֵיהֶם כְּאִלּוּ גָּלוּ, לְפִי כִּי שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים הָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר עֲבָדִים נוֹשְׂאֵי מִנְחָה, וְהָיָה מוֹשֵׁל בָּהֶם וּמוֹלִיכָן בַּגּוֹלָה וְעוֹשֶׂה בָּהֶם כִּרְצוֹנוֹ, וְזֶה הָיָה בְּתוֹךְ אוֹתָן שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים; וּכְבָר גָּלוּ כַּמָּה פְּעָמִים בִּימֵי מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאָחָז, וְהֵם יוֹדְעִים: מִיָּד כְּשֶׁיִּמְרְדוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר – יוֹלִיכֵם בַּגּוֹלָה; וְלָהֶם קוֹרֵא פְּלֵיטַת אַשּׁוּר, לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא מָשַׁל בָּהֶם וְלֹא בְּכָל אֶרֶץ יְהוּדָה:


**so that He may return to the remnant that has escaped from the clutches of the kings of Assyria** Although they had not yet been exiled, he calls them a remnant that has escaped because they considered themselves as if they had already been exiled, and moreover, the kings of Assyria had seized everything in the days of Ahaz, and the reason they saw themselves as if they had already been exiled was that for six years the Israelites were tribute-paying serfs to the king of Assyria, and he ruled over them and led them away into exile and did with them as he wished. This occurred within those six years, and they had already been exiled many times in the days of the kings of Israel and Ahaz, and they knew that as soon as Israel would rebel against the king of Assyria, he would lead them away in exile. He calls them ‘those who escaped from Assyria’ because he ruled neither over them, nor over the entire land of Judah.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:7:1)


**וְאַל תִּהְיוּ כַּאֲבוֹתֵיכֶם וְכַאֲחֵיכֶם.** פִּתְרוֹן: וְאִם תֹּאמַר עֲדַיִן בּוֹטְחִים אָנוּ שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה מֵעָלֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר – אַל תִּהְיוּ כַּאֲבוֹתֵיכֶם, כָּל דּוֹר וָדוֹר שֶׁהָיָה לִפְנֵיכֶם; וּמָה אֵרַע לָהֶם כְּשֶׁעָזְבוּ אֶת ה׳ וַיִּמְעֲלוּ בּוֹ מַעַל? **וַיִּתְּנֵם לְשַׁמָּה כַּאֲשֶׁר אַתֶּם רֹאִים**:


**And do not be like your fathers or like your brothers** The meaning is: Now if you say, “We still trust that the king of Assyria will withdraw from us,” do not be like your fathers, every generation that was before you. What happened to them when they forsook the Lord and acted treacherously? “He made them a desolation, as you see.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:9:1)


**עַתָּה אַל תַּקְשׁוּ עָרְפְּכֶם וְגוֹ׳ בְּשׁוּבְכֶם עַל ה׳ אֲחֵיכֶם וּבְנֵיכֶם יִהְיוּ לְרַחֲמִים לִפְנֵי שׁוֹבֵיהֶם וְלָשׁוּב לָאָרֶץ הַזֹּאת.** אֲפִילּוּ אוֹתָן קְצָת שֶׁגָּלוּ כְּבָר, שֶׁהָיוּ נִלְחָמִים עִם יִשְׂרָאֵל וַיִּכְלוּ לָהֶם, וּמוֹלִיכִין אֶת בְּנֵיהֶם וְאֶת בְּנוֹתֵיהֶם בַּשִּׁבְיָה. וְאִם תָּשׁוּבוּ אֵלָיו, יִתְּנֵם לְרַחֲמִים לִפְנֵי שׁוֹבֵיהֶם לְשׁוּבָם לָאָרֶץ הַזֹּאת, **כִּי חַנּוּן וְרַחוּם ה׳ אֱלֹהֵיכֶם**:


**For when you return to the Lord, your brothers and your sons will find compassion before their captors and shall return to this land** Even those few who have already been exiled, for they waged war with Israel and they overwhelmed them, and they led their sons and daughters into captivity. But if you return to Him, He will grant them compassion before their captors, that they will return to this land, for the Lord your God is gracious and merciful.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:14:1)


**הַמְקַטְּרוֹת.** מָקוֹם שֶׁהָיוּ בּוֹ כֹּהֲנִים וּלְוִיִּם:


**the altars for incense** a place where there were priests and Levites.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:15:1)


**וְהַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם נִכְלְמוּ.** שֶׁעִכְּבוּ לְהִתְקַדֵּשׁ, וּלְפִיכָךְ עִכְּבוּ כָּל כָּךְ מִלָּבוֹא עַד עַתָּה; כִּי לֹא יוּכְלוּ לְהַאֲמִין בַּדָּבָר הַזֶּה קוֹדֶם לָכֵן, לְפִי שֶׁהִזְנִיחָן אָחָז מִכַּהֵן (דברי הימים ב יא:יד), וְכָךְ הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים: אֶפְשָׁר אֶתְמוֹל עָבַד אָחָז עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים, וּבְנוֹ יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ לְאַלְתַּר בְּשָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה מִיָּד בַּחֹדֶשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹן – וְיֹאמַר לַכֹּהֲנִים וְלַלְוִיִּם לַעֲבֹד לָאֵל אֶחָד לְבַדּוֹ, וְיִצְטָרֵךְ לָנוּ! וּלְכָךְ נִתְעַצְּלוּ וְנִתְרַשְּׁלוּ מִלָּבֹא. וְגַם שְׁאָר יְהוּדָה שֶׁלְּפָנָיו הִזְנִיחַ אָחָז, וּכְשֶׁחָקְרוּ וְהֵבִינוּ בַּדָּבָר שֶׁהַכֹּל הָיָה לְשֵׁם שָׁמַיִם, בָּאוּ **וַיִּתְקַדְּשׁוּ** כֻּלָּם:


**and the priests and the Levites were embarrassed** that they had delayed to consecrate themselves. They tarried so long and did not come until now for the following reason: they could not believe that this was true beforehand, because Ahaz had dismissed them from officiating as priests. And so they said, “Is it possible that yesterday Ahaz worshipped idols, and that his son Hezekiah, immediately in the first year and first month would tell the priests and Levites to worship the one God alone and have need of us?” They were therefore lazy and neglected to come, together with the rest of Judah, whom Ahaz had dismissed. But when they investigated and understood that everything was for the name of Heaven, they all came and consecrated themselves.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:16:1)


**הַכֹּהֲנִים זֹרְקִים.** כִּי מִקַּבָּלָה וְאֵילָךְ מִצְוַת כְּהֻנָּה. וְאַל תִּתְמַהּ לָמָּה לֹא שָׁחֲטוּ בְּעָלִים עַצְמָם, לְפִי שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּסָמוּךְ: **כִּי רַבַּת בַּקָּהָל אֲשֶׁר לֹא הִתְקַדָּשׁוּ; וְהַלְוִיִּם** הָיוּ **עַל שְׁחִיטַת** פִּסְחֵיהֶם:


**the priests sprinkled** For [the rituals] from the reception [of the blood] and onwards are the commandments of the priests, and do not wonder why the owners themselves did not slaughter [the sacrifices], as is explained further: **for there were many among the congregation who had not consecrated themselves** and the Levites presided over the slaughter of their Passover sacrifices.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:17:1)


**לְכֹל לֹא טָהוֹר.** שֶׁהָיָה לֵוִי בִּמְקוֹמוֹ לְהַקְדִּישׁ לַה׳:


**for everyone who was unclean** a Levite took his place to sanctify it for the Lord.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:18:1)


**כִּי אָכְלוּ אֶת הַפֶּסַח בְּלֹא כַכָּתוּב.** שֶׁלֹּא כַּהֲלָכָה, שֶׁאֲכָלוּהוּ בְּטֻמְאָה, **כִּי הִתְפַּלֵּל יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ עֲלֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳**:


**for they ate the Passover sacrifice not as it is written** not in accordance with halachah, for they ate it in a state of uncleanness, for Hezekiah prayed for them, saying, etc.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:19:1)


**כָּל לְבָבוֹ הֵכִין.** כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בִּבְרַיְתָא דְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בְּנוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי הַמַּתְחֶלֶת: בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁאַתָּה מוֹצֵא דִּבְרֵי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בְּנוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי בָּאַגָּדָה עֲשֵׂה אָזְנְךָ כַּאֲפַרְכֶּסֶת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״לְהַקְשִׁיב אָזְנְךָ לַתְּבוּנָה״ (משלי ב:ב); מִשְּׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם מִדּוֹת הָאַגָּדָה נִדְרָשׁ וְזֶה אֶחָד מֵהֶם: נֶחְלָק הַסֵּדֶר, כְּגוֹן זֶה: **ה׳ הַטּוֹב יְכַפֵּר בְּעַד** כָּל מִי שֶׁכָּל לְבָבוֹ הֵכִין **לִדְרוֹשׁ הָאֱלֹהִים** – לַעֲשׂוֹת פִּסְחוֹ, לְהַקְרִיב קָרְבָּנוֹ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁ**לֹּא** עָשׂוּ בְּ**טָהֳרַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ**, כִּי לֹא טָהֵרוּ. וִיהוּדָה כֻּלָּם נִטְהֲרוּ, לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ קְרוֹבִים לִירוּשָׁלַיִם וְהָיוּ יְכוֹלִים לְטַהֵר וּלְהִתְקַדֵּשׁ וְלָשׁוּב לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, אֲבָל **רַבַּת מֵאֶפְרַיִם וּמְנַשֶּׁה יִשָּׂשכָר זְבוּלֻן** אֵין לָהֶם כָּל כָּךְ פֶּשַׁע בַּדָּבָר, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה לָהֶם הַדָּבָר רָחוֹק עַל יְדֵי אִגְּרוֹת, לֹא הָיָה לָהֶם לְהַמְתִּין וּלְאַחֵר מִלָּבוֹא עַד שֶׁיִּתְקַדְּשׁוּ, לְפִיכָךְ בָּאוּ וְלֹא נִטְהֲרוּ; וּלְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה פְּשִׁיעָתָם, לְפִיכָךְ תְּכַפֵּר בַּעֲדָם. וְכֵן ״יְשֻׂשׂוּם מִדְבָּר וְצִיָּה וְגוֹמֵר״ (ישעיה לה:א) הוּא נֶחְלָק הַסִּדּוּר:


**who has set his whole heart** So it is explained in the Baraitha of Rabbi Eleazar the son of Rabbi Jose the Galilean which commences: Wherever you find the words of Rabbi Eleazar the son of Rabbi Jose the Galilean in Aggadah, listen attentively, as it is said (Prov. 2:2): “To make your ear attentive to wisdom” of the thirty-two methods by which the Aggadah is expounded, and this is one of them:. If the sequence was separated, e.g. this one: The good Lord will atone for anyone who has set his whole heart to seek God to perform His Passover sacrifice, to offer up his sacrifice—even though they did not offer it with the purity of sacred things, for they had not purified themselves; the entire Judah had purified themselves because they were near Jerusalem, and they could cleanse themselves and consecrate themselves and return to Jerusalem, but many from Ephraim and Manasseh, Issachar and Zebulun were not so much to blame in the matter, because the matter was far away for them through letters, and they did not have time to wait and to delay their coming until they consecrated themselves. They therefore came and were not purified. And because it was not their fault, therefore atone for them. Similarly, (Isa. 35:1f.): “Desert and wasteland shall rejoice over them, etc.”: the sequence is separated.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:21:1)


**וְהַכֹּהֲנִים בִּכְלֵי עֹז.** בַּחֲצוֹצְרוֹת:


**and the priests with powerful instruments** with trumpets.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:22:1)


**הַמַּשְׂכִּילִים שֵׂכֶל טוֹב לַה׳.** לְעִנְיַן שִׁירָה נוֹפֵל לוֹמַר לְשׁוֹן שֵׂכֶל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיִּפֹּל הַגּוֹרָל מִזְרָחָה לְשֶׁלֶמְיָהוּ וּזְכַרְיָהוּ בְנוֹ יוֹעֵץ בְּשֵׂכֶל וְגוֹמֵר״ (דברי הימים א כו:יד). וְדִבֵּר חִזְקִיָּהוּ עַל לִבָּם תַּנְחוּמִים וּדְבָרִים טוֹבִים, לְחַזְּקָם וְלַעֲזֹר לָהֶם כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּחַזְּקוּ אַף הֵם בְּשִׁיר שֵׂכֶל טוֹב לַה׳:


**who were experts in the service of the Lord** it is appropriate to use the term “expertise” (שֵׂכֶל) in regards to song, as it is written, (above I Chron. 26:14): “And the lot to the east fell to Shelemiah, and Zechariah, his son, a prudent counselor, etc.,” (יוֹעֵץבְּשֵׂכֶל). Hezekiah spoke to their hearts with consolations and pleasant words to encourage them and assist them, in order that they too strengthen the song with expertise for the Lord.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:22:2)


**מִתְוַדִּים לַה׳.** עַל זֶבַח תּוֹדָה, וּמוֹדִים לַה׳ עַל הַנֵּס שֶׁעָשָׂה לָהֶם, שֶׁזֶּבַח תּוֹדָה עַל נֵס הוּא בָּא:


**and thanking the Lord** over the thanksgiving offerings, and they thanked the Lord for the miracle that He had wrought for them, for the thanksgiving offering comes for a miracle.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:23:1)


**שִׁבְעַת יָמִים אֲחֵרִים.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ: **וַיַּעֲשׂוּ שִׁבְעַת יָמִים שִׂמְחָה**:


**another seven days** as it is explained: and they kept seven days of joy.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:24:1)


**הֵרִים לַקָּהָל.** מִצֹּאנוֹ וּמִבְּקָרוֹ:


**separated** from his small cattle and from his large cattle.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:24:2)


**לַקָּהָל אֶלֶף פָּרִים וְגוֹ׳.** וְהָיוּ אוֹכְלִים בְּיַחַד, מֶלֶךְ וְשָׂרִים, וְהָעָם שְׂמֵחִים:


**for the congregation one thousand bulls, etc.** and they were eating together, the king, the officers, and the people, with joy.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:26:1)


**כִּי מִימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה בֶן דָּוִד מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לֹא** הָיְתָה **כָזֹאת בִּירוּשָׁלִָם**. שֶׁמָּא עַם רַב יוֹתֵר הָיוּ בָּאִין לָרֶגֶל לִירוּשָׁלַיִם בִּימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה, אֲבָל **שִׂמְחָה גְדוֹלָה** לֹא הָיְתָה **בִּירוּשָׁלִָם כָזֹאת מִימוֹת שְׁלֹמֹה**, כִּי בְּיוֹתֵר שָׂמְחוּ עַל מַה שֶׁאִם הָיוּ כָּל רֶגֶל וָרֶגֶל מְקֻבָּצִים בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לָהֶם חִדּוּשׁ אֵין לָהֶם שִׂמְחָה, אֲבָל עַתָּה, שֶׁכָּל יְמֵי אָחָז וּמַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה הָרְשָׁעִים לֹא עָלוּ לָרֶגֶל לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, וְעַתָּה הוּרְשׁוּ לַעֲלוֹת לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, שָׂמְחוּ **שִׂמְחָה גְדוֹלָה**. וּלְפִי שֶׁהָיָה כַּמָּה שָׁנִים שֶׁלֹּא עָלוּ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם עַד עַתָּה, הוּקְטַן בְּעֵינֵיהֶם שִׁבְעָה יָמִים דְּחַג הַמַּצּוֹת, וְהוֹסִיפוּ עוֹד שִׁבְעָה יָמִים כְּדֵי לִשְׂמֹחַ בָּהֶם, וְשָׂמְחוּ **שִׂמְחָה גְדוֹלָה**:


**for since the days of Solomon the son of David, king of Israel, there had not been the like in Jerusalem** It is possible that a greater multitude had come for the festival to Jerusalem in the days of Solomon, but there had never been such a great rejoicing as this in Jerusalem since the days of Solomon. They were joyful about this [gathering], for if they had gathered in Jerusalem on every festival, it would have been nothing new and they would have had no cause for [special] joy But now, since they had not performed the festival pilgrimage to Jerusalem all the days of Ahaz and the wicked kings of Judah, and they were now permitted to go up to Jerusalem they rejoiced greatly. And since it was so many years since they had gone to Jerusalem, the seven days of the Festival of Matzoth were too few in their eyes and they added another seven days in which to rejoice, and they rejoiced a great rejoicing.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 30:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/30:27:1)


**וַיְבָרֲכוּ אֶת הָעָם.** אִם תִּרְצֶה לוֹמַר: בֵּרְכָם בָּזֶה שֶׁבִּקְשׁוּ לָשׁוּב מֵאֶרֶץ שׁוֹבֵיהֶם, **וַיִּשָּׁמַע** ה׳ **בְּקוֹלָם** וַיְשִׁיבֵם אָז. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **וַיִּשָּׁמַע בְּקוֹלָם.** וְכֵן כָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה **וַיִּשְׁמַע ה׳ אֶל יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ וַיִּרְפָּא אֶת הָעָם**:
