**and they blessed the people** If you wish, you may say that they blessed them with this [blessing] that they begged to return from the land of their captors, and the Lord hearkened to their voice and returned them. Another possible explanation: and He hearkened to their voice, and so it is written above (v. 20): “and the Lord hearkened to Hezekiah and healed the people.”



## Chapter 31


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:2:1)


**אִישׁ כְּפִי עֲבוֹדָתוֹ.** **הַכֹּהֲנִים לְשָׁרֵת, וְהַלְוִיִּם** לִהְיוֹת מְשׁוֹרְרִים וְשׁוֹעֲרִים, וְכָל אִישׁ וָאִישׁ **עַל עֲבוֹדָתוֹ** הַנְּכוֹנָה:


**each man according to his service** the priests to minister, and the Levites to be singers and gate sentries, and every man at his proper service.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:3:1)


**וּמְנַת הַמֶּלֶךְ מִן רְכוּשׁוֹ לָעֹלוֹת וְגוֹ׳.** אֲשֶׁר נָתַן לַעֲשׂוֹת אוֹתָם (בְּרָָצוֹן):


**And the king’s portion from his possessions for the burnt-offerings, etc.** which he (willingly) gave to perform them.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:4:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר לָעָם לְיוֹשְׁבֵי יְרוּשָׁלִַם וְגוֹ׳ לְמַעַן יֶחֶזְקוּ בְּתוֹרַת ה׳.** יוֹתֵר בְּרָצוֹן; וְעוֹד, בְּעוֹד שֶׁיְּהֵא לָהֶם פַּרְנָסָתָם מְזֻמֶּנֶת, יוּכְלוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת וְלַעֲסֹק בְּתוֹרַת ה׳; לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא נִתְּנָה הַתּוֹרָה אֶלָּא לְאוֹכְלֵי הַמָּן (מכילתא בשלח ויהי פתיחתא), וּשְׁנִיִּים לָהֶם אוֹכְלֵי תְּרוּמָה:


**And he commanded the inhabitants of Jerusalem, etc. in order that they be strengthened in the Law of the Lord** with greater enthusiasm. Moreover, as long as their sustenance will be provided, they will be able to perform and occupy themselves with the Law of the Lord, because the Law was given only to those who ate manna, and secondly, to those who eat the heave-offering (*Mechilta, Beshallach* 17, *Tanh. Beshallach 20).*



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:5:1)


**וְכִפְרֹץ הַדָּבָר.** מִפִּי הַמֶּלֶךְ, **לָתֵת מְנָת** לַכֹּהֲנִים **וְהַלְוִיִּם, הִרְבּוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל רֵאשִׁית דָּגָן תִּירוֹשׁ וְיִצְהָר וּדְבַשׁ**, דְּבַשׁ תְּמָרִים:


**And when the word burst forth** from the king’s mouth to give a portion to the priests and the Levites, **the Children of Israel increased the first of corn, wine, oil, honey** date honey.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:5:2)


**וְכָל תְּבוּאַת שָׂדֶה.** וְכָל אֵלּוּ לֹא מִתְּבוּאַת הַשָּׂדֶה הֵם, אֶלָּא כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת נְדָרִים בְּפֶרֶק הַנּוֹדֵר מִן הַיָּרָק (נדרים נד.): הַנּוֹדֵר מִן הַיָּרָק מֻתָּר בִּדְלוּעִים; מְתִיב רַב יוֹסֵף: **וְכִפְרֹץ הַדָּבָר הִרְבּוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ׳**, וְאִי אָמְרַתְּ דָּגָן – כָּל דְּמִדְגַּן מַשְׁמַע, מַאי **וְכִפְרֹץ וְגוֹמֵר**? אָמַר אַבַּיֵּי: לְאָתוֹיֵי פֵּירוֹת הָאִילָן וְיָרָק, עַד אָמַר רָבָא: תְּבוּאָה לְחוּד וּתְבוּאַת שָׂדֶה לְחוּד:


**and all the increase of the field** But all these [aforementioned] are not of the grain of the field, but so it is explained in Tractate *Nedarim*, in the chapter, “Whoever vows [to abstain] from herbs, is permitted to partake of pumpkins,” (55a): Rav Josef questioned: “And when the word burst forth, the Children of Israel increased, etc.” Now if you say that all these [i.e., all plants that are stacked up] are included in the term דָּגָן, what is the meaning of “when the word burst forth”? Replied Abbaye: This is to include fruit of trees and vegetables....Replied Rava: תְּבוּאָה (grain) is one thing, and תְּבוּאַת שָׂדֶה (increase of the field) is another.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:6:1)


**וּמַעְשַׂר קָדָשִׁים הַמְקֻדָּשִׁים לַה׳.** הַמַּעֲשֵׂר מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר שֶׁהַלְוִיִּם נוֹתְנִים לַכֹּהֲנִים (במדבר יח:כה-כו):


**and the tithes of sacred things consecrated to the Lord** the tithe of the tithe, which the Levites give the priests (Num. 18:25f.).


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:6:2)


**וַיִּתְּנוּ עֲרֵמוֹת עֲרֵמוֹת.** הוֹיְ״פִין בִּלְשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַז, דֻּגְמָא: ״עֲרֵמַת חִטִּים״ (שיר השירים ז:ג):


**and they gave many stacks** עֲרֵמוֹת עֲרֵמוֹת (*houffen*, Middle High German, heaps), an example is (Song 7:3): “a stack of (עֲרֵמַת) wheat.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:7:1)


**הֵחֵלּוּ הָעֲרֵמוֹת לְיִסּוֹד.** כִּי תְּחִלַּת דָּבָר נִקְרָא יְסוֹד, כְּמוֹ יְסוֹד הַבַּיִת שֶׁהוּא רֹאשׁ לְבִנְיָן, וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ ״הוּא יְסֻד הַמַּעֲלָה וְגוֹמֵר״ (עזרא ז:ט):


**they commenced to lay the foundations of the stacks** The beginning of anything is called a foundation, like the foundation of a house, which is the beginning of the structure, and like the verse in Ezra (7:9) “that is the commencement of (יְסֻד) the ascent, etc.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:8:1)


**וַיְבָרְכוּ אֶת ה׳.** שֶׁנָּתַן בְּלֵב יִשְׂרָאֵל לְהַפְרִישׁ תְּרוּמוֹתֵיהֶם וּמַעַשְׂרוֹתֵיהֶם:


**and they blessed the Lord** Who inspired Israel to separate their heave-offerings and their tithes.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:9:1)


**וַיִּדְרֹשׁ יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ.** לְפִי שֶׁרָאָה חִזְקִיָּהוּ הָעֲרֵמוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת, אָמַר בְּלִבּוֹ: שֶׁמָּא לֹא לָקְחוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם מֵהֶם וְלֹא נָגְעוּ בָּהֶם? שֶׁאִם נָגְעוּ בָּהֶם, אֵיךְ נִתְרַבּוּ כָּל כָּךְ? וְתָמַהּ וְדָרַשׁ מֵהֶם **עַל** דְּבַר **הָעֲרֵמוֹת** עַל מָה זֶה:


**And Hezekiah demanded** Since Hezekiah saw that the stacks were large, he said to himself, “Perhaps the priests and the Levites have not taken [their share] from them and have not touched them, for had they touched them, how could they be so large? Since he was puzzled, he questioned them concerning the stacks, “What is the reason for this?”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:10:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר אֵלָיו עֲזַרְיָהוּ.** הוּא הָיָה בִּימֵי עֻזִּיָּהוּ (דברי הימים ב כו:כ):


**And Azariah...said to him** He lived in the days of Uzziah.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:10:2)


**מֵהָחֵל הַתְּרוּמָה לָבִיא.** אָכַלְנוּ **אָכוֹל**, וְלֹא אֲכִילַת עֲרַאי אֶלָּא **אָכוֹל וְשָׂבוֹעַ וְהוֹתֵר**; וּמַה שֶּׁאַתָּה מִתְמֵהַּ עַל שֶׁאָכַלְנוּ אַרְבָּעָה חֳדָשִׁים וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן עֲרֵמוֹתֵיהֶם כָּל כָּךְ גְּדוֹלוֹת – זֶה הַדָּבָר, **כִּי בֵּרַךְ ה׳ אֶת עַמּוֹ** וְנָתַן לָהֶם תְּבוּאוֹת הַרְבֵּה, לְפִיכָךְ הִרְבָּה הַתְּרוּמוֹת וְהַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת שֶׁלָּנוּ:


**“From the beginning of bringing the heave-offering** We have surely eaten, and not an incidental meal but we ate, were satisfied, and left over. Now what you are surprised about, that we ate four months, and nevertheless, their stacks are so large - this is because the Lord blessed His people and gave them abundant grains; therefore, they increased our heave-offerings and tithes.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:10:3)


**וְהַנּוֹתָר.** מִן אֲכִילָתֵנוּ זֶה:


**and what is left over** from this eating of ours.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:10:4)


**הֶהָמוֹן הַזֶּה.** אֲשֶׁר אַתָּה רוֹאֶה עֲרֵמוֹת עֲרֵמוֹת:


**is this abundance** that you see: many stacks.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:11:1)


**לְהָכִין לְשָׁכוֹת בְּבֵית ה׳.** וְלָשׂוּם בָּהֶם הַתְּרוּמוֹת:


**to prepare the chambers in the House of the Lord** in which to deposit the heave-offerings.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:17:1)


**וְאֶת הִתְיַחֵשׂ הַכֹּהֲנִים לְבֵית אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם וְהַלְוִיִּם.** עִם יִחוּס טַפָּם, נְשֵׁיהֶם, בְּנֵיהֶם וּבְנוֹתֵיהֶם, לְכָל אוֹתוֹ קָהָל הָיוּ מַסְפִּיקִין מְזוֹנוֹתֵיהֶם מֵהַתְּרוּמוֹת וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת הַקֹּדֶשׁ. וְהַתְּרוּמוֹת וְהַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת, כָּל אֵלּוּ שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ – לְאוֹתָן הָעוֹמְדִים לְשָׁרֵת בָּעֲזָרָה, וְגַם לִבְנֵי אַהֲרֹן הַכֹּהֲנִים שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעַ מִשְׁמְרוֹתָם לְשָׁרֵת, וְהֵם בִּשְׂדֵה מִגְרַשׁ עָרֵיהֶם בְּכָל עִיר וָעִיר:


**And the priests who traced their genealogy to the house of their fathers, and the Levites** with the genealogy of their infants, their wives, their sons, and their daughters. They would supply their sustenance from the heave-offerings and the tithes for that entire congregation. All these whom we mentioned [would supply food] to those who stood in the forecourt, and also to the sons of Aaron, the priests whose watch had not yet come about in which to minister, and who were in the field of the open land of their cities, in every city.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:19:1)


**אֲנָשִׁים אֲשֶׁר נִקְּבוּ בְשֵׁמוֹת.** לְמַעְלָה (יב-יג) כְּנַנְיָהוּ וְאֶחָיו הָיוּ מְמֻנִּים עֲלֵיהֶם לְקַבֵּל הַמָּנוֹת הַתְּרוּמוֹת וְהַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת מֵאֵת יִשְׂרָאֵל, **לָתֵת** הַ**מָּנוֹת לְכָל זָכָר בַּכֹּהֲנִים** וּלְוִיִּם:


**were men who were mentioned by names** Above (verses 12ff.) Conaniah and his brethren were appointed over them to receive the portions: the heave-offerings and the tithes from the Israelites to distribute the portions to every male among the priests and the Levites


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 31:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/31:19:2)


**לְכָל זָכָר בַּכֹּהֲנִים.** וְגַם לְכָל נְשֵׁיהֶם, בְּנֵיהֶם וּבְנוֹתֵיהֶם (לעיל, יח); וּמַה שֶּׁאָמַר כָּאן לְכָל זָכָר – מִשּׁוּם רְבוּתָא, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ לִזְכָרִים קְטַנִּים הָיוּ מַסְפִּיקִין, כִּדְלְעֵיל **לִזְכָרִים מִבֶּן שָׁלֹשׁ וָמַעְלָה**, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁהָיוּ מַסְפִּיקִין לִנְשֵׁיהֶם וְלִבְנֵיהֶם וְלִבְנוֹתֵיהֶם:


**to every male among the priests** and also to their wives, their sons, and their daughters. The Chronicler says “to every male” here to teach something remarkable, i.e., they would provide even to the young male children, as above (verse 16): “of the males from three years old and over,” and they would surely provide for their wives, sons and daughters.



## Chapter 32


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:1:1)


**אַחֲרֵי הַדְּבָרִים וְהָאֱמֶת הָאֵלֶּה וגו׳ וַיֹּאמֶר לְבִקְעָם.** – כְּמוֹ לְבַקְּעָם; כְּמוֹ ״עַל מִכְרָם בַּכֶּסֶף״ (עמוס ב:ו), כְּמוֹ מָכְרָם, וְסָמוּךְ לֵיהּ ״עַל בִּקְעָם הָרוֹת הַגִּלְעָד״ (עמוס א:יג). וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ וְדֻגְמָא: ״וְנַבְקִיעֶנָּה״ (ישעיה ז:ו); ״וַיְבַקַּע עֲצֵי הָעֹלָה״ (בראשית כב:ג):


**After these deeds of integrity, etc. and planned to make a breach therein** - Heb. לְבִקְעָם like לְבָקְעָם like (Amos 2: “For selling (מִכְרָם) [an innocent man] for money,” like (מָכְרָם) and adjacent to that, (ibid. 1:13): “Because they ripped up (בִּקְעָם) the pregnant women of Gilead,” and its meaning and examples of it are (Isa. 7:6): “and let us breach its walls (וְנַבְקְעֶנָה),” and (Gen. 22:3): “Then he split (וַיְבַקַּע) wood for the burnt-offering.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:5:1)


**וַיְחַזֵּק אֶת הַמִּלּוֹא.** דֻּגְמָא: ״וַיִּבֶן אֶת הַמִּלּוֹא״ (שמואל ב ה:ט):


**and he reinforced the millo** An example is (I Kings 11:27): “built the millo.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:5:2)


**וַיַּעַשׂ שֶׁלַח לָרֹב.** כְּלֵי זַיִן, דֻּגְמַת ״וְאִישׁ שִׁלְחוֹ בְּיָדוֹ״ (דברי הימים ב כג:י); ״אִישׁ שִׁלְחוֹ הַמָּיִם״ (נחמיה ד:יז):


**and he made many swords** - Heb. שֶׁלַח, weapons, similar to (above 23:10): “each one with his sword (שִׁלְחוֹ) in his hand”; (Neh. 4:17): “each man took his sword (שִׁלְחוֹ) to the water.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:10:1)


**עַל מָה אַתֶּם בּוֹטְחִים.** לְפִי שֶׁכָּתוּב ״בַּה׳ אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בָּטַח״ (מלכים ב יח:ה), וְגַם כְּתִיב שָׁם ״וַיִּמְרֹד בְּמֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר״ (מלכים ב יח:ז), לָכֵן אָמַר: עַל מָה אַתֶּם בּוֹטְחִים שֶׁ״מָּרַדְתָּ בִּי״ (מלכים ב יח:כ):


**In what do you trust** It is written (I Kings 18:5): “He trusted in the Lord God of Israel,” and it is also written there: (verse 7) “and he rebelled against the king of Assyria.” Therefore, he asked, “In what do you trust” that you have rebelled against me?


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:10:2)


**וְיֹשְׁבִים בְּמָצוֹר בִּירוּשָׁלִָם.** כְּדִכְתִיב **לָמוּת בָּרָעָב וּבַצָּמָא**:


**stay in the siege in Jerusalem** as it is written (here): “to die by famine and thirst.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:12:1)


**הֲלֹא הוּא יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ.** הַבּוֹטֵחַ בֵּאלֹהָיו הֲלֹא הוּא פָּשַׁע, וַאֲפִלּוּ אִם הָיָה יְכֹלֶת לַעֲזֹר לוֹ לֹא יִהְיֶה עוֹזְרוֹ, כִּי חָטָא לוֹ, שֶׁ**הֵסִיר אֶת בָּמֹתָיו וְאֶת מִזְבְּחֹתָיו וַיֹּאמֶר לִיהוּדָה וְלִירוּשָׁלִַם לִפְנֵי מִזְבֵּחַ אֶחָד תִּשְׁתַּחֲווּ**; וְלֹא מוּטָב שֶׁיִּתְחַוֶה יְהוּדָה לִפְנֵי מִזְבֵּחַ אֶחָד, וִירוּשָׁלַיִם לִפְנֵי אֶחָד אוֹ שְׁנַיִם וְיוֹתֵר? וּמַה מּוֹעִיל אֶחָד לְעַם רַב כָּזֶה? שֶׁהָיוּ סְבוּרִים, שֶׁכָּל אוֹתָן בָּמוֹת שֶׁהֵסִיר, כִּדְלְעֵיל ״וַיְגַדַּע אֶת הָאֲשֵׁרִים וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב יד:ב), שֶׁהָיוּ בְּנוּיִם לְשֵׁם אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**Did not he - Hezekiah -** who trusts in his God, did he not transgress? Now even if He had the ability to help him, He would not help him, because he sinned against Him, **for he removed His high places and His altars, saying to Judah and to Jerusalem: Before one altar you shall prostrate yourselves** Now is it not preferable that the Judeans prostrate themselves before one altar and the Jerusalemites before one, or two and more? What does one altar avail such a large people? For they thought that all those high places that he had removed, as above (31:1): “and cut off the asherim, etc.” were built in the name of the God of Israel.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:14:1)


**הֶחֱרִימוּ אֲבוֹתַי.** פּוּל מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר וְתִגְלַת פִּלְאֶסֶר (דברי הימים א ה:כו):


**whom my fathers destroyed** Pul, the king of Assyria, and Tiglath-Pilneser.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:17:1)


**וּסְפָרִים כָּתַב לְחָרֵף לַה׳ אֱלֹהֵי.** וּבִמְלָכִים (מלכים ב יט:יד) וּבִישַׁעְיָה (ישעיה לז:יד) כְּתִיב ״וַיִּקַּח חִזְקִיָּהוּ אֶת הַסְּפָרִים מִיַּד הַמַּלְאָכִים וַיִּקְרָאֵהוּ וַיַּעַל בֵּית ה׳ וַיִּפְרְשֵׂהוּ חִזְקִיָּהוּ לִפְנֵי ה׳״:


**And he wrote letters to blaspheme the Lord, the God of Israel** And in II Kings (19:14) and in Isaiah (37:14) it is written: “And Hezekiah took the letters from the hand of the messengers and read it; he went up to the House of the Lord, and Hezekiah spread it out before the Lord.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:18:1)


**בְּקוֹל גָּדוֹל יְהוּדִית וגו׳ לְיָרְאָם וּלְבַהֲלָם.** כָּאן סָתַם הָעִנְיָן, וּבִמְלָכִים (מלכים ב יח:כה-לה) מְפֹרָשׁ הֵיטֵב מַה אָמַר לָהֶם; וְגַם בִּישַׁעְיָה (ישעיה לו:יג-כ):


**with a loud voice in Judean, etc. to frighten them and to terrify them** Here he relates the matter in generalities, but in II Kings (19) and in Isaiah (37) it is thoroughly elaborated upon.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:19:1)


**וַיְדַבְּרוּ אֶל אֱלֹהֵי יְרוּשָׁלִָם.** מִי שֶׁכָּתַב סֵפֶר זֶה הוּא קוֹבֵל עַל נָבָל זֶה, סַנְחֵרִיב, שֶׁחֵרֵף אֱלֹהִים חַיִּים (מלכים ב יט:ד):


**And they spoke of the God of Jerusalem** The author of this Book complains about the villain Sennacherib, who blasphemed the living God.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:19:2)


**כְּעַל אֱלֹהֵי עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ.** שֶׁהֵם **מַעֲשֵׂה יְדֵי אָדָם**, וְדִמּוּ אֶת אֱלֹהֵי יוֹצְרֵנוּ, יוֹצֵר הַכֹּל, לֵאלֹהֵי הָעַמִּים שֶׁהֵם מַעֲשֵׂה יְדֵיהֶם; כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְעֵיל **מִי בְּכָל אֱלֹהֵי וְגוֹ׳, כִּי** לֹא **יוּכַל אֱלֹהֵיכֶם לְהַצִּיל אֶתְכֶם מִיָּדִי**:


**as of the gods of the peoples of the land** which are the handiwork of man, and they compared the God, our Maker, Who formed everything, to the gods of the peoples, who are their own handiwork, as is written above (v. 14): “Who is it among all the gods of, etc. that your God should be able to save you from my hand?”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:20:1)


**וַיִּתְפַּלֵּל יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ.** בִּמְלָכִים (מב יט:טו-יט) וּבִישַׁעְיָה (יש לז:טו-כ) מְפֹרֶשֶׁת הַתְּפִלָּה:


**And Hezekiah... prayed** In II Kings (19:15–19) and in Isaiah (37:15–19) the prayer is delineated.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:21:1)


**וּמִיצִיאֵי מֵעָיו.** כְּדִכְתִיב ״וּבָנָיו הִכּוּהוּ בֶחָרֶב״ (ישעיה לז:לח):


**and some of his own issue** as it is written (Isa. 37:38): “and his sons slew him with the sword.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:24:1)


**וַיִּתְפַּלֵּל אֶל ה׳.** כְּדִמְפוֹרָשׁ בִּמְלָכִים (מלכים ב כ:ב-ג):


**and he prayed to the Lord** as is delineated in II Kings (20:3).


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:24:2)


**וַיֹּאמֶר לוֹ.** מָה הָאוֹת?


**and he spoke to him** What is the sign?


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:24:3)


**וּמוֹפֵת נָתַן לוֹ.** ״וְזֶה לְךָ הָאוֹת״; הַכֹּל מְפֹרָשׁ שָׁם (מלכים ב כ:ט):


**and he gave him a sign** (ibid. verse 9ff.) “This is your sign, etc.” Everything is delineated there.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:25:1)


**וְלֹא כִגְמוּל עָלָיו הֵשִׁיב.** לֹא כְּמוֹ שֶׁגָּמַל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עַל יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ שֶׁרְפָאוֹהוּ, הֵשִׁיב לוֹ יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ:


**But...did not repay Him according to that which He had bestowed upon him** Hezekiah did not repay the Holy One, blessed be He in accordance with that which He had bestowed upon him by healing him.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:25:2)


**כִּי גָבַהּ לִבּוֹ.** שֶׁהֶרְאָה ״אֶת כָּל בֵּית נְכֹתוֹ וְגוֹ׳״ עַד ״וּבְכָל מֶמְשַׁלְתּוֹ״ (מלכים ב כ:יג; ישעיה לט:ב):


**for his heart became haughty** as it is written (ibid. 20:13): “and he showed them his entire treasure-house...and in all his kingdom.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:25:3)


**וַיְהִי עָלָיו קֶצֶף וְעַל יְהוּדָה וִירוּשָׁלִָם.** עַל זֹאת; וְהַכּוֹתֵב הַסֵּפֶר הַזֶּה קִצְּרוֹ כָּאן, לְפִי שֶׁכָּתוּב בִּמְלָכִים (מלכים ב כ:טז-יח) וּבִישַׁעְיָה (ישעיה לט:ה-ז):


**and there was wrath upon him and upon Judah and Jerusalem** because of this, and the author of this Book abridged it here because it is described in II Kings (20) and in Isaiah (39).



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:26:1)


**וַיִּכָּנַע יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ בְּגֹבַהּ לִבּוֹ.** כְּדִכְתִיב שָׁם ״וַיֹּאמֶר חִזְקִיָּה אֶל יְשַׁעְיָה טוֹב וְגוֹמֵר״ (מלכים ב כ:יט; ישעיה לט:ח), וּכְשֶׁנִּכְנַע, **לֹא בָא עֲלֵיהֶם** הַקֶּצֶף בְּיָמָיו:


**And Hezekiah became humbled when his heart was haughty** as it is written there: “And Hezekiah said to Isaiah: [The word of the Lord that you have spoken is] good.” And when he became humble, the wrath did not come upon them in his days.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:27:1)


**וּלְכָל כְּלֵי חֶמְדָּה. עָשָׂה** מִינֵי **אֹצָרוֹת**:


**and for all precious vessels** he made various kinds of treasure-houses.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:28:1)


**וּמִסְכְּנוֹת לִתְבוּאַת.** עַל אוֹצְרוֹת הַתְּבוּאָה נוֹפֵל לוֹמַר מִסְכְּנוֹת בְּכָל מָקוֹם, כְּמוֹ ״עָרֵי מִסְכְּנוֹת לְפַרְעֹה״ (שמות א:יא), וְכֵן לְעֵיל ״וַיִּבֶן תַּדְמֹר בַּמִּדְבָּר וְאֵת כָּל עָרֵי מִסְכְּנוֹת אֲשֶׁר בָּנָה בַּחֲמָת״ (דברי הימים ב ח:ד); וְעַל כָּרְחֲךָ, לְצֹרֶךְ תְּבוּאָה, שֶׁבַּמִּדְבָּר לֹא הָיָה לוֹ לֶאֱצֹר זָהָב וָכֶסֶף:


**And storehouses for the increase** Regarding storehouses of increase [of grain] it is appropriate to use the term מִסְכְּנוֹת, storehouses, in all places, like (Exod. 1:11): “store cities (עָרֵי מִסְכְּנוֹת) for Pharaoh,” and so, (above 8:4): “And he built Tadmor in the desert and all the store cities (עָרֵי מִסְכְּנוֹת) that he built in Hamath,” and perforce, they were for grain, because in the desert he did not have to store gold and silver.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:28:2)


**וְאֻרָוֹת לְכָל בְּהֵמָה וּבְהֵמָה.** אִיצְטַבְּלָאוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת:


**and stalls for all kinds of beasts** - Heb. וְאֻרָוֹת, large stables.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:30:1)


**וַיַּיִּשְׁרֵם לְמַטָּה מַעְרָבָה.** הִנְהִיגָם דֶּרֶךְ יָשָׁר לְמַטָּה מַעְרָבָה **לְעִיר דָּוִד**:


**and he led them straight down on the west** He led them along a straight way down on the west to the City of David.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:31:1)


**וְכֵן בִּמְלִיצֵי.** שְׁלוּחֵי; דֻּגְמָא: ״כִּי הַמֵּלִיץ בֵּינוֹתָם״ (בראשית מב:כג) – עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַשָּׁלִיחַ מֵלִיץ בֵּין הַשּׁוֹלֵחַ לַמִּשְׁתַּלֵּחַ; וְנוֹפֵל עַל פָּסוּק שֶׁאָמַר ״וַיַּצְלַח יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ בְּכָל מַעֲשָׂיו״ (דברי הימים ב לא:כא). וְאַחַר כָּל הַטּוֹב הַזֶּה שֶׁהִצְלִיחוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, **עֲזָבוֹ הָאֱלֹהִים, לְנַסּוֹתוֹ לָדַעַת כָּל בִּלְבָבוֹ** בְּיַד מְלִיצֵי **שָׂרֵי בָּבֶל הַמְשַׁלְּחִים** אֵלָיו:


**And so, because of the emissaries of** Heb. בִּמְלִיצֵי, messengers, similar to (Gen. 42:23): “for the interpreter (הַמֵּלִיץ) was between them” because the messenger is the liaison between the sender and the one to whom he is sent. This is related to the verse that states: “and Hezekiah prospered in all his work,” and after all this good, which the Holy One, blessed be He, had bestowed on him, God forsook him to test him, to know what was in his heart through the emissaries of the officers of Babylon, who were sent to him.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:31:2)


**לִדְרוֹשׁ הַמּוֹפֵת אֲשֶׁר הָיָה בָאָרֶץ.** לֹא עָמַד בַּנִּסָּיוֹן, וְהֶרְאָה לָהֶם אֶת כָּל בֵּית נְכֹתוֹ (פרקי דרבי אליעזר נב); וּמִסֵּיפֵיהּ לְרֵישֵׁיהּ נִדְרָשׁ:


**to seek the sign that was in the land** He did not stand up to the test, and he showed them the weakness of his whole house. The verse is to be explained as if the phrases are reversed.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 32:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/32:33:1)


**בְּמַעֲלֵה.** בְּמֵיטַב, כְּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּסוֹף פֶּרֶק קַמָּא דְּבָבָא קַמָּא (טז:):


**in the best** - Heb. בְּמַעֲלֵה, in the best, as is explained at the end of the first chapter of *Baba Kamma* (16b).



## Chapter 33


### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:4:1)


**בִּירוּשָׁלִַם יִהְיֶה שְּׁמִי לְעוֹלָם.** הַפָּסוּק קוֹבֵל: זֶה מְנַשֶּׁה שֶׁבָּנָה מִזְבְּחוֹת לַעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת בַּבַּיִת שֶׁאָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: אֲנִי רוֹצֶה שֶׁיִּשְׁכּוֹן שְׁמִי שָׁם. וְגַם פָּסוּק שֶׁאָמַר בְּסָמוּךְ לְשׁוֹן קוֹבְלָנָא הוּא: **וַיָּשֶׂם אֶת פֶּסֶל הַסֶּמֶל אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה בְּבֵית הָאֱלֹהִים וְגוֹ׳**, וְגַם הַפָּסוּק **וְלֹא אוֹסִיף לְהָסִיר אֶת רֶגֶל**:


**“In Jerusalem, My name shall be forever”** Scripture decries Manasseh, who built altars to pagan deities in the very House concerning which the Holy One, blessed be He, said, “I wish My name to rest there.” The verse stated after this (verse 7) is also an expression of decrying: “And he placed the graven image of the idol that he made in the House of God, concerning which, etc.” And also the verse (8): “And I will not continue to remove Israel’s feet, etc.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:6:1)


**וְעָשָׂה אוֹב וְיִדְּעוֹנִי.** מָלֵא בְּוָי״ו, לְפִי שֶׁ**הִרְבָּה לַעֲשׂוֹת**; וְהוּא הוֹסִיף עַל **פֶּסֶל הַסֶּמֶל אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה**:


**and those who divine by the jidoa bone** - Heb. יִדְּעוֹנִי spelled in full with a “vav,” because he did much [evil], and he added to the graven image of the idol that he made. They would take the bone of an animal known as jidoa, and place it in their mouths, whence it would speak.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:7:1)


**לְעֵילוֹם.** לְעוֹלָם:


**forever** - Heb. לְעֵילוֹם, [a variant of] לְעוֹלָם.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:8:1)


**וְלֹא אוֹסִיף לְהָסִיר אֶת רֶגֶל יִשְׂרָאֵל.** מוּסָב עַל פָּסוּק שֶׁל מַעְלָה (פסוק ז), וְהַכֹּל קוֹבְלָנָא הוּא, כְּלוֹמַר: הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אוֹמֵר: כָּךְ אָמַרְתִּי אֶל דָּוִד וְאֶל שְׁלֹמֹה: בַּבַּיִת הַזֶּה אָשִׂים שְׁמִי שָׁם לְעוֹלָם, וְגַם הִבְטַחְתִּים: לֹא אוֹסִיף לְהָסִיר רֶגֶל יִשְׂרָאֵל **מֵעַל הָאֲדָמָה, רַק אִם יִשְׁמְרוּ וְגוֹ׳**; וְעַתָּה **וַיֶּתַע מְנַשֶּׁה וְגוֹ׳**. נִמְצָא שֶׁעַל יָדוֹ נִסְתַּלְּקָה שְׁכִינָה, וְגַם אָנוּ עֲדַיִן בַּגָּלוּת עַל יָדוֹ:


**And I will not continue to remove Israel’s feet** This refers back to the above verse, and all is a complaint; i.e., the Holy One, blessed be He, said, “So I said to David and to Solomon, ‘In this House I will place My name forever,’ and I also promised him, ‘And I will not continue to remove Israel’s feet from the land, if they will but observe, etc.’” But now **And Manasseh led astray, etc.** Because of him the Shechinah departed, and we are also still in exile because of him.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:14:1)


**לְגִיחוֹן בַּנַּחַל.** אֵצֶל נַחַל גִּיחוֹן, שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


**of Gihon in the valley** beside the valley of Gihon, the name of a place.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:14:2)


**וְסָבַב לָעֹפֶל.** וְסָבַב מִשָּׁם חֹזֶק הַמִּגְדָּלִים הַבְּנוּיִים בִּמְלָאכֶת עֹפֶל:


**and he surrounded the Ophel** And he surrounded from there the strength of the towers built with the work of Ophel.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:15:1)


**וַיַּשְׁלֵךְ חוּצָה.** וְלֹא שִׁבְּרָם וְלֹא גְנָזָם מִן הָעַיִן, לְפִיכָךְ נִכְשַׁל בָּהֶם בְּנוֹ, כְּדִכְתִיב **וּלְכָל הַפְּסִילִים אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה מְנַשֶּׁה אָבִיו וְגוֹ׳**, שֶׁלָּקַח אוֹתָם מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁהִשְׁלִיךְ אוֹתָם:


**and he cast them outside** but he neither broke them nor hid them from the eye. Therefore, his son went astray after them, as it is written (below verse 22): “and to all the graven images that [Manasseh his father] had made,” for he took them from the place where he had cast them.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:16:1)


**וַיִּזְבַּח עָלָיו זִבְחֵי שְׁלָמִים וְתוֹדָה.** שֶׁהֵבִיא קָרְבַּן תּוֹדָה, וְהִתְוַדָּה לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהֱשִׁיבוֹ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם וּלְמַלְכוּתוֹ:


**and he sacrificed upon it peace-offerings and thanksgiving offerings** He brought a thanksgiving offering and thanked the Holy One, blessed be He, for restoring him to Jerusalem and to his kingdom.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:19:1)


**וּתְפִלָּתוֹ.** הֵיאַךְ נִתְפַּלֵּל:


**and his prayer** how he prayed.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:19:2)


**וְהֵעָתֶר לוֹ.** וְהֵשִׁיבוֹ עַל מַלְכוּתוֹ:


**and its acceptance** and his return to his kingdom.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 33:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/33:20:1)


**וַיִּקְבְּרֻהוּ בֵיתוֹ.** כְּמוֹ בְּבֵיתוֹ; וּבִמְלָכִים (מלכים ב כא:יח) כְּתִיב ״בְּגַן בֵּיתוֹ״, לֹא זָכָה לִיקָּבֵר עִם אֲבוֹתָיו:


**and they buried him in his house** Like “in his house.” In II Kings (21:18) it is written: “in the garden of his house.” He did not merit to be buried with his forefathers.



## Chapter 34


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:3:1)


**וְהוּא עוֹדֶנּוּ נַעַר הֵחֵל לִדְרוֹשׁ.** פִּתְרוֹן: אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָיָה נַעַר הֵחֵל לִדְרוֹשׁ **אלֹהֵי דָּוִיד אָבִיו**; דּוּגְמַת ״וְהוּא נַעַר אֶת בְּנֵי בִלְהָה... וַיָּבֵא יוֹסֵף אֶת דִּבָּתָם רָעָה״ (בראשית לז:ב) – וְאֵין תֵּימַהּ בַּדָּבָר, אֲבָל אִם הָיָה בֶּן שְׁלֹשִׁים שָׁנָה לֹא הָיָה עוֹשֶׂה; וְזֶה תֵּימַהּ, שֶׁהָיָה נַעַר, וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵּן הֵחֵל לִדְרוֹשׁ וְגוֹ׳:


**when he was still a youth, he started to seek** The meaning is that although he was still a youth, he started to seek after the God of David his father. This is similar to (Gen. 37:2): “and as a youth, with the sons of Bilhah, etc., Joseph brought evil reports of them,” and this is not surprising, but if he were thirty years old, he would not have done it. It is, however, surprising, that he (Josiah) was a youth, and nevertheless, he started to seek, etc.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:3:2)


**הֵחֵל לְטַהֵר אֶת יְהוּדָה וִירוּשָׁלִַם.** מִן הַבָּמוֹת וְהָאֲשֵׁרִים שֶׁעָשָׂה מְנַשֶּׁה וְחִדְּשָׁם אָמוֹן בְּנוֹ, כִּדְלְמַעְלָה (דברי הימים ב לג:ג) ״וַיָּשָׁב וַיִּבֶן אֶת הַבָּמוֹת אֲשֶׁר נִתֵּץ יְחִזְקִיָּהוּ אָבִיו וְגוֹ׳״:


**he started to purge Judah and Jerusalem of the high places, the asherim** that Manasseh had made and that Amon his son had renewed, as above (33:3): “And he rebuilt the high places that Hezekiah his father had demolished, etc.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:4:1)


**וְהַחַמָּנִים.** צוּרוֹת חַמָּה:


**and the sun images** images of the sun.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:4:2)


**אֲשֶׁר לְמַעְלָה.** שֶׁהוֹשִׁיבוּ לְמַעְלָה מֵהֶם, גָּבוֹהַּ כְּנֶגֶד הַחַמָּה, וּמִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לוֹ. כָּל זֶה עָשָׂה מְנַשֶּׁה, כְּדִכְתִיב לְעֵיל ״וַיִּשְׁתַּחֲוֶה לְכָל צְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם וַיַּעֲבֹד אוֹתָם״ (דברי הימים ב לג:ג):


**that were above** That they had placed above them, on high, opposite the sun, and they would prostrate themselves to it. Manasseh had done all this, as it is written above (33:3): “and he prostrated himself to the entire host of the heaven, and he worshipped them.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:4:3)


**וַיִּזְרֹק עַל פְּנֵי הַקְּבָרִים.** כָּאן נִתְקַיֵּים מַה שֶּׁאָמַר לְיָרָבְעָם: ״כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ הִנֵּה בֵן נוֹלָד לְבֵית דָּוִד יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ שְׁמוֹ וְזָבַח עָלֶיךָ עַצְמוֹת אָדָם אֶת כֹּהֲנֵי הַבָּמוֹת הַמַּקְטִרִים״ (מלכים א יג:ב):


**and he cast them upon the graves** Here was fulfilled what he said to Jeroboam, (I Kings 13:2): “so said the Lord, ‘Behold a son will be born to the house of David, Josiah will be his name, and upon you will he sacrifice, etc., and human bones, etc.’”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:6:1)


**וּבְעָרֵי מְנַשֶּׁה וְאֶפְרַיִם וְגוֹ׳.** מֵאוֹתָן עוֹלְלוֹת שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּיְּרוּ מֵחֶרֶב וּמִשְּׁבִי סַנְחֵרִיב (מלכים ב יז:ו):


**And in the cities of Manasseh and Ephraim, etc.** from those “gleanings” [survivors] who survived the sword and the captivity of Sennacherib.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:6:2)


**בְּחַרְבֹתֵיהֶם.** בְּהַר בָּתֵּיהֶם כְּתִיב, לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ כִּי אִם עוֹלֵלוֹת מְעַט, נִתְיָרְאוּ לֵישֵׁב בַּשְּׁפֵלָה, כִּי אִם לִקְבֹּעַ בְּהַר בָּתֵּיהֶם:


**with their instruments of destruction** Since they were but few “gleanings,” they feared to live in the lowland and established their houses on the mountain (בְּחַר בָּתֵּיהֶם).



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:8:1)


**הַמַּזְכִּיר.** בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁכָּתוּב סוֹפֵר כָּתוּב בְּסָמוּךְ מַזְכִּיר, שֶׁהָיָה לְכָל מֶלֶךְ סוֹפֵר לִכְתֹּב כָּל הַמְּאוֹרָע לוֹ בַּמַּלְכוּת, בֵּין טוֹב בֵּין רַע, וַיְהִי לוֹ מַזְכִּיר יוֹשֵׁב אֵצֶל הַסּוֹפֵר לְהַזְכִּיר כָּל דָּבָר וְדָבָר, וְהַסּוֹפֵר כּוֹתֵב עַל פִּיו:


**the recorder** Wherever a scribe is mentioned, a recorder is mentioned in close proximity, for every king had a scribe to write everything that occurred in the kingdom, whether favorable or unfavorable, and he had a recorder sitting beside the scribe, to mention every incident, and the scribe would write according to what he told him.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:9:1)


**אֲשֶׁר אָסְפוּ הַלְוִיִּם שֹׁמְרֵי הַסַּף וְגוֹ׳.** בְּחַרְבוֹתֵיהֶם סָבִיב:


**which the Levites who guarded the threshold had gathered from the hands of Manasseh and Ephraim, etc.** from their ruins round about.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:10:1)


**לִבְדּוֹק וּלְחַזֵּק.** ״לִרְאוֹת אֶת עֶרְוַת הָאָרֶץ״ (בראשית מב:ט) מְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״וּלְמֶחֱזֵי יָת בִּדְקָא דְאַרְעָא״:


**to mend and repair** - Heb. לִבְדוֹק. (Gen 42:9): “to see the nakedness (עֶרְוַת) of the land,” is translated into Aramaic as “to see the crack (בִּדְקָא)” of the land.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:11:1)


**וְעֵצִים לַמְחַבְּרוֹת.** שְׁפַּנְגִי״ן בִּלְשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַ״ז:


**and wood for the couplings** - *Spangen* in Middle High German, clasps.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:11:2)


**וּלְקָָרוֹת.** טְרַבִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and to make beams** - *Traver* in Old French, to provide with beams.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:12:1)


**לְנַצֵּחַ.** רוֹמִי״שׁ טְרִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, וְכֵן (עזרא ג:ט) ״לְנַצֵּחַ עַל הַמְּלָאכָה״, וְכֵן ״שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת מְנַצְּחִים״ לְעֵיל (דברי הימים ב ב:יז):


**to direct** - Heb. לְנַצּחַ, *lemestriyer*, to direct, supervise, and so (Ezra 3:8): “to direct (לְנַצּחַ) the work of the House of the Lord,” and likewise (above 2:17): “and six hundred overseers (מְנַצּחִים) .”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:12:2)


**וְהַלְוִיִּם כָּל מֵבִין.** בְּכָל מָקוֹם אָמוּר לְעִנְיַן שִׁירָה הֲבָנָה, וְכֵן לְמַעְלָה כְּשֶׁהִפִּילוּ גּוֹרָלוֹת כְּתִיב ״כַּקָּטֹן כַּגָּדוֹל מֵבִין עִם תַּלְמִיד״ (דברי הימים א כה:ח), וְכֵן ״כָּל הַמֵּבִין בִּכְלֵי שִׁיר״, וּכְתִיב ״וּכְנַנְיָהוּ שַׂר הַלְוִיִּם וְגוֹמֵר כִּי מֵבִין הוּא״ (דברי הימים א טו:כב), וְהָיָה מְמֻנֶּה עֲלֵיהֶם לְיַסֵּר ״בַּמַּשָּׂא״ הֵיאַךְ יִשָּׂאוּ, כִּי מֵבִין הוּא:


**and the Levites, all who were expert** - Heb. מֵבִין. The term expertise (הֲבָנָה) is universally mentioned regarding song, and so above (I Chron. 25:8), when they cast lots, it is written: “like the small one so the great one, expert (מֵבִין) with pupil.” And likewise, (ibid. verse 7): “trained in song to the Lord: every expert (מֵבִין),” and it is written (ibid. 15:22): “And Chananiah, the leader of the Levites, etc., because he was an expert (מֵבִין),” and he was appointed over them to chastise [them] in raising [the voice], how they should raise [their voice], because he was an expert (מֵבִין).



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:13:1)


**וּמֵהַלְוִיִּם סוֹפְרִים.** לִכְתּוֹב **אֶת הַכֶּסֶף הַמּוּבָא בֵּית ה׳**, וְהֵיאַךְ גָּבוּ אוֹתוֹ:


**and of the Levites, scribes** to write down the [amount of] silver brought to the House of the Lord and how they had collected it.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:13:2)


**וְשׁוֹטְרִים.** בַּמְּלָאכָה:


**and officers** over the work.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:14:1)


**אֶת סֵפֶר תּוֹרַת ה׳ בְּיַד מֹשֶׁה.** מִשְׁנֵה תּוֹרָה (דברים):


**the Scroll of the Law of the Lord by the hand of Moses** the Book of Deuteronomy.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:15:1)


**וַיִּתֵּן חִלְקִיָּהוּ אֶת הַסֵּפֶר אֶל שָׁפָן.** שֶׁהֲרֵי עַל הַסּוֹפֵר לִקְרוֹת אֶת הַכֹּל לִפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**and Hilkiah gave the scroll to Shaphan** because it was the scribe’s duty to read everything before the king.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:16:1)


**וַיָּשֶׁב עוֹד אֶת הַמֶּלֶךְ דָּבָר.** שֶׁמִּתְּחִלָּה הֵשִׁיב לַמֶּלֶךְ שְׁלִיחוּתוֹ, שֶׁשְּׁלָחוֹ הַמֶּלֶךְ **לְחַזֵּק בֵּית ה׳ אֱלֹהָיו**, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה **שָׁלַח אֶת שָׁפָן בֶּן אֲצַלְיָהוּ וְגוֹ׳**:


**and he further brought back word to the king** for he first reported to the king about the errand that the king had sent him on to repair the House of the Lord his God, as is written above (verse 8): “he sent Shaphan the son of Azaliah, etc.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:17:1)


**וַיַּתִּיכוּ.** וַיַּצִּיקוּ; כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּצֹק שֶׁמֶן״ (בראשית כח:יח) וּמְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״וְאַתִּיךְ״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


**And they poured** - Heb. וַיַּתִּיכוּ, they poured, like (Gen. 28:18): “and he poured (וַיִּצֹק) oil” and the *Targum* renders: וְאַתִּיךְ



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:18:1)


**וַיַּגֵּד שָׁפָן הַסּוֹפֵר.** אַחַר שֶׁהֵשִׁיב הַמֶּלֶךְ דָּבָר מִשְּׁלִיחוּתוֹ, הִגִּיד לוֹ שְׁלִיחוּת שֶׁל חִלְקִיָּהוּ הַכֹּהֵן **וַיִּקְרָא** אֶת הַסֵּפֶר **לְפָנָיו**:


**And Shaphan the scribe told the king** After bringing back word to the king regarding his errand, he gave him the message of Hilkiah the priest, and read the scroll before him.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:19:1)


**וַיִּקְרַע אֶת בְּגָדָיו.** כְּשֶׁשָּׁמַע שֶׁהָיוּ קוֹרִין ״יוֹלֵךְ ה׳ אוֹתְךָ וְאֶת מַלְכְּךָ וְגוֹמֵר״ (דברים כח:לו), וְדָרַשׁ בְּעַצְמוֹ ״אֲשֶׁר תָּקִים עָלֶיךָ״ (דברים כח:לו) – הָעָם הִמְלִיכוּ וְלֹא נָבִיא עַל פִּי הַדִּבּוּר (יומא נב:):


**that he rent his garments** When he heard them reading (Deut. 28:36): “The Lord will lead you and the king, etc.,” he interpreted [the following words] as referring to himself: “whom you will appoint over yourself.” The people had made him king, and he was not a prince by the word [of God].



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:21:1)


**אֲשֶׁר נִתְּכָה בָנוּ.** לְפִי שֶׁהַחֵמָה הִיא אֵשׁ, נוֹפֵל עָלֶיהָ לְשׁוֹן נִתּוּךְ, וְדֻגְמָתוֹ לְמַטָּה **וְתִתַּךְ חֲמָתִי בַּמָּקוֹם הַזֶּה וְלֹא תִכְבֶּה**:


**which has been poured upon us** Since wrath is [compared to] fire, the term pouring applies to it, and a similar instance is below (verse 25): “My wrath shall be poured down upon this place, and it shall not be quenched.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:22:1)


**אֶל חוּלְדָּה הַנְּבִיאָה.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ (מגלה יד:): לְפִי כִּי הָאִשָּׁה רַחֲמָנִית יוֹתֵר מִן הָאִישׁ, לָכֵן לֹא שָׁלַח אֶל יִרְמְיָהוּ הַנָּבִיא:


**to Huldah the prophetess** Our Sages (*Meg.* 14b) explained that since a woman is more merciful than a man, he did not send them to Jeremiah the prophet.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:22:2)


**שׁוֹמֵר הַבְּגָדִים.** אִם תִּרְצֶה לוֹמַר: אוֹ בִּגְדֵי כְהֻנָּה אוֹ בִּגְדֵי מַלְכוּת:


**the keeper of the raiment** If you wish, you may say that this is either the priestly raiment or the royal raiment.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:22:3)


**יוֹשֶׁבֶת בִּירוּשָׁלִַם בַּמִּשְׁנֶה.** שְׁנֵי חוֹמוֹת הָיוּ לָעִיר, וְהִיא יוֹשֶׁבֶת בֵּין שְׁנֵי הַחוֹמוֹת. וְתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״בֵּית אוּלְפָנָא״ – פֵּירוּשׁ: בִּמְקוֹם תּוֹרָה, כִּי לִשְׁכָּה אַחַת הָיְתָה לָהּ לְחוּלְדָּה, סְמוּכָה לְלִשְׁכַּת הַגָּזִית; לִשְׁכַּת חוּלְדָּה הָיְתָה פְּתוּחָה לַחוּץ וּסְתוּמָה כְּלַפֵּי סַנְהֶדְרִין שֶׁבְּלִשְׁכַּת הַגָּזִית, כָּךְ כָּתוּב בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (מדות א:ג) – מִפְּנֵי צְנִיעוּת:


**and she was sitting in Jerusalem in the study-hall** - Heb. בַּמִּשֶׁנֶה. The city had two walls, and she lived between the two walls. The Targum renders: in the study-hall. The meaning is: in the place of Torah, for Huldah had a chamber adjacent to the Chamber of Hewn Stone. The Chamber of Huldah was open to the outside and closed toward the Sanhedrin in the Chamber of Hewn Stone. So it is written in Tractate *Middoth* (unknown) because of modesty.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:23:1)


**אִמְרוּ לָאִישׁ.** לְפִי שֶׁרָצָה לְהַפְסִיק חֲזוֹן הָעָם **עַל הַמָּקוֹם הַזֶּה וְעַל יוֹשְׁבָיו**, וּכְשֶׁשִּׁלֵּם אוֹתוֹ חֲזוֹן, אָז מְבָאֵר הֵטֵיב: **וְאֶל מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה הַשּׁוֹלֵחַ אֶתְכֶם**, וּמְקַצֵּר נְבוּאַת הַמָּקוֹם וְיוֹשְׁבָיו וּמְסַלְּקוֹ, וְתוֹפְשׂוֹ בִּנְבוּאַת הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁהוּא מְרֻבָּה:


**Say to the man** He wished to interrupt the prophecy of the people “upon this place and upon its inhabitants,” and when he finished that prophecy, he then delineates, “And concerning the king of Judah, who sent you,” and he deals briefly with the prophecy of the palace and its inhabitants and puts it aside and focuses upon the prophecy of the king, which is long.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:28:1)


**וְנֶאֱסַפְתָּ אֶל קִבְרוֹתֶיךָ בְּשָׁלוֹם.** וַהֲלֹא נֶהֱרַג (דברי הימים ב לה:כג-כד)! אֶלָּא בְּשָׁלוֹם מִזֶּה שֶׁ**לֹּא תִרְאֶינָה עֵינֶיךָ בְּכֹל הָרָעָה אֲשֶׁר אֲנִי מֵבִיא עַל הַמָּקוֹם הַזֶּה וְעַל יֹשְׁבָיו**, כְּלוֹמַר: חֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת:


**and you shall be gathered to your graves in peace** But was he not slain? It [means] in peace from this, that your eyes will not see all the evil that I am bringing upon this place and upon its inhabitants, i.e., the destruction of the Temple.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:30:1)


**סֵפֶר הַבְּרִית.** הֲרֵי כָּל הַחֲרָדָה שֶׁחָרַד הַמֶּלֶךְ עַל שֶׁנִּמְצָא כָתוּב ״יוֹלֵךְ ה׳ אוֹתְךָ״ (דברים כח:לו), וְרָאָה שֶׁקָּרְאָה הַתּוֹרָה אוֹתָהּ בְּרִית, ״אֵלֶּה דִבְרֵי הַבְּרִית״ (דברים כח:סט), וּלְכָךְ אַף הוּא כָּרַת בְּרִית. וְרָאָה כָתוּב בְּסָמוּךְ אַחֲרָיו ״וּשְׁמַרְתֶּם אֶת דִּבְרֵי וְגוֹ׳ וַעֲשִׂיתֶם אוֹתָם״ (דברים כט:ח), גַּם רָאָה יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ כָּתוּב בְּסָמוּךְ ״אַתֶּם נִצָּבִים הַיּוֹם כֻּלְּכֶם וְגוֹ׳״, עַד ״טַפְּכֶם נְשֵׁיכֶם״, עַד ״לְעָבְרְךָ בִּבְרִית״, עַד ״כֹּרֵת עִמְּךָ הַיּוֹם״ (דברים כט:ט-יא), אָמַר: אַף בְּאוֹתוֹ דֻגְמָא אֶכְרוֹת אַף אֲנִי בְּרִית, שֶׁשָּׁם כָּתוּב ״אַתֶּם נִצָּבִים וְגוֹ׳״, אַף אֲנִי ״וַיַּעֲמֹד כָּל הָעָם הַנִּמְצָא בִּירוּשָׁלִַם״ – מִקָּטֹן וְעַד גָּדוֹל:


**the Scroll of the Covenant** All the fright that the king experienced was due to the words (Deut. 28: 36): “The Lord will lead you,” and he saw that the Torah called it a covenant: “These are the words of the covenant.” Therefore, he too enacted a covenant and saw written immediately following it (ibid. 29:8): “And you shall keep the words, etc., and practice them.” Josiah also saw written immediately following (ibid. verses 9ff.): “You are standing today, all of you, etc....your children, your wives...that you should pass into the covenant...enacts with you today.” He said, “In such a manner, I too shall enact a covenant, for there it is written: ‘You are standing, etc.’ I too [will do so], ‘And he caused all those found in Jerusalem, both small and great, to stand.’”



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:31:1)


**בְּכָל לְבָבוֹ וּבְכָל נַפְשׁוֹ וְגוֹ׳.** אֲשֶׁר מָצָא שָׁם כָּתוּב (דברים ל:ב) ״וְשַׁבְתָּ עַד ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ בְּכָל לְבָבְךָ וּבְכָל נַפְשֶׁךָ״:


**with all his heart and with all his soul** as he found written there, (Deut. 30:2): “And you will return to the Lord your God, etc. with all your heart and with all your soul.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:31:2)


**וַיַּעֲמֹד הַמֶּלֶךְ עַל עָמְדוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן ״נִצָּבִים״ (דברים כט:ט):


**in his place** An expression of נִצָּבִים, standing.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 34:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/34:32:1)


**כְּבְרִית אֱלֹהִים אֱלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם.** כְּכָל אוֹתָן שֶׁכָּתוּב שָׁם ״אֱלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם״, לְפִיכָךְ כְּתִיב כָּאן אֱלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם, שֶׁכָּרַת לַאֲבוֹתָם בַּמִּדְבָּר. וְכָל בְּרִית שֶׁלְּשָׁם – עַל עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים, דִּכְתִיב ״וַתִּרְאוּ אֶת שִׁקּוּצֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳״ (דברים כט:טז), עַד ״פּוֹנֶה הַיּוֹם מֵעִם ה׳״ (דברים כט:יז), לְפִיכָךְ **וַיָּסַר יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ אֶת כָּל הַתּוֹעֵבוֹת וְגוֹ׳**:


**according to the covenant of God, the God of their forefathers** According to all that which is written there: “the God of their forefathers.” Therefore, it is written here, “the God of their forefathers,” which He enacted for their forefathers in the desert. And every covenant that is there concerns idolatry, as it is written (Deut. 29:16f.): “And you saw their abominations and their depravities, etc....turns away today from the Lord.” Therefore, Josiah “removed all the abominations, etc.”



## Chapter 35


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:1:1)


**וַיַּעַשׂ יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ בִירוּשָׁלִַם פֶּסַח.** לְפִי שֶׁכָּל מַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה הָרְשָׁעִים לֹא עָשׂוּ פֶּסַח כְּמִשְׁפָּטוֹ:


**And Josiah made the Passover sacrifice in Jerusalem** since none of the wicked kings of Judah had made the Passover sacrifice according to its law.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:3:1)


**תְּנוּ אֶת אֲרוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** לְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ, שֶׁהֶעֱבִירוּ מְנַשֶּׁה וְאָמוֹן אֶת הָאָרוֹן וַיָּשִׂימוּ תַּחְתָּיו פְּסִילֵיהֶם, כְּדִמוֹכַח לְעֵיל בִּמְנַשֶּׁה ״וַיָּשֶׂם פֶּסֶל הַסֶּמֶל וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב לג:ז), לְפִיכָךְ צִוָּה יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ לְהוֹשִׁיבוֹ וְלִיתֵּן הָאָרוֹן בִּמְקוֹמוֹ **אֲשֶׁר בָּנָה שְׁלֹמֹה**. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (ירושלמי שקלים ו:א; יומא נב:), שֶׁאָמַר לַלְוִיִּם לְגוֹנְזוֹ שָׁם:


**Place the Holy Ark** According to its simple meaning, [they were to return it to its place] because Manasseh and Amon had removed the Holy Ark and placed their graven images in its place, as is proven above (33:7) concerning Manasseh: “And he placed the graven image of the idol, etc.” Therefore, Josiah commanded to return it and to place the Ark in the place that Solomon had built for it, but our Rabbis said (*Yoma* 42b) that he commanded the Levites to hide it there.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:3:2)


**אֵין לָכֶם מַשָּׂא בַּכָּתֵף.** זוֹ טַעֲנָה אַחֶרֶת הִיא, וּתְחִלַּת מִלָּה הוּא, שֶׁאָמַר לָהֶם: אֵין לָכֶם שׁוּם טֹרַח מַשָּׂא בַּכָּתֵף מִכָּל כְּלֵי בֵּית ה׳, שֶׁתּוּכְלוּ לוֹמַר: מִשּׁוּם טֹרַח מַשָּׂא בַּכָּתֵף אֵין אָנוּ יְכוֹלִין **לַעֲבֹד אֶת ה׳ וְעַמּוֹ**, לְפִיכָךְ מֵעַתָּה **עִבְדוּ אֶת ה׳ אֱלֹהֵיכֶם** בְּשִׁיר, **וְאֶת עַמּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל** לִשְׁחֹט וּלְהַפְשִׁיט פִּסְחֵיהֶן; כְּדִמוֹכַח לְמַטָּה **וְשַׁחֲטוּ הַפֶּסַח וְהִתְקַדְּשׁוּ וְגוֹ׳**:


**you have no burden on the shoulders** This is another argument and the beginning of a statement. He said to them. You have no more toil of carrying a burden on your shoulders, i.e., all the vessels of the House of the Lord. You can no longer say, “Because of the toil of the burdens on our shoulders, we cannot serve the Lord and His people.” Therefore, from now on, serve the Lord your God with song and His people Israel by slaughtering and flaying their Passover sacrifices, as is proven below (verse 6): “And slaughter the Passover sacrifice and prepare yourselves, etc.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:5:1)


**לִפְלֻגּוֹת.** לְמַחְלֹקֶת, לַעֲשׂוֹת הַפֶּסַח כְּהִלְכָתוֹ **כִּדְבַר ה׳ בְּיַד מֹשֶׁה**:


**according to the divisions** in divisions to make the Passover sacrifice according to its law, according to the word of the Lord by the hand of Moses.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:7:1)


**וַיָּרֶם יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ לִבְנֵי הָעָם.** דֻּגְמָא: ״כֵּן תָּרִימוּ גַם אַתֶּם״ (במדבר יח:כח), לְשׁוֹן הַפְרָשָׁה:


**And Josiah separated for the members of the people** - Heb. ויָּרֶם. An example is (Num. 18: 28): “So shall you too separate (תָּרִימוּ),” an expression of separation.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:7:2)


**לְכָל הַנִּמְצָא.** לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל הַנִּמְצָאִים הִפְרִישׁ **צֹאן לַפְּסָחִים**:


**for all that were present** He separated small cattle for the Passover sacrifices for all the Israelites present.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:7:3)


**לְמִסְפַּר שְׁלֹשִׁים אֶלֶף.** בֵּין **צֹאן** בֵּין **כְּבָשִׂים** בֵּין **עִזִּים**:


**for the number of thirty thousand** of small cattle, of lambs, and goats.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:7:4)


**וּבָקָר שְׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים.** לְשַׁלְמֵי חֲגִיגָה:


**and large cattle—three thousand** for Festival peace offerings.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:13:1)


**וַיְבַשְּׁלוּ הַפֶּסַח בָּאֵשׁ כַּמִּשְׁפָּט.** ״צְלִי אֵשׁ״ (שמות יב:ח):


**And they cooked the Passover sacrifice with fire according to the law** Exod. 12:8): “Roasted with fire.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:13:2)


**וַיָּרִיצוּ לְכָל בְּנֵי הָעָם.** הַלְוִיִּם, וּכְשֶׁהֵכִינוּ לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל אַחַר כָּךְ הֵכִינוּ לָהֶם הַלְוִיִּם לְעַצְמָם, וְלַכֹּהֲנִים שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לָהֶם פְּנַאי לְהָכִין:


**and carried them quickly to all the members of the people** [i.e.] the Levites [did this]; and after they had prepared for all Israel, the Levites prepared for themselves and for the priests, who did not have time to prepare.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:15:1)


**חוֹזֵה הַמֶּלֶךְ.** כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד הָיָה חוֹזֶה:


**the king’s seer** Each one was a seer.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:18:1)


**וְלֹא נַעֲשָׂה פֶסַח כָּמוֹהוּ.** בִּמְלָכִים (מלכים ב כג:כב) כְּתִיב ״כִּי לֹא נַעֲשָׂה כַּפֶּסַח הַזֶּה מִימֵי הַשּׁוֹפְטִים״, מִיּוֹם שֶׁנֶּחְלְקוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לִשְׁתֵּי מַמְלָכוֹת, שֶׁסָּרוּ עֲשֶׂרֶת שְׁבָטִים מֵעַל מַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה וְיַשְׁלִיטוּ עֲלֵיהֶם מֶלֶךְ לְבַדָּם; שֶׁכָּל הַיָּמִים שֶׁהָיוּ לִשְׁנֵי גוֹיִם, הָיוּ שֵׁבֶט יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין עוֹשִׂים פֶּסַח לְשֵׁם ה׳ בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, וְאֵלּוּ לְשֵׁם עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים בְּבֵית אֵל וּבְדָן. וְדָבָר זֶה לֹא אֵרַע כָּל יְמֵי הַשּׁוֹפְטִים שֶׁנֶּחְלְקוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לִשְׁתֵּי מִשְׁפָּחוֹת, וְלֹא הָיָה לָהֶם לְכָל דּוֹר וָדוֹר רַק שׁוֹפֵט אֶחָד, וְאוֹתוֹ הַשּׁוֹפֵט הָיָה מַכְרִיחָם לָלֶכֶת בְּדֶרֶךְ ה׳, כַּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּשׁוֹפְטִים ״וְהָיָה ה׳ עִם הַשּׁוֹפֵט וְהוֹשִׁיעָם מִיַּד אֹיְבֵיהֶם כָּל יְמֵי הַשּׁוֹפֵט״ (שופטים ב:יח); וְכָל עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים הָיוּ הוֹלְכִים לְשִׁילֹה וְעוֹשִׂים פֶּסַח לְשֵׁם ה׳, וְכַפֶּסַח הַזֶּה לֹא נַעֲשָׂה כָּל יְמֵי מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהוּדָה יַחַד עוֹשִׂים פֶּסַח, אֲבָל בִּימֵי יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ שֶׁכְּבָר פָּסַק מַלְכוּת עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים, וּמִשֶּׁהֶחֱזִיר יִרְמְיָה עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים, לֹא הִמְלִיכוּ עֲלֵיהֶם עוֹד מֶלֶךְ אֶלָּא יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ מָלַךְ עֲלֵיהֶם, וְעָשׂוּ כֻּלָּם פֶּסַח יַחַד לְשֵׁם שָׁמַיִם בִּירוּשָׁלִָם; וְזֶהוּ ״לֹא נַעֲשָׂה כַּפֶּסַח הַזֶּה״ לַה׳ בִּירוּשָׁלִָם. וּפִתְרוֹן זֶה מַטְעֶה אוֹתִי מְאֹד – וְלֹא נִרְאֶה לִי, נְהִי נַמִּי דְּהֶחֱזִיר יִרְמְיָה עֲשֶׂרֶת שְׁבָטִים וְיֹאשִׁיָּהוּ מָלַךְ עֲלֵיהֶם בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם – הֵיאַךְ יָכוֹל לִהְיוֹת שֶׁלֹּא עָשׂוּ ״כַּפֶּסַח הַזֶּה״ כָּל ״יְמֵי הַשּׁוֹפְטִים״, וּבִימֵי שְׁמוּאֵל הָרוֹאֶה וּבִימֵי דָּוִד וּשְׁלֹמֹה, שֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל שְׁרוּיִן עַל אַדְמָתָם שְׁלֵוִים וְשׁוֹקְטִים וְלֹא הָיָה לָהֶם אֶלָּא מֶלֶךְ אֶחָד וְהָיָה לִבָּם לַשָּׁמַיִם?! אַךְ כָּךְ פִּתְרוֹנוֹ, וְכָךְ פָּתַר לִי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בְּרַבִּי מְשֻׁלָּם זַצַ״ל: **וְלֹא נַעֲשָׂה פֶסַח כָּמוֹהוּ** מֵעוֹלָם שֶׁיִּתֵּן הַמֶּלֶךְ וְהַשָּׂרִים בְּהֵמוֹת לַפְּסָחִים דֵּי סִפּוּקָם וּבָקָר לְשַׁלְמֵי חֲגִיגָה, וְצִוָּה לַלְוִיִּם לְהָכִין לָהֶם וְלִשְׁלוֹחַ לְכָל אִישׁ וָאִישׁ לְפִי אָכְלוֹ:


**And such a Passover sacrifice had not been made** In II Kings (23:22) it is written: “For such a Passover sacrifice had not been performed since the time of the judges,” since the day that Israel was divided into two kingdoms, when the ten tribes separated from the kings of Judah and crowned a king over themselves. For all the time that they were two nations, the tribes of Judah and Benjamin made a Passover sacrifice for the sake of the Lord in Jerusalem, and these [made one] for the sake of pagan deities in Beth-El and in Dan. And this did not occur all the days of the judges, until Israel was divided into two families, and every generation had only one judge, and that judge would compel them to walk in the way of the Lord, as is explained in Judges (2:18): “and the Lord was with the judge and saved them from the hand of their enemies all the days of the judge.” And all the ten tribes would go to Shiloh and make a Passover sacrifice for the sake of the Lord. But such a Passover sacrifice had not been made all the days of the kings of Israel and Judah, that they should make a Passover sacrifice together. But in the days of Josiah, when the kingdom of the ten tribes had already ceased to exist, for since Jeremiah had returned the ten tribes, they did not crown another king, but Josiah reigned over them, and they all made a Passover sacrifice together in Jerusalem. This is the meaning of: “such a Passover sacrifice had not been performed...to the Lord in Jerusalem.” This interpretation, however, misleads me very much, and does not appear correct to me, for although Jeremiah had returned the ten tribes and Josiah reigned over them in Jerusalem, how is it possible that they did not make such a Passover sacrifice all the days of the judges and in the days of Samuel the Seer and in the days of David and Solomon, when Israel was dwelling in its land in peace and tranquility, and they had only one king, and their hearts were directed toward Heaven? Rather, this is the explanation, and this is the way Rabbi Eliezer the son of Rabbi Meshullam interpreted it to me: “And such a Passover sacrifice had not been made” ever, [it had never happened] that the king and the officers should supply enough animals for the Passover sacrifices and cattle for the festive peace offerings, and that he commanded the Levites to prepare for them and send each person according to how much he could eat.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:20:1)


**אַחֲרֵי כָל זֹאת אֲשֶׁר הֵכִין יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ אֶת הַבַּיִת עָלָה נְכוֹ מֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם.** הַפָּסוּק קוֹבֵל וּמְקוֹנֵן עַל יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ, שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲשָׂה לוֹ נֵס כְּמוֹ לְחִזְקִיָּהוּ שֶׁכָּתוּב בּוֹ ״אַחֲרֵי הַדְּבָרִים וְהָאֱמֶת בָּא סַנְחֵרִיב״ (דברי הימים ב לב:א) עַל חִזְקִיָּהוּ, וְנַעֲשָׂה לוֹ נֵס:


**After all this, that Josiah had prepared the Temple, Neco, the king of Egypt, went up** Scripture decries and laments Josiah, for a miracle was not performed for him as it had been for Hezekiah, about whom it is written (32:1): “After these deeds of integrity, Sennacherib the king of Assyria came” upon Hezekiah, and a miracle was performed for him.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:20:2)


**לְהִלָּחֵם בְּכַרְכְּמִישׁ עַל פְּרָת.** ״עַל מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר״, כְּדִכְתִיב בִּמְלָכִים (מלכים ב כג:כט), וְרָצָה לַעֲבֹר דֶּרֶךְ אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל עָלָיו; וּפַרְעֹה נְכוֹ לֹא הָיָה יָכוֹל לָבוֹא עָלָיו דֶּרֶךְ נְהַר פְּרָת אִם לֹא יַעֲבֹר דֶּרֶךְ אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁהֲרֵי מִצְרַיִם בִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּנְהַר פְּרָת בִּצְפוֹנוֹ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**went up to wage war in Carchemish on the Euphrates** against the king of Assyria. So it is written in (II) Kings (23:29), and he wished to pass to him through the land of Israel, and Pharaoh Neco could not go to him by the way of the Euphrates River unless he were to pass through Israel, for Egypt is south of the land of Israel, and the Euphrates River is north of the land of Israel.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:20:3)


**וַיֵּצֵא לִקְרָאתוֹ.** שֶׁדָּרַשׁ ״וְחֶרֶב לֹא תַעֲבֹר בְּאַרְצְכֶם״ (ויקרא כו:ו): אֲפִלּוּ ״חֶרֶב״ שֶׁל שָׁלוֹם ״לֹא תַעֲבֹר בְּאַרְצְכֶם״; וְלֹא יָדַע חֵטְא לֵיצָנֵי הַדּוֹר, אֲשֶׁר אַחֲרֵי הַדֶּלֶת קָמוּ לְסַדֵּר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת תַּעֲנִית (כב.-כב:):


**went forth toward him** for he interpreted (Lev. 26:6): “and a sword will not pass through your land” to mean that even peaceful armies passing through the land [to attack other countries] should not pass through your land, but he was unaware of “the sin of the scorners of the generation, who arose to set up [idolatrous symbols] behind the door,” [i.e., he did not know that his generation found but little favor in God’s eyes], as is explained in Tractate *Taanith* (22b)



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:22:1)


**וְלֹא שָׁמַע אֶל דִּבְרֵי נְכוֹ מִפִּי אֱלֹהִים.** שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ מִפִּי אֱלֹהִים, כִּי כֵּן אָמַר יְשַׁעְיָה ״וְסִכְסַכְתִּי מִצְרַיִם בְּמִצְרַיִם״ (ישעיה יט:ב); וְזֶהוּ שֶׁיִּסֵּד הַקַּלִּיר (קינות לתשעה באב יב): ״כָּל הֲמוֹנָיו לָלֶכֶת אֲרַם נַהֲרַיִם, לְמַעַן לֹא תַעֲבֹר חֶרֶב כָּל שֶׁהוּא בְּאֶפְרַיִם, לֹא שָׁמַע לַחוֹזֶה וְיָשׁוּב אָחוֹרִים, כִּי נִגְזְרָה גְּזֵרָה לְסַכְסֵךְ מִצְרַיִם בְּמִצְרַיִם״:


**and he did not hearken to the words of Neco from the mouth of God** who said to him from the mouth of God, for so said Isaiah, (19:2): “And I will stir up Egyptians against Egyptians.” This is what the Kallir composed: “He withheld his troops from marching to Aram Naharaim, in order that no single sword should pass through Ephraim; and he did not hearken to the prophet to turn back, for it was decreed that Egyptian be set against Egyptian.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:24:1)


**עַל רֶכֶב הַמִּשְׁנֶה.** לְפִי שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הַמֶּלֶךְ לִרְכּוֹב עַל שְׁנֵי מֶרְכָּבוֹת, הָרִאשׁוֹנָה עִקָּר, וְאִם יִצְטָרֵךְ יֵשֵׁב בַּשְּׁנִיָּה; בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה יָצָא לִפְנֵיהֶם, וּכְשֶׁחָלָה – הִרְכִּיבוּהוּ עַל הָרֶכֶב הַמִּשְׁנֶה:


**into his second chariot** for it was customary for the king to ride on two chariots; he rode mainly on the first, but if necessary, he would ride on the second. The first one went before them, and when he was stricken, they put him into the second chariot.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:25:1)


**וַיֹּאמְרוּ כָל הַשָּׁרִים.** לְשׁוֹן שִׁירָה, מְשׁוֹרְרִים; כִּי גַּם שִׁירָה וְקִינָה בְּקוֹל אַחַת נֶאֱמָרִין. עָלָיו נִיבָּא עָמוֹס ״וְהֵבֵאתִי אֶת הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ בַּצָּהֳרַיִם וְהַחֲשַׁכְתִּי לָאָרֶץ בְּיוֹם אוֹר״ (עמוס ח:ט) – זֶה יוֹמוֹ שֶׁל יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ שֶׁמֵּת בֶּן תִּשְׁעָה וּשְׁלֹשִׁים שָׁנִים, בַּחֲצִי יָמָיו, דִּכְתִיב ״יְמֵי שְׁנוֹתֵינוּ בָהֶם שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה״ (תהלים צ:י) וּכְתִיב ״וְהָפַכְתִּי חַגֵּיכֶם לְאֵבֶל וְכָל שִׁירֵיכֶם לְקִינָה וְשַׂמְתִּיהָ כְּאֵבֶל יָחִיד וְאַחֲרִיתָהּ כְּיוֹם מָר״ (עמוס ח:י); וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר: **וַיֹּאמְרוּ כָל הַשָּׁרִים וְשָׁרוֹת בְּקִינוֹתֵיהֶם, וְכָל יְהוּדָה וִירוּשָׁלִַם מִתְאַבְּלִים עָלָיו**:


**And all the male...singers** - הַשָּׁרִים, an expression of song, choristers, for both song and lamentation are recited with the same voice. Concerning him, Amos prophesied (8:9): “and I will cause the sun to set at midday, and I will darken the land on a sunny day.” This is the day of Josiah, who died at the age of thirty-nine, in the middle of his days, for it is written (Ps. 90:10): “The days of our years because of them are seventy years,” and it is written (Amos 8:10): “And I will turn your festivals into mourning, and all your songs into lamentation, etc. and I will make it like the mourning for an only son, and its end like a bitter day.” This is what [the Chronicler] says: “And all the male and female singers said in their lamentations,” “and all of Judah and Jerusalem were mourning” for him.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:25:2)


**וַיִּתְּנֵם לְחֹק.** כְּשֶׁמִּזְדַּמֵּן לָהֶם שׁוּם צַעַר וּבְכִיָּה, שֶׁהֵם מְקוֹנְנִים וּבוֹכִים עַל הַמְּאוֹרָע, הֵם מַזְכִּירִים זֶה הַצַּעַר עִמּוֹ; דּוּגְמָא: בְּתִשְׁעָה בְּאָב שֶׁמַּזְכִּירִים קִינוֹת עַל הַהֲרוּגִים בַּגְּזֵרוֹת שֶׁאֵרְעוּ בְּיָמֵינוּ, כֵּן יִבְכְּיוּן עַל מוֹת יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ. דּוּגְמָא: ״וַתְּהִי חֹק בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹמֵר, לְתַנּוֹת לְבַת יִפְתָּח הַגִּלְעָדִי אַרְבָּעָה יָמִים בַּשָּׁנָה״ (שופטים יא:לט-מ):


**and they made them a statute** When any grief or weeping befalls them, for which they lament and weep, they mention this grief with it. An example is the Ninth of Av, in which we recite lamentations for those slain in the persecutions that occurred in our times. They will similarly bewail the day of Josiah’s death. An example is (Jud. 11:39f.): “and it became a statute in Israel, etc. to lament the daughter of Jephthah the Gileadite, four days in a year.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:25:3)


**וְהִנָּם כְּתוּבִים עַל הַקִּינוֹת.** עַל סֵפֶר הַקִּינוֹת:


**and behold, they are written in the lamentations** in the Book of Lamentations.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 35:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/35:26:1)


**וַחֲסָדָיו כַּכָּתוּב בְּתוֹרַת ה׳.** וּבִמְלָכִים כְּתִיב ״וְכָמֹהוּ לֹא הָיָה לְפָנָיו מֶלֶךְ״ (מלכים ב כג:כה), וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר כָּאן **הִנָּם כְּתוּבִים עַל סֵפֶר מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהוּדָה**:


**and his kind deeds** And in II Kings (23:25) it is written: “Now, before him there was no king like him.” This is what is stated here: “Behold they are written in the Book of the Kings of Israel and Judah.”



## Chapter 36


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:1:1)


**וַיַּמְלִיכֻהוּ תַּחַת אָבִיו.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָיָה יְהוֹיָקִים אָחִיו גָּדוֹל מִמֶּנּוּ עֶשְׂרִים וְאֶחָד חֳדָשִׁים:


**and made him king instead of his father** although Jehoiakim his brother was twenty-one months his senior.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:3:1)


**וַיְסִירֵהוּ מֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם בִּירוּשָׁלִָם.** מְבֹאָר בִּמְלָכִים (מלכים ב כג:לג) הֵיטֵב: ״וַיַּאַסְרֵהוּ פַּרְעֹה נְכֹה בְּרִבְלָה בְּאֶרֶץ חֲמָת״:


**And the king of Egypt deposed him in Jerusalem** This is explained thoroughly in II Kings (23: 33): “And Pharaoh-Neco imprisoned him in Riblah in the land of Hamath.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:3:2)


**בִּירוּשָׁלִָם.** מִמְּלוֹךְ בִּירוּשָׁלִָם:


**in Jerusalem** from reigning in Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:3:3)


**וַיַּעֲנֹשׁ אֶת הָאָרֶץ.** לִיתֵּן מִסִּים; דּוּגְמָא: ״וְעָנְשׁוּ אוֹתוֹ מֵאָה כָּסֶף״ (דברים כב:יט):


**and he imposed a fine upon the land** to give taxes. An example is (Deut. 22: 19): “And they shall fine him one hundred pieces of silver.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:4:1)


**וַיַּסֵּב אֶת שְׁמוֹ יְהוֹיָקִים.** כָּךְ דֶּרֶךְ הַמְּלָכִים וְהַשָּׂרִים שֶׁמְּכַנִּים לִמְשָׁרְתֵיהֶם שֵׁם שֶׁהֵם רוֹצִים; דֻּגְמָא: ״וַיִּקְרָא פַרְעֹה שֵׁם יוֹסֵף וְגוֹמֵר״ (בראשית מא:מה), וְכֵן ״וַיִּקְרָא מֹשֶׁה לְהוֹשֵׁעַ בִּן נוּן יְהוֹשֻׁעַ״ (במדבר יג:טז), וְכֵן ״וַיָּשֶׂם שַׂר הַסָּרִיסִים שֵׁמוֹת וְגוֹ׳״ (דניאל א:ז); וְהַכֹּל – כְּדֵי לְהוֹדִיעַ שֶׁהוּא מוֹשֵׁל בּוֹ לְכַנּוֹת לוֹ שֵׁם כִּרְצוֹנוֹ:


**and he changed his name to Jehoiakim** It is the custom of kings and princes to bestow a by-name upon their servants as they wish. An example is (Gen. 41:45): “And Pharaoh named Joseph, etc.,” and so (Num. 13:16): “and Moses called Hoshea the son of Nun, Joshua,” and so, (Dan. 1:7): “Now the chief officer gave them names, etc.” All this is to make known that he rules over him to name him as he desires.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:4:2)


**וַיַּסֵּב אֶת שְׁמוֹ יְהוֹיָקִים.** לָמָּה קָרָא שְׁמוֹ יְהוֹיָקִים? לְמַעְלָה מְפֹרָשׁ, כִּי לְאַחַר שֶׁהָרַג פַּרְעֹה יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ (דברי הימים ב לה:כד) הִמְלִיךְ מֶלֶךְ, וְהֶעֱנִישָׁם לְמַס עֹבֵד:


**and he changed his name to Jehoiakim** Why did he change his name to Jehoiakim? It is explained above that after Pharaoh slew Josiah, he appointed a king and fined them by imposing a tribute of enslavement upon them.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:6:1)


**עָלָיו עָלָה נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּר.** כִּי לֹא יָכוֹל פַּרְעֹה לָצֵאת מֵאַרְצוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב ״וְלֹא הוֹסִיף עוֹד מֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם לָצֵאת מֵאַרְצוֹ כִּי לָקַח מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל מִנַּחַל מִצְרַיִם עַד נְהַר פְּרָת כֹּל אֲשֶׁר... לְמֶלֶךְ מִצְרָיִם״ (מלכים ב כד:ז), שֶׁנִּצַּח בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית בְּכַרְכְּמִישׁ שֶׁל נְהַר פְּרָת, כַּמְפֹרָשׁ בְּיִרְמְיָהוּ (ירמיהו מו:ב):


**Nebuchadnezzar the king of Babylon advanced upon him** because Pharaoh could not go forth from his land, as it is written (II Kings 24:7): “And the king of Egypt no longer went out of his land, for the king of Babylonia had taken from the river of Egypt until the Euphrates River, all that belonged to the king of Egypt,” for he defeated him in the fourth year in Carchemish on the Euphrates River, as is delineated in Jeremiah (46:2).


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:6:2)


**לְהוֹלִיכוֹ בָּבֶלָה.** וְלֹא הוֹלִיכוֹ לְאַשּׁוּר, אֶלָּא בַּדֶּרֶךְ מֵת וְהִשְׁלִיךְ נִבְלָתוֹ; וְכֵן נִיבָּא ״קְבוּרַת חֲמוֹר יִקָּבֵר סָחוֹב וְהַשְׁלֵךְ מֵהָלְאָה לְשַׁעֲרֵי יְרוּשָׁלִָם״ (ירמיהו כב:יט) – וְהֵיכָן קְבוּרַת חֲמוֹר? בִּמְעֵי הַכֶּלֶב. וְכֵן מוֹכִיחַ: ״וַיִּשְׁכַּב יְהוֹיָקִים עִם אֲבוֹתָיו״ (מלכים ב כד:ו) – וְלֹא כָתַב וַיִּקְבְּרֵהוּ:


**to bring him to Babylon** He did not actually bring him, however, but he died on the way, and they cast away his corpse, and so did Jeremiah prophesy (22:19): “A donkey’s burial shall he be buried, dragged and tossed past the gates of Jerusalem.” Now where is a donkey’s burial? In a dog’s stomach, and so it is apparent in II Kings (24:6): “And Jehoiakim slept with his forefathers,” but it is not written: “and they buried him.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:7:1)


**וּמִכְּלֵי בֵית ה׳.** וּמִקְצָת כְּלֵי בֵית ה׳, וְעַל זֶה נִתְנַבֵּא יִרְמְיָה: ״כִּי כֹה אָמַר ה׳ צְבָאוֹת אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל הַכֵּלִים הַנּוֹתָרִים וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיהו כז:כא). וּמַקְשֶׁה בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה כה): ״בִּשְׁנַת שָׁלֹשׁ לְמַלְכוּת יְהוֹיָקִים מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה וַיִּתֵּן ה׳ בְּיָדוֹ״ (דניאל א:א-ב), וְכָאן מוֹכִיחַ שֶׁהָיָה בְּאַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה לְמַלְכוּת, וּמַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר: ״בִּשְׁנַת שָׁלֹשׁ״ – לְמִרְדּוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיְהִי לוֹ יְהוֹיָקִים עֶבֶד שְׁלֹשָׁה שָׁנִים וַיָּשָׁב וַיִּמְרֹד בּוֹ״ (מלכים ב כד:א) שְׁלֹשָׁה שָׁנִים, וּבִשְׁנַת שְׁלֹשָׁה לְמִרְדּוֹ עָלָה עָלָיו, וְהוּא שְׁנַת שְׁמוֹנֶה לִנְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּר; דְּאָמַר מַר: שָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה כָּבַשׁ נִינְוֵה, שְׁנִיָּה – כָּבַשׁ יְהוֹיָקִים, וְשָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים מָרַד בּוֹ; וְהוּא לֹא מָלַךְ כִּי אִם בִּשְׁנַת אַרְבַּע לִיהוֹיָקִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית לִיהוֹיָקִים בֶּן יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה הִיא הַשָּׁנָה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר״ (ירמיהו כה:א):
