**And some of the vessels of the House of the Lord** And some of the vessels of the House of the Lord, and concerning this, Jeremiah prophesied (27:21): “For so said the Lord of Hosts, the God of Israel, concerning the vessels remaining, etc.” Seder Olam questions (Dan. 1:1): “In the third year of the reign of Jehoiakim, king of Judah, etc. and the Lord delivered...into his hand.” Here, however, it is apparent that it was in the eleventh year of his reign. Now what is the meaning of “in the third year”? Counting from his rebellion, for it is stated (II Kings 24:1): “and Jehoiakim was his vassal for three years, then he turned and rebelled against him” for three years, and in the third year of his rebellion, he advanced against him, and that is the eighth year of Nebuchadnezzar, for the master taught: In the first year [of his reign] he conquered Nineveh; in the second year he vanquished Jehoiakim, who served him for three years and rebelled against him for three years, and he did not reign until the fourth year of Jehoiakim, for it is stated (Jer. 25:1): “in the fourth year of Jehoiakim, the son of Josiah, king of Judah - that was the first year of Nebuchadnezzar.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:8:1)


**וְתוֹעֲבוֹתָיו.** שֶׁבָּא עַל אִמּוֹ:


**and the abominations** that he was intimate with his mother.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:8:2)


**וְהַנִּמְצָא עָלָיו.** כְּתוֹבֶת קַעֲקַע:


**and that which was found upon him** This alludes to a tattoo, [something found on the body].



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:10:1)


**וְלִתְשׁוּבַת הַשָּׁנָה.** שֶׁהִגְלָה יְהוֹיָקִים:


**And at the return of the year** that Jehoiakim was exiled.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:10:2)


**עִם כְּלֵי חֶמְדַּת בֵּית ה׳.** דְּבָרִים הַחֲמוּדִים אוֹתָם לָקַח, וְהַשְּׁאָר הִנִּיחַ עַד גָּלוּת צִדְקִיָּהוּ, אָז לָקַח הַכֹּל עִמּוֹ; בִּמְלָכִים ב׳ מְפֹרָשׁ שֶׁ״יָּצָא יְהוֹיָכִין מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה עַל מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים ב כד:יב):


**with the precious vessels of the House of the Lord** the desirable things. Those he took, and the rest he left until the exile of Zedekiah. Then he took everything with him. And in II Kings (24:12), it is explained that “Jehoiachin, the king of Judah, came out to the king of Babylonia, etc.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:10:3)


**וַיַּמְלֵךְ אֶת צִדְקִיָּהוּ אָחִיו.** אָחִיו שֶׁל יְהוֹיָקִים; וְכֵן ״וַיַּמְלֵךְ מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל אֶת מַתַּנְיָה דֹּדוֹ תַּחְתָּיו וַיַּסֵּב אֶת שְׁמוֹ צִדְקִיָּהוּ״ (מלכים ב כד:יז) – דּוֹדוֹ שֶׁל יְהוֹיָכִין:


**and he made Zedekiah his brother king** Jehoiakim’s brother, and so in II Kings (24:17), “And the king of Babylonia crowned Mattaniah his uncle in his stead and changed his name to Zedekiah,” Jehoiachin’s uncle.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:12:1)


**וַיַּעַשׂ הָרַע.** מַהוּ הָרַע? שֶׁ**לֹּא נִכְנַע לִפְנֵי יִרְמְיָהוּ הַנָּבִיא מִפִּי ה׳**, כַּמְפֹרָשׁ ״וַיֹּאמֶר יִרְמְיָה אֶל צִדְקִיָּהוּ כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ אֱלֹהֵי צְבָאוֹת אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אִם יָצֹא תֵצֵא וְגוֹמֵר״ עַד ״דּוֹאֵג״ אֲנִי ״אֶת הַיְּהוּדִים וְגוֹמֵר״ (ירמיהו לח:יז-יט). וְחוּץ מֵחֵטְא זֶה לֹא חָטָא אֶלָּא בָּזֶה שֶׁלֹּא שָׁמַע לִדְבַר נָבִיא מִפִּי ה׳, וְשֶׁעָבַר עַל שְׁבוּעַת נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּר, אֲבָל בְּתוֹעֲבוֹתָיו וְהַנִּמְצָא עָלָיו, עַל יְהוֹיָקִים, לֹא הֵרַע לַעֲשׂוֹת; מִפִּי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בְּרַבִּי מְשֻׁלָּם זַצַ״ל:


**And he did that which was evil in the eyes of the Lord** What is the evil? That he did not humble himself before Jeremiah the prophet, [who spoke] from the mouth of the Lord, as is explained in Jeremiah (38:17ff.): “And Jeremiah said to Zedekiah, ‘So said the Lord God of Hosts, the God of Israel: If you go out, etc.’...’I fear the Jews, etc.’” But aside from this sin, he did not sin except insofar as he did not obey the words of the prophet speaking from the mouth of the Lord, and that he transgressed Nebuchadnezzar’s oath. But he did not do “his abominations and that which was found in” Jehoiakim, i.e., the evil done by Jehoiakim. [This was heard] from Rabbi Eliezer the son of Rabbi Meshullam.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:13:1)


**אֲשֶׁר הִשְׁבִּיעוֹ בֵאלֹהִים.** כְּמְפֹרָשׁ: ״אֱמֹר נָא לְבֵית הַמֶּרִי וְגוֹמֵר״ (יחזקאל יז:יב); וְכָל הַפָּרָשָׁה נֶאֱמַר עַל יְהוֹיָכִין וְצִדְקִיָּהוּ: ״וַיִּקַּח מַלְכָּהּ וְשָׂרֶיהָ וַיָּבֵא אוֹתָם אֵלָיו בָּבֶלָה״ (יחזקאל יז:יב) – זֶה יְהוֹיָכִין וְסִיעָתוֹ, ״וַיִּקַּח מִזֶּרַע הַמְּלוּכָה וַיִּכְרֹת אִתּוֹ בְּרִית״ (יחזקאל יז:יג) – זֶה צִדְקִיָּהוּ:


**who adjured him by God** as is explained in Ezekiel (17:12): “Say now to the rebellious house, etc.” The entire chapter was written regarding Jehoiachin and Zedekiah: (verse 13f.) “took its king and its princes, and brought them to him to Babylon,” refers to Jehoiachin and his company. “And he took of the royal seed and formed a covenant with him,” refers to Zedekiah.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:14:1)


**גַּם כָּל שָׂרֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים.** לְהוֹרוֹת אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, כְּדִכְתִיב ״כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר יוֹרוּ אֶתְכֶם הַכֹּהֲנִים הַלְוִיִּם״ (דברים כד:ח); לֹא כֹהֲנִים הֶדְיוֹטִים לִמְחוֹת, אֶלָּא שָׂרֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים, שֶׁהַיְּכֹלֶת בְּיָדָם לִמְחוֹת וְלֹא מִחוּ:


**Also, all etc.** They should have instructed Israel, as it is written (Deut. 24:8): “in accordance with all that the priests, the Levites, instruct you.” This does not refer to simple priests, who have no power to protest, but to the chiefs of the priests, who have the power to protest, but they did not protest.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:14:2)


**וַיְטַמְּאוּ אֶת בֵּית ה׳.** בְּשִׁקּוּצֵיהֶם:


**and they defiled the House of the Lord** with their abominations.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:14:3)


**אֲשֶׁר הִקְדִּישׁ בִּירוּשָׁלִָם.** לְשַׁכֵּן שְׁמוֹ שָׁם (דברים יב:יא) לְעוֹלָם:


**which He had hallowed in Jerusalem** to cause His glory to rest there forever.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:15:1)


**וַיִּשְׁלַח ה׳.** כָּל זֶה צֶדֶק הַדִּין, שֶׁלֹּא תֹאמַר: לֹא הִתְרָה בָּהֶם לָשׁוּב מִדַּרְכֵּיהֶם הָרָעָה, אֶלָּא לְאַלְתַּר ״וַיִּטְרֹף לָעַד אַפּוֹ״ (עמוס א:יא); לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: **וַיִּשְׁלַח ה׳ אֱלֹהֵי אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם עֲלֵיהֶם בְּיַד מַלְאָכָיו**, וְהִתְרָה בָּהֶם לָשׁוּב מֵרָעָתָם, וְלֹא פַּעַם אַחַת בְּשָׁנָה אוֹ בְחֹדֶשׁ, אֶלָּא בְּכָל יוֹם וָיוֹם, **הַשְׁכֵּם וְשָׁלוֹחַ**:


**And the Lord...sent upon them** All this is an acknowledgment of divine justice, so that you should not say that He did not forewarn them to repent of their evil ways, as it is written: “grasped forever His anger and kept His fury forever (Amos 1:12).” Therefore, it is stated: “And the Lord God of their forefathers sent upon them through His messengers,” and forewarned them to repent of their evil, and not once a year or a month, but every day, “sending them with every fresh morn.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:15:2)


**כִּי חָמַל עַל עַמּוֹ.** וְלֹא חָפֵץ בְּמִיתָתָן:


**for He had pity on His people** and did not desire their death.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:15:3)


**וְעַל מְעוֹנוֹ.** עַל דִּירַת בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ; שֶׁאֲפִילּוּ מֶלֶךְ בָּשָׂר וָדָם חוֹמֵל עַל עַמּוֹ וְעַל בֵּית מְדוֹרוֹ מִלְּהַחֲרִיבָם:


**and on His abode** on the abode of the Temple, for even a flesh and blood king has pity on his people and on his abode, not to destroy them.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:16:1)


**וַיִּהְיוּ מַלְעִבִים בְּמַלְאֲכֵי הָאֱלֹהִים.** בִּשְׁלוּחֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**But they mocked the messengers of God** the messengers of the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:16:2)


**וּבוֹזִים דְּבָרָיו.** שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁוַּדַּאי הַדִּין עִמּוֹ, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ שְׁלוּחֵי בָּשָׂר וָדָם אֵין מְבַזִּין אוֹתָן וִירֵאִים לַעֲבֹר עַל צִוּוּיוֹ, בֵּין לְרַע בֵּין לְטוֹב, וְעַמּוֹ בָּזוּ דְּבָרָיו וְהִלְעִיבוּ וְתִעְתְּעוּ **בִּנְבִיאָיו** כָּל כָּךְ וְגוֹ׳:


**and despised His words** [The words] of the Holy One, blessed be He, for surely He is just. No one mocks even messengers of a flesh and blood [king] and they are afraid to transgress his command, whether for bad or for good, but His people despised His words and mocked and scoffed at His messengers, etc.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:17:1)


**וַיַּעַל עֲלֵיהֶם אֶת מֶלֶךְ כַּשְׂדִּים.** שֶׁהֵם אֻמָּה שְׁפֵלָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״הֵן... כַּשְׂדִּים... עַם לֹא הָיָה״ (ישעיה כג:יג); וְכָאן נִתְקַיֵּם ״הֵם קִנְאוּנִי בְלֹא אֵל וְגוֹמר״, עַד ״בְּגוֹי נָבָל אַכְעִיסֵם״ (דברים לב:כא):


**And He brought upon them the king of the Chaldeans** who were a lowly nation, as it is said (Isa. 23:13): “Behold the land of the Chaldees, this people has never been,” and here was fulfilled (Deut. 32:21): “They provoked Me with a non-god, etc....I will provoke them to anger with a vile nation.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:17:2)


**וַיַּהֲרֹג בַּחוּרֵיהֶם בַּחֶרֶב בְּבֵית מִקְדָּשָׁם.** וְהוּשַׁב לָהֶם גְּמוּלָם, שֶׁכָּתוּב **וַיְטַמְּאוּ אֶת בֵּית ה׳**, וְנִתְקַיֵּם מַה שֶׁאָמַר ״וְהַכּוּ אַל תָּחוֹס עֵינֵיכֶם וְגוֹ׳״ (יחזקאל ט:ה), עַד ״צְאוּ וַיֵּצְאוּ וְהִכּוּ בָעִיר״ (יחזקאל ט:ז):


**and he slew their young men by the sword in their Temple** and their recompense was requited them, as is written above (verse 14): “and they defiled the House of the Lord,” and that which He said was fulfilled, (Ezek. 9:5ff.): “’and smite, let not your eye spare, etc....and go out,’ and they went out and smote in the city.”


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:17:3)


**וְלֹא חָמַל עַל בָּחוּר וּבְתוּלָה זָקֵן וְיָשֵׁשׁ.** מַה שֶׁאֵין כֵּן מִנְהַג אַנְשֵׁי חֲיָלִים כְּשֶׁנּוֹצְחִים בַּמִּלְחָמָה וְהוֹרְגִים אַנְשֵׁי הַמִּלְחָמָה, אֲבָל עַל זָקֵן בָּחוּר וּבְתוּלָה חוֹמְלִים וּמִתְמַלְּאִים רַחֲמִים; וְגַם נְבוּאַת מֹשֶׁה רַבֵּינוּ נִתְקַיְּמָה: ״מִחוּץ תְּשַׁכֶּל חֶרֶב וּמֵחֲדָרִים אֵימָה גַּם בָּחוּר גַּם בְּתוּלָה וְגוֹמֵר״ (דברים לב:כה):


**and he had pity neither on youth nor virgin, elder nor ancient one** unlike the custom of soldiers, who, when they are victorious in war, slay the combatants, but pity the elders, the youths, and the virgins, and are filled with compassion for them, and Moses’ prophecy was fulfilled (Deut. 32:25): “The sword will bereave from outside and terror from within, youth, etc.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:18:1)


**וְכָל כְּלֵי בֵית הָאֱלֹהִים הַגְּדוֹלִים וְהַקְּטַנִּים.** שֶׁנּוֹתְרוּ מִגָּלוּת יְהוֹיָקִים וִיכָנְיָה:


**And all the vessels of the House of God, both large and small** which were left over from the exiles of Jehoiakim and Jeconiah.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:20:1)


**וַיִּהְיוּ לוֹ וּלְבָנָיו לַעֲבָדִים עַד מְלֹךְ מַלְכוּת פָּרָס.** וְלָמָּה עַד מַלְכוּת פָּרַס? כִּי עַד מַלְכוּת פָּרַס – עַד אוֹתוֹ הַזְּמַן לֹא נִתְפַּיְּסָה **הָאָרֶץ אֶת שַׁבְּתוֹתֶיהָ**, וְהוּא שֶׁכָּתוּב ״כָּל יְמֵי הָשַּׁמָּה תִּשְׁבֹּת״ (ויקרא כו:לה):


**and they became vassals to him and to his sons until the reign of the kingdom of Persia** And why until the kingdom of Persia? Because until that time, the land was not appeased for its Sabbaths, as it is written (Lev. 26:35): “As long as it is desolate, it will rest.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:21:1)


**עַד רָצְתָה הָאָרֶץ אֶת שַׁבְּתוֹתֶיהָ.** תִּרְצֶה, כִּי קֹדֶם לָכֵן לֹא רָצְתָה הָאָרֶץ שַׁבְּתוֹתֶיהָ:


**until the land was appeased for its Sabbaths** Because prior to that, the land was not appeased for its Sabbaths.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:21:2)


**כָּל יְמֵי הָשַּׁמָּה שָׁבְתָה לְמַלֹּאת שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה.** שֶׁל שְׁמִטָּה וְיוֹבֵל שֶׁבִּטְּלוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בָּאָרֶץ וּכְנֶגְדָּן גָּלוּ שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה. אוֹתָן שִׁבְעִים שָׁבְתָה אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שָׁנִים שֶׁהִכְעִיסוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּאַרְצָם אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וּשְׁלֹשִׁים שָׁנָה, מֵהֶם שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת וְתִשְׁעִים מִשֶּׁנִּכְנְסוּ לָאָרֶץ עַד שֶׁגָּלוּ מִמֶּנָּה עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים, וְאַתָּה מוֹצֵא אוֹתָם מְפֹרָשִׁים מָאתַיִם וְאַרְבָּעִים וּשְׁלֹשָׁה מִשֶּׁמָּלַךְ יָרָבְעָם עַד שֶׁהִגְלָה הוֹשֵׁעַ בֶּן אֵלָה, וּבִימֵי שְׁפוֹט הַשּׁוֹפְטִים שֶׁהִכְעִיסוּ מֵאָה וְאַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנִים, וְהַשְּׁאָר סְתוּמִים. הוּא שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בִּיחֶזְקֵאל (יחזקאל ד:ד-ו) ״וְאַתָּה שְׁכַב עַל צִדְּךָ הַשְּׂמָאלִי וְשַׂמְתָּ אֶת עֲוֹן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ׳ שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת וְתִשְׁעִים יוֹם וְגוֹ׳ וְכִלִּיתָ אֶת אֵלֶּה וְשָׁכַבְתָּ עַל צִדְּךָ הַיְמָנִי שֵׁנִית וְנָשָׂאתָ אֶת עֲוֹן בֵּית יְהוּדָה אַרְבָּעִים יוֹם יוֹם לְשָׁנָה״ – מְלַמֵּד שֶׁחָטְאוּ בֵית יְהוּדָה מִשֶּׁגָּלוּ עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים עַד שֶׁחָרְבָה יְרוּשָׁלַיִם אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה. כֵּיצַד? עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁתַּיִם שֶׁל מְנַשֶּׁה כִּדְכְתִיב (מלכים ב כא:ג) ״כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה אַחְאָב״, וְאַחְאָב מָלַךְ עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁתַּיִם שָׁנִים וּשְׁלֹשִׁים וְשָׁלֹשׁ עָשָׂה תְּשׁוּבָה, הֲרֵי חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם מָלַךְ, וּשְׁתַּיִם דְּאָמוֹן וְאַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה דִּיהוֹיָקִים הֲרֵי שְׁלֹשִׁים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנָה, וּנְבוּאָה זוֹ נֶאֶמְרָה לִיחֶזְקֵאל בַּשָּׁנָה הַחֲמִישִׁית לְצִדְקִיָּהוּ, הֲרֵי אַרְבָּעִים. סַךְ כֻּלָּם מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל וִיהוּדָה אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וּשְׁלֹשִׁים, הֲרֵי לְאַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת שָׁנָה שִׁשִּׁים וְאַרְבַּע שְׁמִטִּין וְיוֹבְלוֹת שְׁלֹשִׁים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים, הֲרֵי חָמֵשׁ שְׁמִטִּין, הֲרֵי שִׁשִּׁים וָתֵשַׁע, וּשְׁנַת הַיּוֹבֵל הָאַחֲרוֹנָה נֶחְשְׁבָה לָהֶם לְעָוֹן שֶׁהֲרֵי בַּעֲוֹנָם גָּלוּ מִמֶּנָּה, הֲרֵי שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה מְקֻדָּשִׁים שֶׁל שְׁמִטָּה שֶׁלֹּא שִׁמְּטוּם יִשְׂרָאֵל. לְפִיכָךְ גָּלוּ שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה לְקַיֵּם מַה שֶּׁנֶּאֱמַר (ויקרא כו:לד) ״אָז תִּרְצֶה הָאָרֶץ אֶת שַׁבְּתוֹתֶיהָ״. כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם. וְכָאן מְסַיֵּם בָּזֶה דִּבְרֵי הַיָּמִים, לְפִי שֶׁאָמַר לְמַלֹּאת שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה הֻצְרַךְ לוֹמַר כָּאן פְּסוּקִים אֵלּוּ וְשֶׁל עֶזְרָא (עזרא א:א-ג), לְהוֹדִיעֵנוּ כְּשֶׁכָּלוּ אוֹתָן שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה הֵיאַךְ חָזַר הַגַּלְגַּל שֶׁחָזְרוּ לְאַרְצָם, לְכָךְ הֻצְרַךְ לוֹמַר כָּאן **בִשְׁנַת אַחַת לְכוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס** עַד **מִי בָכֶם מִכָּל עַמּוֹ וְיָעַל**, וּלְכָךְ נוֹסֶפֶת וָי״ו זוֹ שֶׁל **וּבִשְׁנַת** לְפִי שֶׁסֵּפֶר דָּנִיֵּאל מַפְסִיק בֵּנְתַיִם, שֶׁהֲרֵי סֵפֶר עֶזְרָא מָשׁוּךְ וְסָדוּר אַחַר סֵפֶר דָּנִיֵּאל, וְכָךְ וָי״ו מוּסָב מִזֶּה לָזֶה:


**all the days of its desolation it rested until the completion of seventy years** [corresponding to] the Sabbatical and Jubilee years that Israel had neglected to observe in the land, and corresponding to them they were exiled for seventy years. The land of Israel rested for those seventy years. The Israelites provoked [God] in their land for 430 years. These included 390 years from the time they entered the land until the ten tribes were exiled therefrom, and they are explained (as follows): 243 years from the time Jeroboam ascended the throne until Hoshea the son of Elah was exiled, and 111 years during the administration of the judges, when they provoked [Him], and the rest are obscure. That is what is stated in Ezekiel (4:4ff.): “And you shall lie on your left side, and you shall place the iniquity of the house of Israel, etc., 390 days, etc. And when you complete these, you shall lie on your right a second time, and you shall bear the iniquity of the house of Judah, 40 days, a day for a year, a day for a year.” This teaches that the house of Judah sinned from the time that the ten tribes were exiled until Jerusalem was destroyed, for 40 years. How so? Twenty-two years of Manasseh, as it is written (II Kings 21:3): “according to all that Ahab did,” and Ahab reigned 22 years (and he [Manasseh] repented for thirty-three years, totaling fifty-five years.) Add 2 years of Amon, and 11 of Jehoiakim, totaling thirty-five years, and this prophecy was transmitted to Ezekiel in the 5th year of Zedekiah, totaling 40 years. The sum total of them all, of both Israel and Judah, is 430 years. Note that there are 64 Sabbatical and Jubilee years in 400 years. There are 5 Sabbatical years in 35 years, totaling 69, and the last Jubilee year was counted for them as iniquity, for because of their iniquity they were exiled therefrom. Hence the 70 hallowed years of Shemittah that Israel did not observe. They were consequently exiled for seventy years to fulfill what was said, (Lev. 26:34): “Then the land will be appeased regarding its Sabbaths.” So it is explained in Seder Olam (see ch. 26). Chronicles is concluded with this since he says: “until the completion of seventy years.” He had to state these verses and those of Ezra (in the beginning) to let us know that when those seventy years were over, “the wheel rotated” and they returned to their land. He therefore said here: “in the first year of Cyrus, the king of Persia” until “Who among you is of all His people, etc., and he may ascend.” The reason that the “vav” [and] of “And in the first year” is added, is because the Book of Daniel intervenes, for the book of Ezra continues in sequence after the Book of Daniel, and this “vav” connects them together.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:22:1)


**אֶת רוּחַ כּוֹרֶשׁ.** רְצוֹנוֹ:


**the spirit of Cyrus** his will.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:22:2)


**וַיַּעֲבֶר קוֹל.** כָּרוֹז הִכְרִיז לְ**כָל** בְּנֵי **מַלְכוּתוֹ**:


**and he issued a proclamation** lie made an announcement to all his subjects.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:22:3)


**וְגַם בְּמִכְתָּב.** שָׁלַח לְ**כָל** בְּנֵי **מַלְכוּתוֹ לֵאמֹר**:


**and also in writing** He sent to all his subjects, saying.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:23:1)


**כֹּה אָמַר כּוֹרֶשׁ מֶלֶךְ פָּרַס כָּל מַמְלְכוֹת הָאָרֶץ.** שֶׁאֲנִי מוֹשֵׁל, מֶלֶךְ עַל כָּל מַמְלָכוֹת:


**So said Cyrus, the king of Persia, All the kingdoms of the earth** For I reign as king over all the kingdoms.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:23:2)


**וְהוּא פָקַד עָלַי.** וְהוּא צִוָּה עָלַי עַל יְדֵי יְשַׁעְיָה לִבְנוֹת לוֹ בַּיִת בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״הָאוֹמֵר לְכוֹרֶשׁ רֹעִי וְגוֹ׳״ (ישעיהו מד:כח), ״כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ לִמְשִׁיחוֹ לְכוֹרֶשׁ״ (ישעיהו מה:א); וּכְבָר נֶאֶמְרָה לוֹ נְבוּאָה זוֹ שֶׁל יְשַׁעְיָהוּ:


**and He commanded me** And He commanded me through Isaiah to build Him a House in Jerusalem, as it is said (Isa. 44:2845:1): “Who says of Cyrus, etc. So said the Lord to His anointed one, to Cyrus.” And this prophecy of Isaiah had already been said to him.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:23:3)


**אֲשֶׁר בִּיהוּדָה.** יְרוּשָׁלַיִם בִּמְדִינוֹת יְהוּדָה:


**which is in Judea** Jerusalem is in the provinces of Judea.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:23:4)


**מִי בָכֶם מִכָּל עַמּוֹ ה׳ אֱלֹהָיו עִמּוֹ וְיָעַל.** כָּךְ הָיָה הַכָּרוֹז עַל הַמְּדִינוֹת: מִי בְּכָל עַמּוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא יָבוֹא **לִבְנוֹת** בֵּיתוֹ **אֲשֶׁר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם**:


**Who among you is of all His people, may the Lord his God be with him, and he may ascend** So was the proclamation to the provinces: Whoever is of the people of the Holy One, blessed be He, may come to build His House, which is in Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on II Chronicles 36:23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20II%20Chronicles/r/36:23:5)


**וְיָעַל.** וִיהִי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּעֶזְרוֹ וּבְעֶזְרָתֵינוּ:


**and he may ascend** and may the Holy One, blessed be He, help him and us.
